### [1] [AlphaEarth distinguishes cities but compresses urban variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30356)
*Andrew Renninger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究审计 AlphaEarth 等卫星基础模型的城市表征：这些表示可用于跨区域比较，但年度变化尚未被验证为可靠的时间比较依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市在建成形态、地表覆盖和发展历史上差异显著，但现有卫星模型训练任务缺少全球一致的城市功能标签，常见分类也会过度简化建成环境。因此需要检验模型嵌入是否能有效表征城市差异，以及其适用边界。

Method: 对 162 个国家的 1,000 个城市区域进行嵌入探测，分析城市表示在高维球面上的位置与离散程度；评估大陆、气候、城市化程度、国家发展水平及植被和纹理对表示差异的解释力，并检查年度表示变化及 Sentinel-1B 数据缺失的影响。

Result: 城市嵌入整体位于偏离全球均值方向 62.7°、但仍与全球区域重叠的位置。大陆和气候解释被排除国家城市中心均值方向差异的 24.3%。城市内部城市化程度解释 8.9% 的变化，但剩余变化呈现局部方向，而非统一的城市化轴。城市内部离散度随国家发展水平每增加一个标准差而高 14.1%；进一步控制植被和纹理对比后，该梯度最多剩 6.4%。年度表示移动幅度接近像素重绘所能解释幅度的八倍；Sentinel-1B 于 2022 年停用、导致缺失一个观测轨道方向后，表示离散度收缩。

Conclusion: AlphaEarth 表示能够支持跨区域城市比较，但城市化并非嵌入空间中的单一、普适方向，且表示离散程度受到国家发展水平及地表特征对比影响。年度层间变化可能受观测条件变化干扰，目前尚不能据此可靠地进行跨时间比较。

Abstract: Cities differ in built form, land cover and development history, complicating comparison across places and time. Satellite foundation models map Earth's surface onto common numerical representations. Yet the tasks and targets used to shape them typically do not focus on cities: globally consistent labels for urban function do not exist, and many datasets - especially land cover and land use classifications - collapse the built environment into few classes. Here we audit the representation, focusing on AlphaEarth but with broader applicability to other Earth embeddings, by probing the geometry and geography of embeddings for 1,000 urban areas in 162 countries. We find that cities occupy a shifted but overlapping region on the hypersphere, 62.7° from the global mean direction, and continent and climate predict 24.3% of variation among the mean directions of urban centres in excluded countries. Inside cities, degrees of urbanisation carry 8.9% of the variation, and what they leave holds shared directions whose local orientation varies, not one universal axis of urbanisation. Retained variation is itself unequal: dispersion within urban centres is 14.1% greater per standard deviation of national development, even after adjusting for population, land area and continent. Further controls suggest cities in developing countries present less contrast in vegetation and texture, and dispersion follows that contrast: full adjustment for it leaves at most 6.4% of the gradient. Annually, a city's representation moves nearly eight times more than redrawing its own pixels explains, and contracts where the 2022 loss of Sentinel-1B removed a pass direction. AlphaEarth's representations therefore support comparison across regions, while the differences between its annual layers are not yet validated for comparison over time.

</details>


### [2] [LiTe-GS: Oracle-Efficient Next Best View Selection for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30393)
*Vivek Pandey,Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiTe-GS 通过随机子集评估减少 3D Gaussian Splatting 下一最佳视角选择中的信息预言机调用，在保持接近 Fisher 信息基线重建质量的同时降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息驱动的视角选择有助于高效训练和自适应细化 3D Gaussian Splatting，但候选视角增加时，反复计算信息增益或 Fisher 信息等昂贵预言机的成本会迅速上升。

Method: 提出 LiTe-GS，对候选视角进行随机子集评估，而非对整个候选池逐一打分。方法以 ε 控制预言机效率与近似质量之间的权衡，并给出预言机复杂度及近似性能的理论保证。

Result: 理论上，预言机调用的期望复杂度为 O(M log(1/ε))，其中 M 为候选视角数量，且与选择数量 K 无关。Blender 和 Mip-NeRF 360 实验表明，LiTe-GS 在不同采集设置下以显著更少的 Fisher 预言机评估次数，达到与 Fisher 信息基线相近的重建质量。

Conclusion: 随机子集评估可在基本维持重建效果的同时显著降低视角选择的预言机开销，使 LiTe-GS 成为适用于大规模候选视角池的高效方案。

Abstract: Selecting informative camera views is critical for efficient training and adaptive refinement in 3D Gaussian Splatting, where each observation significantly influences model parameters. However, information-driven view-selection strategies can require repeated evaluations of expensive information-gain oracles as the number of candidate views increases. We propose LiTe-GS, an oracle-efficient method for next best view selection in 3D Gaussian Splatting. LiTe-GS reduces the number of information-oracle evaluations by performing randomized subset evaluation of candidate views rather than exhaustively scoring the full candidate pool. The resulting approach achieves expected $O(M\log(1/ε))$ oracle complexity with respect to the number of candidate views $M$, independent of the selection cardinality $K$, while providing an explicit trade-off between oracle efficiency and approximation quality through $ε$. We provide theoretical guarantees on oracle complexity and approximation performance under the proposed selection scheme. Experiments on Blender and Mip-NeRF 360 demonstrate that LiTe-GS maintains reconstruction quality comparable to Fisher-information-based baselines while substantially reducing the number of Fisher-oracle evaluations across different acquisition settings.

</details>


### [3] [CSCWD: Cross-Scale Channel-wise Knowledge Distillation for Lightweight Tiny Object Detection on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30395)
*Amir Zamani,Zeinab Ghasemi-Naraghi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨尺度通道知识蒸馏（CSCWD），将教师网络高分辨率特征迁移至轻量学生网络，在不改变推理结构的前提下提升航拍微小目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航拍图像中的微小目标空间证据弱，轻量检测器又容易丢失高分辨率细节；常规同尺度特征蒸馏难以充分利用教师网络浅层的空间信息。

Method: 训练阶段采用YOLO11m-P2作为教师、YOLO11n作为学生。对齐特征后，将教师P2特征蒸馏到学生P3，同时在更深层金字塔维持同尺度通道蒸馏；学生推理架构不变。

Result: 在统一的七序列Drone-vs-Bird验证协议下，模型达到50.17% mAP@0.5和59.73%召回率，较匹配的CA-YOLO11n基线分别提升2.92和3.55个百分点；跨尺度配置较对应的同尺度通道蒸馏配置提升2.09个百分点mAP@0.5。DUT-Anti-UAV零样本评估中，mAP@0.5由48.29%升至50.06%。树莓派5上使用NCNN-FP16、640×640输入时，2.58M参数学生模型达到50.32% mAP@0.5，平均延迟82.32 ms（12.15 FPS），运行和内存需求与基线基本相同。

Conclusion: 跨尺度蒸馏能将教师的高分辨率空间知识有效迁移给轻量学生，改善微小目标检测表现，且不增加推理阶段的模型复杂度。性能证据来自特定验证协议和硬件设置；实际部署表现仍需结合目标设备及数据分布评估。

Abstract: Real-time tiny object detection in aerial imagery is constrained by the weak spatial evidence of very small objects and the loss of high-resolution detail in lightweight detectors. This study presents Cross-Scale Channel-wise Knowledge Distillation (CSCWD), a training-time framework that transfers high-resolution spatial representations from a YOLO11m-P2 teacher to a compact YOLO11n student without altering the student's inference architecture. Unlike conventional same-scale feature distillation, CSCWD transfers supervision from teacher P2 to student P3 after feature alignment while retaining same-scale distillation at deeper pyramid levels. Under the unified seven-sequence Drone-vs-Bird validation protocol, YOLO11n-CSCWD achieves 50.17% mean average precision at an intersection-over-union threshold of 0.5 (mAP@0.5) and 59.73% recall, improving the matched CA-YOLO11n baseline by 2.92 percentage points in mAP@0.5 and 3.55 points in recall. Cross-scale alignment further increases mAP@0.5 by 2.09 points over the corresponding same-scale channel-wise distillation configuration. In zero-shot evaluation on DUT-Anti-UAV, mAP@0.5 increases from 48.29% to 50.06% without target-domain fine-tuning. This domain was included because its challenging small targets make low-latency, computationally efficient detection particularly relevant. On Raspberry Pi 5 using NCNN-FP16 at 640x640 resolution, the 2.58-million-parameter student achieves 50.32% mAP@0.5 at 82.32 ms mean wall-clock latency, or 12.15 frames per second, while retaining essentially the same runtime and memory requirements as the matched baseline. The results support cross-scale distillation for improving tiny-target detection without increasing inference-time model complexity.

</details>


### [4] [What Improves Multimodal Misinformation Detection? Answers from a Large-Scale Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30402)
*Akshit Sharma,Prashant W. Patil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文对多模态虚假信息检测中的设计选择进行了大规模、系统性的实证比较，覆盖3个基准数据集、多种预训练视觉与语言骨干网络及超过3,375项实验，旨在总结有效做法及其失效情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态虚假信息常以文本声明搭配看似佐证的图片，提升其迷惑性。检测系统的效果又可能受模型、数据与流程中的多项设计选择影响，但这些选择此前缺少受控、系统的比较，难以判断哪些选择真正有益、何时会失效。

Method: 在三个基准数据集和多种预训练视觉、语言骨干网络上开展超过3,375项实验；通过系统比较不同多模态设计选择，并进行有针对性的鲁棒性分析，围绕四个研究问题评估设计选择对检测表现及模型行为的影响。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验结果、性能数值或四个研究问题的内容，因此无法判断哪类设计选择最佳、在哪些情形下失效。摘要仅说明研究将归纳实用建议，并分析影响模型行为较大的流程因素。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在为更强、更可靠的多模态虚假信息检测系统提供经系统实验支持的设计依据。其核心贡献是比较设计选择并检验鲁棒性；具体结论仍需结合论文正文或完整实验结果确认。

Abstract: Multimodal misinformation is increasingly crafted to look convincing by pairing a textual claim with an image that appears to "prove" it. Yet in practice, building effective detectors often hinges on a small set of design choices that are rarely examined in a controlled way. In this paper, we conduct a large-scale study of multimodal design choices for misinformation detection with over 3,375 experiments- spanning three benchmark datasets and a broad range of pre-trained vision and language backbones. Through systematic comparisons and targeted robustness analyses, we distill practical guidance on which design choices help, when do they fail silently, and what aspects of the pipeline most strongly shape model behavior, answering 4 key Research Questions (RQs). We aim to provide a reliable foundation for designing stronger and more dependable multimodal misinformation detection systems, thus contributing to the broader research community.

</details>


### [5] [ProCAP: Probabilistic Cross-Attentive Prompt Learning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30434)
*Hiwa Azeez Abbas,Fatemeh Daneshfar,Moloud Abdar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProCAP 是一种面向 CLIP 的概率式跨注意力提示学习框架，在冻结骨干网络的前提下，通过双向跨模态交互、概率化提示正则和对比对齐，提升少样本泛化与迁移表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 可通过提示识别新类别，但在标注数据稀缺或测试分布变化时表现容易下降。已有多模态提示方法对视觉与文本分支的交互不足，在低样本场景中也容易过拟合或不稳定。

Method: 冻结 CLIP 全部权重，同时学习视觉和文本提示 token；用堆叠的双向多头跨注意力连接两条分支，使其随提示层级相互更新。将提示 token 参数化为高斯均值与方差，并采用轻量 KL 与 L2 正则抑制过拟合；另设紧凑的对称 InfoNCE 头，在低维共享空间中对齐跨注意力图像特征与类别级文本表征。

Result: 在 11 个数据集的少样本基类到新类泛化、跨数据集迁移，以及 ImageNet 分布偏移基准的域泛化实验中，ProCAP 获得较强的基类—新类综合表现，并保持有竞争力的迁移性能；CLIP 骨干网络保持不变。摘要未提供具体数值，无法进一步比较提升幅度或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 该方法的核心价值在于以参数高效方式加强视觉—文本提示的双向交互，并通过概率正则化和特征对比对齐提升低样本训练稳定性与泛化能力。其效果已有多种评测设置支持，但具体优势幅度及各组件的独立贡献仍需结合论文正文和消融实验判断。

Abstract: Pre-trained vision-language models such as CLIP can recognize new categories via prompting, but they often struggle when labeled data are scarce or the test distribution shifts. Prompt learning adapts only a small set of parameters while keeping the backbone frozen, yet many existing multimodal prompt learners couple the visual and textual branches weakly and can be brittle in low-shot regimes. We propose ProCAP, a probabilistic cross-attentive prompt learning framework that improves cross-modal interaction and training stability without updating any CLIP weights: it learns both visual and textual prompt tokens and links them through stacked bidirectional multi-head cross-attention so the two branches refine each other across prompt depth. To reduce overfitting under limited supervision, we parameterize prompt tokens with Gaussian means and variances and regularize them with lightweight KL and L2 penalties, and we further add a compact symmetric InfoNCE head that aligns cross-attended image features with class-level text representations in a shared low-dimensional space. Across few-shot base-to-novel generalization on 11 datasets, cross-dataset transfer, and domain generalization on ImageNet shift benchmarks, ProCAP achieves strong aggregate base-to-novel performance and competitive transfer performance while keeping the CLIP backbone unchanged.

</details>


### [6] [LensDesigner: A Self-Improving Agent for Optical Lens Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30450)
*Lei Sun,Haoran Liang,Dannong Xu,Yao Gao,Yuyu Geng,Jinjin Gu,Kaiwei Wang,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 LensDesigner 自动化光学镜头设计框架，结合镜头库检索、物理仿真反馈和持续学习，并在包含 120 项任务的 LensArena 基准上取得优于公开基线的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学镜头设计是复杂的非凸优化问题，参数空间广阔，传统自动优化方法难以有效搜索，且常依赖大量人工调参与专家经验；同时，自动设计从初始方案开始也面临冷启动问题。

Method: 构建 LensLib100K 镜头库，并采用光学感知检索提供物理上有效的结构初始方案；在交互式物理仿真环境中，由智能体进行宏观任务编排并接收即时光学反馈；引入课程智能体驱动的持续自进化机制，使系统通过逐步增加难度的设计任务提炼、积累和复用设计启发式；另构建 LensArena，包含 120 项多样化且覆盖极端配置的光学设计任务。

Result: 在 LensArena 上的大量实验显示，LensDesigner 相较公开基线算法取得更高的任务成功率和更优的优化效率。摘要未提供具体数值或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一种融合有效初始结构检索、仿真闭环反馈与持续经验积累的自主镜头设计方案，可能推动智能光学设计的发展；代码计划公开。

Abstract: Optical lens design is a complex, non-convex optimization challenge that relies heavily on human experience and intuition. Existing optimized-based automatic lens design methods struggle to navigate this vast parameter space without meticulous manual tuning. In this paper, we present LensDesigner, an autonomous agent framework that mirrors the problem-solving workflow of expert opticians. To overcome the initial cold start problem, we construct LensLib100K, an extensive optical lens library, and employ Optics-Aware Retrieval to supply physically valid structural seeds. Within an interactive physical simulation environment, the agent executes macroscopic orchestration while receiving immediate optical feedback. Furthermore, we introduce a continuous self-evolving mechanism guided by a curriculum agent. By iteratively solving design tasks with progressively increasing difficulty, the agent autonomously extracts, accumulates, and reuses design heuristics, effectively evolving its optical lens design expertise over time. At the evaluation level, we introduce LensArena, a standardized evaluation benchmark comprising $120$ diverse optical design tasks, covering extreme configurations. Extensive experiments on this benchmark demonstrate that LensDesigner significantly outperforms publicly available baseline algorithms, achieving superior success rates and optimization efficiency. We hope this work sheds light on the emerging field of intelligent optics. The code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [7] [The Shape of Events: Edge-Based Inductive Biases via Cross-Domain Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30478)
*Soshun Kihara,Shunsuke Yasuki,Masato Taki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文通过事件相机数据向RGB模型进行知识蒸馏，发现由此形成的模型更偏向形状与边缘信息，并表现出颜色不变性及对高频噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ImageNet训练的CNN往往依赖局部高频纹理，在真实环境分布变化下容易失效。事件相机突出亮度变化与轮廓，但事件域缺少成熟诊断基准，因此其对视觉模型归纳偏置的影响尚未充分研究。

Method: 将事件域知识蒸馏到RGB域，借助RGB领域已有的评估工具系统分析模型偏置；进一步检查早期网络层的颜色与空间信息处理、频谱响应，以及不同下游任务中的作用。

Result: 事件域蒸馏使RGB模型呈现颜色不变性、形状偏置和对高频噪声的鲁棒性。机制分析表明，模型降低了对高频纹理的依赖，转而更多利用基于边缘的物体形状。该模型对高频成分缺失更稳健，但若其依赖的频带受到污染或几何结构被破坏，则仍容易受影响。此偏置不同于已有鲁棒化方法，并对多种需要形状与轮廓信息的下游任务有帮助。

Conclusion: 事件域知识可作为一种有用的归纳先验迁移到RGB模型，促使其从纹理依赖转向形状与轮廓线索；这种偏置带来特定鲁棒性收益，但并非对所有类型扰动都稳健。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks trained on ImageNet are known to exhibit a strong preference for local high-frequency texture, an inductive bias that translates into fragile robustness against distribution shifts in real-world environments. Event cameras, in contrast, record only changes in scene brightness and are therefore well suited to capturing contour information; however, due to the absence of diagnostic benchmarks in the event domain, the inductive bias that event-camera data instills in vision models has remained underexplored. In this work, we use knowledge distillation from the event domain to the RGB domain so as to exploit the rich evaluation toolkit available in the RGB domain and systematically dissect this inductive bias. Our experiments show that distillation from the event domain induces, in the RGB domain, color invariance, shape bias, and robustness to high-frequency noise. We identify the underlying mechanism as the model suppressing its dependence on high-frequency texture while acquiring a stronger dependence on edge-based object shape. This hypothesis is supported by changes in how color and spatial information are processed at the early layers, together with a spectral trade-off in which robustness to the absence of high-frequency components coexists with vulnerability to contamination of the relied-upon frequency bands and to disruption of geometric structure. We further show that this inductive bias differs from existing robustification methods and that it functions as a useful prior for diverse downstream tasks in which shape and contour information contribute alongside other cues. The code is available at https://github.com/snskysk/event2rgb-distillation .

</details>


### [8] [Atlases Are Already Inside: Recovering Population Templates from Pretrained Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30566)
*Jian Shi,John Femiani,Peter Wonka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需重新训练的扩散模型推理时采样器，可从预训练生成模型中直接提取其生成数据群体的内在图谱；该方法在多种影像与形状领域表现良好，并支持条件亚群图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图谱构建通常依赖形变配准，计算流程复杂，且难以直接适配不同数据域或条件亚群。论文希望利用扩散模型已学习到的人群结构，在推理阶段直接构建具有代表性的群体中心解剖模板。

Method: 设计一种推理时采样器，使其从任意随机种子收敛到生成群体的中心解剖，称为“内在图谱”。无需额外训练，也不使用形变配准；方法可应用于脑 MRI、胸部 X 光、人脸和三维形状，并可利用条件扩散模型构建不同条件（如年龄）下的图谱。

Result: 摘要报告称，该图谱在各数据集上作为配准目标时均达到最好或第二好的表现；在留出的脑 MRI 队列中，它是中心性最高的模板。年龄条件模型生成的图谱序列还呈现健康衰老相关的脑脊液扩张。

Conclusion: 扩散模型不仅能生成样本，也隐式编码了群体结构；内在图谱可视为这种已学习结构的直接产物，从而将图谱构建转化为生成建模的推理时副产物。

Abstract: We present a new inference-time sampler for diffusion models that gives a pretrained model a capability it was never trained for: constructing the atlas of the population it synthesizes. The sampler converges from every random seed to the population's central anatomy, which we call the \emph{intrinsic atlas}. The advantage is threefold. (1) It requires no retraining. A diffusion model that has already learned a coherent population, including the released ones, yields its atlas in a single inference pass without involving deformable registration. (2) It applies to multiple domains, such as brain MRI, chest X-ray, faces, and 3D shapes. (3) It extends to subpopulations. One age-conditioned model gives an atlas at any age in its training range, and the resulting family reproduces the CSF expansion of healthy aging. Evaluated as a registration target, the intrinsic atlas is best or second-best on every dataset against classical and learned templates, and the most central template on held-out brain MRI cohorts. Atlas construction can be reframed as a byproduct of generative modeling: a diffusion model is a learned representation of population structure, and the atlas is what it already contains.

</details>


### [9] [Action Forcing: Training World Models on Unsupervised Video by Recovering Underlying Egomotion Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30595)
*Ashish Sundar,Tiankuo Hou,Zhong Fan,Chunbo Luo,Xiaoyang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文从无标注视频中自动提取有物理语义的动作控制信号，并用其训练可控视频世界模型；提出不依赖常规视频生成指标的评估维度，实验显示模型能较好地反向、停止及组合控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练可控世界模型需要与视频时间同步、且具有明确语义的动作标注，但现有数据获取途径依赖昂贵的传感器平台或人工标注；潜在动作方法则缺少可解释的物理锚定。与此同时，常用视频生成指标难以揭示模型在控制和几何一致性方面的缺陷。

Method: 直接从普通无标注视频的帧间像素位移中追踪运动，并利用自运动造成的重复、连贯结构，通过主成分分析（PCA）估计数据驱动的自运动基底，得到带方向、可缩放且可组合的油门与转向控制信号。为避免高容量视频扩散变换器（DiT）利用像素级监督，采用在线潜在评论器蒸馏冻结的解码器—追踪器—PCA教师，且不对解码器或追踪器反向传播。评估方面提出参考无关的替代方案，衡量可控性、合理性、凭空生成物体（conjuring）及几何完整性。

Result: PCA主成分可提供有物理依据的控制轴，但只能恢复训练数据中出现过的运动轴。多数基线能够沿常见动作方向运动，却难以反向或保持静止。所提模型能实现反向、停止，并保持组合控制与生成质量；尽管训练数据中的反向动作不足1%，模型仍学会反向、线性调节响应强度，以及组合油门与转向。

Conclusion: 从无标注视频中恢复有依据的动作空间，可作为构建动作监督数据和训练可控世界模型的简洁途径。模型在稀少反向样本下仍展现出方向反转、响应缩放和控制组合能力；多维、参考无关的评估也能揭示常规视频指标遗漏的控制与几何问题。方法的运动轴覆盖范围受数据中已有运动限制。

Abstract: Synchronised action annotations are needed to train controllable world models and these datasets remain elusive. Existing approaches make use of instrumented platforms with calibrated sensors, costly manual annotation, or latent-action models which lack grounding. We instead turn ordinary unlabelled video into action-supervised training data by recovering (without training) a data-derived egomotion basis. We track pixel displacements across frames and exploit the recurring coherent structure induced by egomotion to obtain grounded control signals directly. Using a method as simple as principal components analysis perform this, we find that the leading components provide signed, scalable, and composable throttle--yaw controls, although the method can recover only motion axes represented in the data. To prevent a high-capacity video DiT from exploiting pixel-level supervision, an online latent critic distils a frozen decoder--tracker--PCA (Principal Components Analysis) teacher without backpropagating through the decoder or tracker. Finally we critique the use of video generation metrics to evaluate WMs and introduce an example of an alternative, reference-free evaluation method. We measure \textit{controllability}, \textit{plausibility}, \textit{conjuring} (creating objects out of thin air) and \textit{geometric integrity}, revealing failures that conventional video metrics miss. We show that most baselines follow familiar action directions but struggle to reverse or remain stationary. Our model handles both while retaining compositional control and generation quality. Despite backwards actions being less than $1\%$ of our training data, we find that the model learns to reverse, scale its response linearly, and compose throttle with steering, all simply by learning through a grounded action space.

</details>


### [10] [MVAgent: Multi-Agent Video Generation via Consistent Condition Construction and Shot-Level Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30609)
*Xiangyu Kong,Wenjie Zhou,Fengping Tian,Lihua Fang,Haoqin Sun,Chenyang Lyu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MVAgent：通过多智能体协作构造逐镜头条件，并用逐镜头强化学习优化多镜头视频生成；在 ViMax-Bench 上取得较强的跨镜头一致性和叙事规划表现，且在人类评估中优于最强智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多镜头视频生成需要角色外观、空间布局和角色状态在不同镜头间保持一致。但若每个镜头都独立提交给冻结的视频生成器，重复文本提示并不能可靠约束这些因素，因而容易出现镜头间漂移。

Method: MVAgent 将任务视为条件构造问题，由多个智能体协作：Spatial Grounding 从摄像机移动片段中采样视角，为每个镜头匹配合适的环境视图；Observer 记录生成镜头的结束状态并形成连续性记忆；Transition 据此为下一镜头构造角色动作与空间参考；Orchestrator 将条件组合成生成请求。训练方面提出 Trunk-GDPO，在每个镜头比较渲染候选并将最佳候选继续作为后续生成的主干，而不是仅在整段视频结束后评估。

Result: 在生成器与评审器均冻结的设定下，MVAgent 在 ViMax-Bench 的跨镜头一致性和叙事规划质量方面优于所比较的方法；人类评估也更偏好 MVAgent，而非最强的智能体基线。摘要未给出具体数值或显著性检验。

Conclusion: 通过显式构造视角、空间、角色状态和连续性条件，并采用逐镜头候选选择进行优化，MVAgent 有望缓解多镜头生成中的一致性与规划问题。其结论目前主要由摘要所述的基准测试和人类偏好评估支持。

Abstract: Multi-shot agentic video generation requires consistent character appearance, stable spatial layout across camera angles, and continuous character state between shots. When every shot is a separate request to a frozen generator, repeated text does not determine appearance, layout or state. We therefore recast the problem as condition construction and present MVAgent, a multi-agent pipeline whose agents collaborate through typed conditioning inputs. Because an environment image shows one viewpoint, a Spatial Grounding agent samples views from generated camera-traversal clips and anchors each shot to the view matching its framing. As generated shots drift from the plan, an Observer records how each shot ends in a continuity memory, from which a Transition agent builds character action and spatial references for the next shot. An Orchestrator composes these inputs into each request. Since a request reveals its effect only after rendering, we train it by agentic reinforcement learning with Trunk-GDPO, which compares rendered candidates at every shot rather than once per video and continues the best as the trunk. With generator and judges frozen, MVAgent attains the highest cross-shot consistency and narrative-planning quality among the compared methods on ViMax-Bench and is preferred over the strongest agentic baseline in human evaluation.

</details>


### [11] [MedTokenBudget: Lesion-Preserving Token Routing for Dermoscopic Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30613)
*Zhexiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MedTokenBudget，一种利用病灶掩码监督的视觉 Transformer 令牌路由方法，在受限令牌预算下优先保留病灶相关图像块；在 ISIC 2019 上，LATS 相比随机选择和 ToMe 于主要预算设置下保留更多病灶信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉 Transformer 对皮肤镜图像的所有图像块一视同仁，但诊断证据主要集中在病灶区域。现有令牌剪枝方法通常依据通用显著性或相似度进行筛选，无法直接保证预算压缩后仍保留足够的病灶证据。

Method: 提出监督式后骨干网络令牌路由框架 MedTokenBudget。其病灶感知令牌评分模块 LATS 将注意力熵、特征范数和局部特征对比度融合，通过学习得到评分，并在目标预算内选择 top-K 图像块。训练采用预算课程学习、多样性正则化、注意力蒸馏及病灶掩码监督；评估指标包括直接衡量真实病灶信息保留程度的病灶保留率。

Result: 在 ISIC 2019 数据集上，掩码监督的 LATS 在主要令牌预算设置下稳定优于随机选择和 ToMe，并保留了显著更多的病灶图像块。摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 利用病灶掩码监督令牌路由，可以让视觉 Transformer 在压缩表示时更有针对性地保留病灶证据。该方法效果得到 ISIC 2019 实验支持；代码及完整表格结果据称在论文材料中提供。

Abstract: Dermoscopy classifiers built on Vision Transformers process all image patches uniformly, although diagnostic evidence is concentrated in the lesion region. Existing token pruning methods reduce tokens using generic saliency or similarity signals, but rarely ask whether the retained subset still contains the lesion. This paper introduces MedTokenBudget, a supervised post-backbone token routing framework that learns to construct compact lesion-enriched representations when auxiliary lesion masks are available. Its Lesion-Aware Token Scoring (LATS) module fuses attention entropy, feature norm, and local feature contrast through a learned scorer, then routes the top-$K$ patches under a target budget. LATS is trained with budget curriculum learning, diversity regularization, attention distillation, and lesion-mask supervision. The trained router is evaluated with a lesion retention rate that directly measures how much ground-truth lesion evidence survives the token budget. On ISIC 2019, mask-supervised LATS consistently outperforms Random and ToMe at headline budgets while retaining substantially more lesion patches. Code is provided for reproducibility, and complete tabulated results are included in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [12] [Conditional Predictive Sufficient Statistics for Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30647)
*Yuzhou Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出条件预测充分统计量（CPSS）作为视觉表示目标：保留过去与未来共享的潜在因素，去除局部噪声；实验发现有用信息往往出现在中间层，而非最终输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文试图澄清预测式视觉学习究竟应从观测过去中提取什么信息，并解释为何预测损失可能得到有用表示、却也可能收敛到常量或产生低秩表示。

Method: 在图像块共享因子模型下形式化 CPSS，并分析过去与下一图像块之间的互信息。将下一块嵌入的余弦损失解释为方向服从 von Mises–Fisher 分布时的最大似然目标；以浅层嵌入作为回归目标，并通过 stop-gradient 阻断导致常量解的对称梯度。使用小型因果 Transformer，在 MNIST 与 CIFAR-10 上作诊断性实验。

Result: MNIST 上，改变预测未来位置及是否使用 stop-gradient，会使探针准确率变化数十个百分点；CPSS 读出在输出层之前的中间层达到峰值。CIFAR-10 在相同短训练预算且无数据增强时，各目标表现接近像素线性分类器。几何结果与推导一致：CPSS 输出不如最佳中间层，下一像素回归没有同样的读出损失；移除 stop-gradient 会使嵌入有效秩塌缩，即使预训练损失看起来很好。

Conclusion: 预测目标可作为预测信息的可处理代理，但低预训练损失并不保证获得充分、非退化的表示。stop-gradient 能阻断特定的常量解梯度机制，却不能单独保证充分统计量；模型中间层及表示几何需纳入评估。实验主要用于检验理论机制，不构成大规模基准比较。

Abstract: A useful visual representation is a statistic of the observed past that retains the latent factors shared with the future and discards patch-private noise. We formalize this requirement as a conditional predictive sufficient statistic (CPSS). Under a shared-factor model of image patches, the mutual information between the past and the next patch equals the information the past carries about the shared factor, up to a remainder that the next patch itself fails to reveal. Predicting the next patch embedding with a cosine loss is maximum likelihood for a von Mises-Fisher model of that embedding's direction, and is therefore a tractable surrogate for the predictive information. The same population loss is also minimized by a constant embedding, so stop-gradient does not by itself select the sufficient statistic; it only blocks the symmetric gradient that implements the constant solution in one step. The regression target is a shallow embedding, which forces the network output back into that shallow range and leaves the sufficient statistic in intermediate blocks. Small causal Transformers on MNIST and CIFAR-10 are used as diagnostics, not as a leaderboard. On MNIST the future shift and the stop-gradient move probe accuracy by tens of points, and the CPSS readout peaks before the output. On CIFAR-10, with the same short budget and no augmentation, every objective lands near a linear classifier on pixels. What still matches the derivation is the geometry: the CPSS output is a worse readout than its best intermediate block, next-pixel regression does not pay that penalty, and removing the stop-gradient collapses the effective rank of the embedding even when the pretext loss looks perfect.

</details>


### [13] [StarWM: Self-Supervised Trained Attention Routing for Robust World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30667)
*Zeqiang Zhang,Fabian Wurzberger,Maximilian Otte,Daniel Schmid,Sebastian Gottwald,Arne Peter Raulf,Daniel Alexander Braun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 StarWM，通过动态相关性引导的选择性重建，在保留任务相关视觉信息的同时抑制视频背景干扰；在 DeepMind Control 动态背景实验中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型需要既准确捕捉环境动力学，又忽略与任务无关的视觉内容。全图重建容易按像素面积分配表示能力，使大面积干扰内容主导表征；不使用重建的方法则可能丢弃有用信息。

Method: StarWM 使用基于自监督动力学训练的交叉注意力模块，判定哪些区域适合重建；双流解码器仅对注意区域进行重建，并通过 stop-gradient 屏障隔离动力学学习与重建目标，减少非预测性信息对潜变量的干扰。另提出加入奖励信息的变体。

Result: 在带动态视频背景的 DeepMind Control 任务中，默认的无奖励 StarWM 在随机帧干扰下表现最佳，并在连续视频背景下显著优于重建式基线。奖励增强版在连续视频背景下匹配或超过无重建方法，并在各类干扰条件下取得最高总体回报。机制分析显示，StarWM 能在长时域想象中近乎完好地保留状态属性，同时系统性地丢弃干扰信息。

Conclusion: 选择性重建可在视觉监督与任务相关表征之间取得平衡，使世界模型保留有助于预测和控制的内容、抑制动态背景干扰；实验支持 StarWM 在复杂视觉干扰下的有效性。

Abstract: A robust world model must strike the balance between faithfully capturing environmental dynamics and abstracting away from irrelevant content. While reconstruction-based world models ensure faithful supervision, they misallocate representational capacity by pixel area rather than dynamics relevance for visual tasks, which can cause task-irrelevant content to dominate the learned representation. Alternatively, reconstruction-free methods avoid this bias but risk discarding possibly relevant information. We propose StarWM, which uses a cross-attention module trained on self-supervised dynamics to decide where reconstruction applies. A dual-stream decoder then restricts reconstruction to the attended regions, with stop-gradient barriers preventing interference between the two objectives. These components allows reconstruction to supervise the visual content of attended regions without contaminating the latent with non-predictive information. On DeepMind Control with dynamic video backgrounds, default (reward-free) StarWM achieves the strongest performance under random-frame distractors and substantially outperforms reconstruction-based baselines under sequential video. In addition, its reward-augmented variant matches or exceeds reconstruction-free methods on sequential video, achieving the highest overall return across all distractor regimes. Mechanistic probing confirms StarWM preserves state attributes with near-perfect fidelity through long-horizon imagination while systematically discarding distractors.

</details>


### [14] [Structure-Guided Masked Autoencoders for Ultra-High Resolution Scientific Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30682)
*Enzhi Zhang,Du Wu,Rui Zhong,Cong Ma,Isaac Lyngaas,Amir Koushyar Ziabari,Xiao Wang,Peng Chen,Tao Luo,Toshio Endo,Fumiyoshi Shoji,Kento Sato,Kentaro Uesugi,Takayuki Nonoyama,Ryuji Kiyama,Masahiro Yoshida,Masaru Tezuka,Tetsuya Ishikawa,Satoshi Matsuoka,Masaharu Munetomo,Mohamed Wahib*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SGMA 面向超高分辨率科学图像提出结构引导的掩码自编码预训练框架。它通过内容自适应四叉树分词将图像压缩为固定长度序列，并利用结构条件掩码与阻尼累积（DA）突出空间信息丰富区域。在多类显微与CT数据集上优于同架构MAE基线，特定任务Dice提升显著，推理速度最高提升24.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将MAE等自监督视觉Transformer预训练方法用于千兆像素科学图像时面临两项核心困难：随机掩码不适配科学图像具有结构化、多尺度特征的形态；均匀分词会生成过长序列，使二次复杂度注意力计算难以承受。

Method: 提出SGMA，由两个主要机制构成：内容自适应四叉树分词器，将超高分辨率图像压缩为固定长度序列；结构条件掩码过程，使重建更关注空间信息丰富的区域。另提出阻尼累积（DA），在四叉树不同尺度间聚合依赖信号的响应，形成用于引导掩码的结构画布。框架兼容标准ViT编码器与MAE式重建。

Result: 在电子显微镜、全切片光学显微镜和X射线CT数据集上，SGMA整体优于MAE基线。SpringXCT（8K × 8K × 28K）上达到95.68% Dice，较同架构MAE高13.00个百分点；PAIP WSI（32K²）上达到83.21% Dice，高16.84个百分点；推理速度最高提升24.8倍。

Conclusion: SGMA通过结合自适应层次分词和结构感知掩码，缓解了超高分辨率科学图像中的序列规模与掩码策略问题，并在所报告数据集上兼顾了细微结构保留、任务性能和推理效率。摘要未提供具体实验设置、统计显著性或各数据集上的完整比较细节。

Abstract: Self-supervised pre-training with Vision Transformers, including Masked Autoencoders (MAE), is difficult to apply to gigapixel scientific images. Random masking is poorly matched to the structured, multi-scale morphology of scientific data, while uniform tokenization produces prohibitively long sequences that make $O(N^2)$ attention impractical. We propose SGMA, a structure-guided masked autoencoding framework for ultra-high-resolution scientific images. SGMA couples two components: a content-adaptive quadtree tokenizer that compresses gigapixel images into a fixed-length sequence, and a structure-conditioned masking process that biases reconstruction toward spatially informative regions. To stabilize this process across scales, we introduce Damped Accumulation (DA), which aggregates signal-dependent responses across the tree into a structure canvas used to guide masking. The resulting pre-training task preserves fine microstructure while remaining compatible with standard ViT encoders and MAE-style reconstruction. Across electron microscopy, whole-slide optical microscopy, and X-ray CT datasets, SGMA consistently outperforms MAE baselines. It achieves 95.68% Dice on the 8K x 8K x 28K SpringXCT dataset, improving over the same-architecture MAE baseline by +13.00 points, and 83.21% Dice on the 32K^2 WSI PAIP dataset, improving by +16.84 points, while providing up to a 24.8x inference speedup.

</details>


### [15] [MM-VeriAgent: Learning to Use Extensive Tools to Verify Multimodal Misinformation with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30698)
*Peipei Li,Shuhan Xia,Shengyang Liu,Zekun Li,Ran He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MM-VeriAgent：通过强化学习训练多模态大语言模型调用专用核验工具，检测混合来源的多模态虚假信息；训练时使用工具执行缓存减少在线调用，实验显示检测准确率提升且训练效率改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的多模态虚假信息可能同时包含文本、图像及跨模态伪造，需按样本灵活选用检测手段。既有工具增强方法依赖固定工作流或推理时规划，前者适应性有限，后者增加推理成本；而专用工具在强化学习训练期间频繁在线执行，也会拖慢训练。

Method: 构建 MM-VeriTools，基于子任务基准测试选出文本、视觉和跨模态伪造分析方法，并封装为统一接口的可调用工具。随后通过强化学习训练 LVLM 学习工具使用策略。另提出 Tool-Execution Cache，预先执行候选工具调用并缓存输出，在训练 rollout 中复用，以减少在线工具执行，同时保留多步交互过程。

Result: 在 MMFakeBench 上，MM-VeriAgent 相较于不进行显式工具搜索的基础模型取得显著准确率提升。消融实验支持所学工具使用策略的有效性；效率分析表明，Tool-Execution Cache 减少了训练中的在线工具调用。

Conclusion: 该工作将专用检测工具、强化学习策略学习与执行缓存结合，使模型能够在不依赖推理时显式工具搜索的情况下，更有效地检测混合来源多模态虚假信息，并提升训练效率。摘要未提供具体指标、数据集划分或统计显著性信息，因而无法进一步量化提升幅度。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal misinformation often involves mixed forgery sources, requiring sample-specific detection strategies. Existing tool-augmented methods rely on predefined workflows or inference-time planning, limiting adaptability or increasing inference cost. To address this issue, we introduce \textbf{MM-VeriAgent}, which learns to verify mixed-source multimodal misinformation with tools. We first build \textbf{MM-VeriTools}, a specialized toolkit for misinformation detection agents. By benchmarking various candidate models and methods on the sub-tasks required by mixed-source detection, we select the strongest for textual, visual, and cross-modal forgery analysis and encapsulate them as callable tools with a unified interface. On top of this toolkit, we train the LVLM agent with reinforcement learning to teach it how to use these tools to better solve mixed-source detection. Since many of the tools are specialized models whose online execution at every rollout severely limits RL efficiency, we further introduce \textbf{Tool-Execution Cache}, which pre-executes candidate tool calls and reuses their cached outputs during training. This preserves multi-step rollouts while reducing online tool execution, largely improving the training efficiency.Experiments on MMFakeBench demonstrate substantial accuracy gains over the base model without explicit tool search at inference time. Ablation and efficiency analyses further validate the learned tool-use policy and show that Tool-Execution Cache reduces online tool executions during training.

</details>


### [16] [SAGE: Source-Anchored Guidance via Frequency Equalization for Hierarchical RGB-T Alignment and Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30703)
*Timing Li,Yiming Sun,Boan Tao,Xiyuan Gao,Haifang Cao,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 SAGE，将频率均衡、分层配准与子带融合统一起来，旨在改善弱配准 RGB-T 图像的对齐与融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-T 图像存在空间错位和跨模态差异，容易造成重影、结构模糊及内容失衡。现有方法常将外观适配、几何对齐和信息融合分阶段处理，限制了各阶段间的依赖信息传递。

Method: SAGE 采用可逆联合编码和源特定低频调制，提取结构与增益引导并保留源信息；先由低频近似估计全局仿射几何，再将几何和上下文线索传递至高频相关性推理，进行可靠性感知的残差细化；随后在传递的源信息与对齐引导下联合聚合对齐后的频率系数，并通过逆小波变换重建融合图像。

Result: 在包含真实与合成错位的 RGB-T 数据集上，SAGE 在图像对齐和融合方面均取得持续且有竞争力的表现。摘要未提供具体数据集、指标或数值。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持该统一框架的有效性，表明源锚定引导有助于弱配准 RGB-T 图像的对齐与融合。

Abstract: Spatial misregistration and cross-modal discrepancies often cause ghosting, structural blurring, and content imbalance in RGB-T fusion. Existing methods typically decouple appearance adaptation, geometric alignment, and information fusion, limiting dependency propagation across stages. We propose Source-Anchored Guidance via Frequency Equalization for Hierarchical RGB-T Alignment and Fusion (SAGE), a unified framework integrating frequency equalization, hierarchical alignment, and subband fusion. SAGE employs invertible joint encoding and source-specific low-frequency modulation to derive structural and gain guidance while preserving source information. Hierarchical frequency collaborative alignment estimates global affine geometry from low-frequency approximations and transfers geometric and contextual cues to high-frequency correlation reasoning for reliability-aware residual refinement. Guided subband fusion jointly aggregates the aligned frequency coefficients under propagated source and alignment guidance, coordinates complementary low- and high-frequency information, and reconstructs the fused image through the inverse wavelet transform. Extensive experiments on RGB-T datasets with real-world and synthetic misalignments demonstrate consistently competitive performance in alignment and fusion, validating the effectiveness of source-anchored guidance for weakly registered RGB-T images.

</details>


### [17] [Combining General and Domain-Specific Pretext Tasks for Brain MR Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30708)
*Tasneem Nasser,Susanne Schmid,Roberto Souza,Naser El-Sheimy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究比较脑龄预测与图像修复两种自监督预训练任务，并提出联合多任务框架；该框架在多数脑MRI分割实验中优于单任务预训练和从头训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特定人群和疾病的医学影像标注数据稀缺，深度学习对标注数据依赖较强，因此需要通过自监督预训练学习可迁移表示，降低人工标注需求。

Method: 将体素级脑龄预测作为神经影像领域特定的自监督前置任务，将图像修复作为通用前置任务；进一步联合优化两项任务，学习互补表示。通过多发性硬化病灶、缺血性卒中病灶和皮层脑结构三类MRI分割任务评估，并与单任务预训练及从头训练比较。

Result: 联合多任务预训练在多数实验设置下持续优于单任务预训练模型和从头训练。摘要未提供具体数值、统计显著性或各任务的详细表现。

Conclusion: 结合领域特定与通用自监督任务，有望提升脑MRI表示的可迁移性，支持构建更具泛化能力的神经影像基础模型。

Abstract: A key challenge in medical image analysis is the scarcity of large annotated datasets for specific populations and diseases. As deep learning models rely heavily on labeled data, effective transfer learning strategies are needed to reduce the dependence on manual annotations. Self-supervised learning has emerged as a promising approach for developing foundation models by enabling the learning of transferable feature representations from large-scale unlabeled medical imaging datasets. In this study, we investigate voxel-level brain age prediction as a domain-specific self-supervised pretext task and compare it with image inpainting, a widely used non-domain-specific alternative. We further propose a multitask self-supervised pretraining framework that jointly optimizes both objectives to learn complementary neuroimaging representations. The pretrained models are evaluated on three downstream magnetic resonance image segmentation tasks: multiple sclerosis lesion segmentation, ischemic stroke lesion segmentation, and cortical brain structure segmentation. Overall, the proposed multitask pretraining framework consistently outperformed the single-task pretrained models and training from scratch across most experimental settings, demonstrating the benefit of combining domain-specific and general self-supervised learning pretext tasks for the development of generalizable neuroimaging foundation models.\ Code Availability: The source code used in this study is publicly available at https://github.com/TasneemN/Combining-General-and-Domain-Specific-Pretext-Tasks-for-Brain-MR-Image-Segmentation/

</details>


### [18] [VLALight: Lightweight Vision-Language-Action Models for Emergency-Aware Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30709)
*Kemou Jiang,Maonan Wang,Xingchen Zou,Jiayue Zhu,Yuhang Fu,Sicheng Wang,Xi Chen,Yirong Chen,Zhiyong Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 VLALight，一个轻量级端到端视觉-语言-动作交通信号控制框架，可直接根据路口图像和信号相位信息预测离散信号动作。实验显示其紧急车辆服务表现优于对比方法，并具备实时运行及跨路口、跨交通流模式泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统或级联式视觉语言交通信号控制存在模块耦合松散、信息反复转换的问题，可能损失细粒度视觉信息；顺序推理也会带来较高延迟。论文旨在实现更直接、高效且能利用视觉上下文的信号控制。

Method: 将多个方向摄像头视图融合为统一视觉输入，并通过文本指令描述视图与交通流向、信号相位之间的对应关系。采用紧凑的 0.5B 参数模型，端到端地将路口观测和信号相位信息映射为离散信号动作，无需中间图像转文本描述或人工设计的交通状态表示。

Result: 实验中，VLALight 在所有对比方法中提供了最佳的紧急车辆服务；与级联式 VLMLight 相比，汇总紧急车辆等待时间降低 21.1%。模型可在本地硬件上实时运行，并能泛化到未见过的路口拓扑和交通流模式。

Conclusion: 结果表明，端到端视觉-语言-动作建模有望减少中间表示造成的信息损失与推理延迟，同时提升紧急车辆优先服务能力，并保持轻量化和一定的场景泛化能力。

Abstract: Traffic signal control (TSC) is essential for mitigating urban congestion. Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) enable richer interpretation of intersection scenes, opening new opportunities for visual-context-aware TSC. However, the loose coupling and repeated information conversion between modules can lead to the loss of fine-grained visual details, while sequential inference introduces substantial latency. To address these limitations, we propose VLALight, a lightweight end-to-end vision-language-action framework that directly maps intersection observations and signal-phase information to discrete signal actions. To handle the multi-view nature of TSC, VLALight combines multiple directional camera views into a unified visual input and uses textual instructions to establish their correspondence with traffic movements and signal phases. This design enables direct action prediction with a compact 0.5 B-parameter model, without intermediate image-to-text descriptions or handcrafted traffic-state representations. Experiments show that VLALight delivers the best emergency-vehicle service of all compared methods, reducing pooled emergency waiting time by 21.1% over the cascaded VLMLight while running in real time on local hardware and generalizing to unseen intersection topologies and traffic-flow patterns.

</details>


### [19] [TrafficImag: A Benchmark for Counterfactual Roadside Traffic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30722)
*Xiangyu Li,Tianyi Wang,Zhihao Dou,Christian Claudel,Zhaomiao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrafficImag 是面向路侧交通反事实视频生成的基准，整合数据集与可执行干预协议，并从状态、路线行为、交互及非目标保持四方面评估生成结果。当前最佳端到端成功率为55.0%，主要瓶颈在条件视频执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路侧交通数据集覆盖感知、预测和视觉问答，但缺乏对反事实视频生成的评估：当指定交通参与者行为被修改时，生成未来视频还需符合道路拓扑并保持其他交通主体的一致性。

Method: 构建 TrafficImag，包含9,022张标注图像、7,043段去重视频片段及31,145个以参与者为中心的历史—未来样本。每项干预以参与者级程序表示目标、意图行为、合法路线、交互顺序和时间约束，并通过统一协议支持行为推理、干预感知图像编辑与条件视频生成。评估维度包括初始状态正确性、路线与行为有效性、交互一致性及非目标保持；四项均满足才判定端到端成功。

Result: 最强推理模型的宏平均F1为80.4%；完整条件接口使最佳生成器的端到端成功率从23.3%提升至55.0%。Oracle研究表明，条件视频执行是当前主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: TrafficImag提供可复现的反事实交通视频生成基准，能够超越单纯感知视频质量的评估，并诊断系统在推理、约束执行和保持无关交通内容方面的表现。

Abstract: Existing roadside traffic datasets support perception, forecasting, and visual question answering, but they do not evaluate counterfactual video generation, in which a selected actor is modified and the generated future should remain consistent with road topology and unrelated traffic. We introduce TrafficImag, the first benchmark for counterfactual roadside traffic video generation. TrafficImag combines a large-scale roadside dataset (9,022 annotated images, 7,043 deduplicated video clips, and 31,145 actor-centered history-future samples) with an executable protocol that supports behavior reasoning, intervention-aware image editing, and conditional video generation. Each intervention is represented as an actor-level program describing the target actor, intended behavior, legal route, interaction order, and temporal constraints, enabling a unified evaluation interface across heterogeneous foundation models. TrafficImag evaluates four complementary validity dimensions: initial-state correctness, route and behavior validity, interaction consistency, and non-target preservation, and considers an end-to-end counterfactual successful only when all four are satisfied. Across state-of-the-art foundation models, the strongest reasoner reaches 80.4% macro F1, the complete condition interface raises end-to-end success from 23.3% to 55.0% for the best generator. Oracle studies further show that conditional video execution is the primary remaining bottleneck. TrafficImag provides a reproducible benchmark for evaluating and diagnosing counterfactual traffic video generation beyond perceptual video quality.

</details>


### [20] [EviDETR: Preserving Query-Relevant Temporal Evidence for Moment Retrieval and Highlight Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30724)
*Haoran Sun,Yufan Li,Qichen Zhang,Haoran Zhao,Shuqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 EviDETR，通过证据保留机制联合完成视频片段检索与高光检测，并在 QVHighlights 等数据集上取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合视频时刻检索与高光检测既要定位与文本查询相关的时间片段，也要估计视频片段的显著性。论文指出，DETR 风格方法未能在编码、解码及跨任务预测过程中显式保留查询相关证据，导致两项任务间的信息利用不足。

Method: 提出 EviDETR，包含三个模块：1）语义感知特征重加权（SFR），通过显著性估计与跨模态交互增强查询相关片段表示；2）时间 Top-2 专家混合解码器（TTop2MoE），通过稀疏专家路由进行查询自适应细化；3）检索到高光融合（MR2HD），以置信度加权的多尺度聚合，将片段级检索证据传递给片段级高光预测。使用 CLIP 与 SlowFast 特征。

Result: 在 QVHighlights 上，时刻检索达到 R1@0.5 69.29、R1@0.7 54.77、Avg. mAP 48.41；高光检测达到 HD-mAP 41.83、HIT@1 68.33。论文还报告了在 TACoS 和 Charades-STA 上的强结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在检索与高光检测的流程中保留并传递查询相关证据，有助于提升联合预测表现；在多个数据集上的结果也支持该方法具有一定的跨数据集迁移能力。

Abstract: Joint video moment retrieval and highlight detection requires identifying query-relevant temporal segments while estimating clip-level saliency, yet DETR-style pipelines do not explicitly preserve query-relevant evidence throughout encoding, decoding, and cross-task prediction. We propose EviDETR, an evidence-preserving framework with three components. Semantic-aware Feature Reweighting (SFR) enhances query-relevant clip representations through saliency estimation and cross-modal interaction. A Temporal Top-2 Mixture-of-Experts (TTop2MoE) decoder performs query-adaptive refinement via sparse expert routing. MR-to-HD (MR2HD) fusion transfers span-level retrieval evidence to clip-level highlight prediction through confidence-weighted multi-scale aggregation. Using CLIP+SlowFast features, EviDETR achieves 69.29 R1@0.5, 54.77 R1@0.7, and 48.41 Avg. mAP for moment retrieval on QVHighlights, together with 41.83 HD-mAP and 68.33 HIT@1. Strong results on TACoS and Charades-STA further demonstrate cross-dataset transferability.

</details>


### [21] [Learning Polarization Image Restoration with General Restoration Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30728)
*Chenggong Li,Jinhao Liu,Caiyun Wu,Yidong Luo,Junchao Zhang,Degui Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向多种及复合退化的全能型偏振图像复原框架。方法采用归一化 Stokes 表示和强度/偏振双分支结构，并利用预训练通用复原先验、混合专家及跨域知识蒸馏提升偏振复原能力；同时构建复合退化偏振基准。实验表明方法在公开数据集和新基准上有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中的偏振成像常受多种相互耦合的退化影响，复原是偏振视觉应用的重要前提。现有方法通常针对单一退化设计，且偏振数据的规模与质量有限，难以处理多样或复合退化。

Method: 比较不同偏振表示对复原的影响，选用归一化 Stokes 表示以分离强度与偏振信息；设计强度与偏振双分支架构。强度分支利用预训练通用图像复原先验，并通过混合专家扩展至复合退化；再以跨域特征变换，将强度分支的复原知识自适应蒸馏到对称的偏振分支。此外，建立复合退化偏振图像基准。

Result: 作者报告，在公开数据集及其构建的复合退化偏振基准上的大量实验验证了该方法的有效性。摘要未提供具体指标、对比幅度或消融实验结果。

Conclusion: 该研究通过表示选择、双分支建模、通用复原先验迁移和新基准建设，尝试解决偏振复原对数据规模的依赖及对复合退化适应不足的问题。其核心思路是借助强度域较丰富的复原知识增强偏振域复原。

Abstract: Polarization imaging captures distinctive surface and geometric cues that benefit a wide range of vision tasks. However, real-world polarization acquisition is often affected by multiple coupled degradations, making image restoration essential for practical polarization vision. Existing methods are largely tailored to specific degradations and remain constrained by the limited scale and quality of polarization data. To address these limitations, we develop an all-in-one polarization restoration framework for diverse and composite degradations. We first study the impact of different polarization representations on restoration performance and identify the normalized Stokes representation as an effective choice for separating intensity and polarization information. Accordingly, we devise a dual-branch architecture that separates intensity and polarization modeling. To overcome the limitations of polarization-specific training, the intensity branch leverages pretrained general restoration priors and a mixture-of-experts extension for composite degradations, while its restoration knowledge is adaptively distilled into the symmetric polarization branch via a cross-domain feature transform. In addition, we establish a composite-degradation polarization benchmark to support all-in-one restoration research. Extensive experiments on public datasets and our proposed benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [22] [Amplify What You Gaze At: Target Saliency Boosting in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30733)
*Shengqi Dang,Zhengxi Yu,Feilin Han,Xingyu Lan,Nan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 GazeME，通过在文本提示中加入可学习的标记，控制文本生成图像中指定对象的视觉显著性，同时尽量保持语义一致性与图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像生成已能控制物体内容、位置和呈现方式，但生成场景中注意力如何在不同物体间分配尚未得到充分研究。论文将提升指定对象显著性定义为 Target Saliency Boosting 任务，并指出显著性具有相对性：突出目标也需要考虑场景中其他对象的整体显著性。

Method: 提出轻量框架 GazeME，在对象描述周围插入可学习的标记 token，以指示需要强调或弱化的对象。作者构建包含图像—提示词配对、对象级显著性分数的 saliency-semantics 数据集，并提出 Saliency Prior Marker Activation（SPMA），利用对象间的相对显著性关系进行随机标记激活，以增强训练鲁棒性。推理时，GazeME 自动为提示词插入合适标记。

Result: 摘要报告的大量实验表明，GazeME 能有效提升目标对象的视觉显著性，并保持语义对齐和图像质量。摘要未提供具体数据集、基线或量化指标，因此无法据此评估提升幅度。

Conclusion: 该工作把文本到图像生成中的对象级注意力控制作为新任务，利用相对显著性建模和提示词标记实现无需视觉先验的目标显著性调节。其主要价值在于提供一种轻量、可通过文本提示直接操作的控制机制；实际效果与泛化能力仍需结合论文中的定量实验和更广泛场景验证。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation has advanced in controlling what, where, and how objects appear, yet how visual attention is distributed among objects remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we introduce Target Saliency Boosting, a new task aimed at boosting the visual saliency of a specific object during text-to-image generation without requiring any visual priors. Our key insight is that visual saliency is inherently relative: boosting the saliency of a target object also depends on the global saliency distribution across all objects in the scene. Based on this insight, we propose GazeME, a lightweight framework that uses saliency-marked prompts, inserting learnable marker tokens around object descriptions to indicate which objects to visually emphasize or suppress. To learn these markers, we construct a saliency-semantics dataset that associates objects in image--prompt pairs with object-level saliency scores, and propose Saliency Prior Marker Activation (SPMA), a saliency-aware stochastic marker activation strategy that exploits relative saliency relationships for robust training. During inference, GazeME automatically inserts appropriate markers into the prompt, thereby directly enhancing the visual saliency of the target object. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GazeME effectively boosts target saliency while preserving both semantic alignment and image quality.

</details>


### [23] [From Mono to Stereo: Accelerating Binocular Gaussian Splatting via Reprojection and Selective Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30741)
*Hongfei Zhu,Ling Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向静态场景双目渲染的2D高斯泼溅加速流程：完整渲染主眼，再将图像重投影至另一只眼并修补空洞；相较顺序双遍渲染，时间降低15.5%–28.8%、峰值显存降低6%–11%，但辅眼画质略有下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双目渲染需为同一场景生成两个相邻视角，传统逐眼完整渲染会重复大量可见性计算和着色工作。论文希望减少这部分冗余，同时控制重投影造成的画面缺失与质量损失。

Method: 使用2D Gaussian Splatting完整渲染主眼RGB图像，并利用主眼光栅化时已有的alpha混合权重计算加权深度代理，避免额外深度渲染。随后将图像重投影到辅眼：对小型内部空洞进行插值，对较大的去遮挡区域通过自适应ROI生成器定位并选择性重渲染。ROI依据重投影图像边界确定，并可选用连通中心空洞检测。

Result: 在DTU、Tanks and Temples及MipNeRF-360数据集上，相较顺序双遍双目参考方案，测得渲染时间减少15.5%–28.8%，峰值GPU显存减少6%–11%。辅眼图像质量的最大退化为1.3 dB PSNR、0.02 SSIM和0.02 LPIPS。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了静态场景双目渲染中具有实际意义的效率—画质折衷，但画质下降是否可接受取决于具体应用，需要进行感知验证。当前结论主要适用于受控静态场景，后续还需在连续运动场景和实体VR硬件上评估。

Abstract: Binocular rendering requires two nearby views of the same scene and therefore repeats substantial visibility and shading work. We present a 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) pipeline that fully renders a dominant-eye RGB image and an alpha-weighted depth proxy, reprojects that image to the affiliated eye, and repairs uncovered pixels. Small interior gaps are interpolated, whereas larger disoccluded regions are identified as regions of interest (ROIs) and selectively re-rendered. The depth proxy reuses the alpha-blending weights computed during dominant-eye rasterization, avoiding a separate depth-rendering pass. An adaptive ROI generator localizes the required updates using reprojected image boundaries and optional connected center-hole detection. On DTU, Tanks and Temples, and MipNeRF-360, the method reduces the measured time of a sequential two-pass binocular reference by 15.5\% to 28.8\% and peak GPU memory by 6\% to 11\%. The corresponding affiliated-eye quality degradation is at most 1.3 dB PSNR, 0.02 SSIM, and 0.02 LPIPS, representing a measurable trade-off that requires application-specific perceptual validation. These results establish a practical efficiency-quality trade-off for controlled static-scene stereo rendering and motivate future evaluation under continuous motion and on physical VR hardware.

</details>


### [24] [Training-Free Bottleneck Width Planning for Convolutional Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30755)
*Guannan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MS-SRD：无需训练神经网络，依据训练图像协方差与给定 NMSE 阈值，估计不同空间切点所需的瓶颈通道数；在所测数据上预测精度较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络结构宽度通常通过大量训练和验证试错来确定，成本高且难以预先判断瓶颈通道需求。论文希望建立一种基于数据统计、无需拟合网络的宽度估计方法，并兼顾不同尺度结构及可部署模型选择。

Method: MS-SRD 根据训练图像和用户指定空间切点计算协方差谱尾部，以 NMSE 约束估计所需通道数；对共享线性、分块卷积自编码器的平方误差情形，该协方差尾部规则具有精确性。论文还利用嵌套尺度支配关系，同时报告激活量—参数量 Pareto 前沿和最小潜变量候选，并在非线性 patch 自编码器及闭合跳连 U 形自编码器上进行比较。

Result: 在 NMSE≤0.01、13 个灰度数据集的实验中，潜变量规模预测相对非线性 patch 自编码器边界的平均绝对百分比误差为 0.84%；13 项中 10 项完全准确，其余均只差 1 个通道。四数据集可部署比较中，MS-SRD 与所有回顾性外部宽度一致，所选四个模型均通过阈值，无需训练选择器；46 次拟合的验证网格和 4 次 Least-Volume 拟合则各仅在两个数据集上通过。闭合跳连 U 形自编码器实验中，14 项预测有 5 项准确、9 项相差 1 个通道，所有失败预测均少估 1 个通道。阈值放宽时，非线性方法相对基线的节省逐渐增大。

Conclusion: 结果支持 MS-SRD 作为低成本、无需训练的结构宽度估计与候选筛选工具，在特定设置下预测接近非线性模型的经验边界。其理论精确性有明确适用范围；在非线性及 U 形结构上的表现主要是实验验证，且误差呈现少估一个通道的倾向。

Abstract: Multiscale Spectral Rate-Distortion (MS-SRD) estimates the bottleneck channels required at user-supplied spatial cuts from training images and a normalized mean-squared error (NMSE) bound, without fitting a neural network. Its covariance-tail rule is exact for shared linear block-convolutional autoencoders under squared error. A nested-scale dominance result motivates reporting the activation-parameter Pareto frontier alongside the minimum-latent candidate. At NMSE <= 0.01 on thirteen grayscale datasets, its latent-size prediction has 0.84% mean absolute percentage error against nonlinear patch-autoencoder boundaries; ten predictions are exact and the remaining three differ by one channel. In a four-dataset deployable comparison, MS-SRD matches all retrospective external widths and all four selected models pass, without training a selector; a 46-fit validation grid and four Least-Volume fits each pass on two datasets. In a skip-closed U-shaped autoencoder at the same bound, five predictions are exact, nine are within one channel, and every failing prediction is one channel short. Experiments at looser bounds show progressively larger nonlinear savings.

</details>


### [25] [LLPR: Location-aware learning and physics-based reconstruction for raindrop removal from a single image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30758)
*Zewei He,Xingyu Liu,Xing Luo,Guizhong Fu,Zixuan Chen,Yu Chen,Jinlei Li,Zhe-Ming Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 LLPR（位置感知学习与基于物理模型的重建）框架，用于单幅图像去雨滴；通过训练期位置监督和显式物理分解提升效果，推理时不增加位置分支开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雨滴附着在窗户或镜头上会遮挡并扭曲背景。现有方法主要改进 CNN/Transformer 架构，或额外使用预处理子网络预测雨滴掩码，后者会增加参数量和计算成本；直接重建背景也未充分利用雨滴成像的物理过程。

Method: 框架包含两项设计：一是训练阶段加入位置感知分支，使编码器学习雨滴位置，该分支在推理时可移除；二是采用基于物理模型的重建，先估计透明度矩阵和雨滴层，再由物理关系反向推导背景层。方法可与现成 CNN 或 Transformer 架构结合，并构建真实雨滴退化图像数据集。

Result: 作者报告，在所构建的真实数据集及实验中，LLPR 相较当前先进的单幅图像去雨滴方法取得更优表现，并显示出较好的有效性与通用性。摘要未提供具体指标或实验设置。

Conclusion: 通过在训练期注入雨滴位置信息，并以显式物理分解替代直接背景重建，LLPR 在不增加推理阶段位置分支开销的前提下改善去雨滴性能；其结论仍需结合论文全文中的定量结果、数据集规模与跨架构实验细节评估。

Abstract: Raindrops can cause occlusion and distortion in the background scenes due to their adherence to windows or camera lenses. Existing raindrop removal methods concentrate on designing sophisticated CNN or Transformer architectures to recover distorted and missing texture. In this paper, we try to integrate location information and physical model into off-the-shelf CNN or Transformer architectures to help improve their performance. Specifically, we notice that existing methods deploy a preprocessing sub-network to generate a binary or soft mask to indicate the raindrop location, which will increase the network parameters and computational complexity. In contrast, a location-aware learning branch is embedded to teach the encoder in the training phase with the capability of perceiving the position of the raindrops. Note that this location-aware learning branch can be removed during the inference process (achieving performance improvements at no cost). Furthermore, instead of directly reconstructing the raindrop-free image (i.e., background scene), we devise a physics-based reconstruction scheme to first learn the transparency matrix and the raindrop layer. The latent background layer is then reversely derived based on the physical model. By combining the above-mentioned components, we propose our location-aware learning and physics-based reconstruction (LLPR) framework for this challenging ill-posed problem. We also collect a real-world raindrop-degraded image dataset, which is challenging for single-image raindrop removal (SIRR) methods. Extensive experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of our LLPR framework, achieving superior performance against state-of-the-art SIRR methods. The code will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [26] [Timo: $\textbf{T}$aming Mult$\textbf{i}$modal Diffusion Transformer for Human $\textbf{Mo}$tion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30761)
*Zhao Wang,Jiangtao Hu,Jack Yu,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Timo：一种面向文本驱动人体动作生成的运动学感知多模态扩散 Transformer，在统一基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 HMG 方法多用交叉注意力注入文本语义，缺少文本与动作 token 的双向建模；直接将 MMDiT 与流匹配用于动作生成，又未充分考虑动作的时间连贯性与关节间相关性较弱的特点，容易产生协调性差、抖动的动作。

Method: Timo 将全共享多模态注意力与流匹配结合，实现文本—动作双向建模；加入几何与旋转运动学监督，比较实际旋转及其时间变化；采用两阶段课程学习，先学习广泛动作模式，再强化细粒度文本对齐。论文另构建包含六个公开数据集、共 40,025 个留出片段的基准，并以统一评估器和评分协议从六个维度评测。

Result: 据摘要，Timo 在定量与定性评估中均显著优于当前先进方法；在六项基准维度中有五项超过 Kimodo，平均基准分数相对提升 40.8%。

Conclusion: 摘要的核心贡献是针对人体动作的时序与关节运动学特性改造 MMDiT，并通过双向文本—动作交互、运动学监督和课程学习提升生成质量与文本对齐。所报告结果显示该方案具有较强竞争力；但摘要未提供具体指标、基线设置或显著性检验，无法据此判断各组件的独立贡献及结论的统计稳健性。

Abstract: Most existing human motion generation (HMG) methods use cross-attention modules to inject text semantics, but ignore the importance of bidirectional modeling between motion and text tokens, which limits text comprehension. A straightforward idea is introducing multimodal diffusion transformers (MMDiT), which have shown effective joint text--visual modeling in vision generation, into HMG. However, we find that articulated motion is temporally coherent but weakly correlated across joints, in which directly applying an MMDiT with flow matching produces poorly coordinated and jerky motion. In this work, we propose Timo, a novel kinematics-aware MMDiT framework tailored for HMG. Timo combines fully shared multimodal attention for bidirectional text--motion modeling with flow matching, geometric and rotational-kinematics supervision that compares actual rotations and their changes over time, and a two-stage curriculum progressing from broad motion learning to detailed caption alignment. Further, we construct a benchmark of $40{,}025$ held-out clips from six public datasets spanning diverse actions, assessing six complementary dimensions under a common evaluator and scoring protocol. Our model substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both quantitative and qualitative evaluations. Remarkably, Timo surpasses Kimodo on five of six dimensions, achieving a $40.8$% relative improvement in the average benchmark score. Project page: https://kyfafyd.wang/projects/timo. Demo page: https://timo.kyfafyd.wang.

</details>


### [27] [Query-Conditioned Prototype Adaptation for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning: Single-Query Inference, Controlled Comparisons, and Failure Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30769)
*Rushab Rasik Karania,Tomas Maul*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究固定全局视觉表征下，联合处理查询样本与支持样本能否改进跨域少样本分类的原型构造。WIPT在部分低样本目标域有小幅收益，但效果依赖数据集与设置，并非普遍优于ProtoNet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域少样本学习需要在仅有少量目标域标注、且测试时不更新模型参数的条件下适配分类器。本文聚焦于一个具体问题：固定表征后，查询—支持联合适配对类别原型构造有何贡献？

Method: 提出Within-Instance Prototypical Transformer（WIPT）：在测试时联合变换一个未标注查询及带标签支持集嵌入，再据此构造该查询专属的类别均值原型。使用冻结的ViT-S/16编码器，在miniImageNet上训练，并在CUB、EuroSAT和ISIC上测试；以五个独立训练随机种子复现对比，同时考察多查询处理、计算开销、置信度变化、错误救回/引入分解，以及源域偏移和评分器控制。

Result: 1-shot时，WIPT相较冻结ProtoNet在CUB每次运行均提升，平均报告增幅为0.21个百分点；在EuroSAT提升2.07个百分点；在ISIC则下降0.22个百分点。5-shot时，ProtoNet总体仍最强，但WIPT在ISIC上稳定优于容量匹配的仅支持集Transformer（+0.99个百分点）。联合处理最多五个查询未带来可靠精度提升；在仅分类头的5-shot基准中，g=5相对g=1使解析注意力token对减少73%、峰值分配内存减少29%，但延迟并非单调下降。WIPT主要改变ProtoNet原本不确定的预测，救回与引入错误的分解解释了收益和损失。

Conclusion: WIPT提供一种兼容流式推理、无需目标时参数优化的测试时原型适配方式，在部分困难的低样本跨域决策中能带来改善；但收益幅度有限且具有数据集和设置依赖性，不能据此认为联合查询—支持适配普遍优于ProtoNet。

Abstract: Cross-domain few-shot learning requires adapting a classifier to a new visual domain from very few labelled examples without target-time parameter updates. We isolate one question: under a fixed global representation, what does joint query-support adaptation contribute to prototype construction? The Within-Instance Prototypical Transformer (WIPT) implements single-query test-time prototype adaptation by jointly transforming one unlabelled query and the labelled support embeddings, then forming query-specific class means. Using a shared frozen ViT-S/16 encoder, miniImageNet source training, and CUB, EuroSAT and ISIC targets, we replicate the key comparisons across five independent training seeds. In 1-shot evaluation, WIPT improves frozen ProtoNet in every run on CUB (+0.21 percentage points) and EuroSAT (+2.07), but decreases ISIC (-0.22). In 5-shot evaluation, ProtoNet remains strongest overall, while WIPT consistently improves a capacity-matched support-only Transformer on ISIC (+0.99). Joint processing of up to five queries yields no reliable accuracy gain; in a head-only 5-shot benchmark, g = 5 reduces analytical attention-token pairs by 73% and peak allocated memory by 29% relative to g = 1, although latency is non-monotonic. Across all target/shot conditions, WIPT changes uncertain ProtoNet decisions far more than confident ones, and rescue/break decomposition accounts for the observed gains and losses. Source-shift and scorer controls further show that the benefit is not universal. Overall, WIPT provides a streaming-compatible form of test-time prototype adaptation that can improve difficult low-shot cross-domain decisions without target-time optimization.

</details>


### [28] [Skip the Talk, Re-Focus on Vision: Latent Reasoning for Reasoning Segmentation in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30783)
*Tianhang Guo,Yulin He,Wei Chen,Wenjuan Zhou,Yuhang Li,Xinbiao Gan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LIRSeg用一组可学习的紧凑潜在 token 替代显式思维链进行推理分割，通过两阶段训练及三种信息优化机制提升分割效果并降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理分割方法依赖多模态大语言模型生成显式思维链。冗长文本 token 会干扰感知阶段的注意力，并拉大视觉 token 间的有效距离，导致推理效率和分割表现受影响。

Method: 提出 LIRSeg，以可学习潜在 token 完全替代显式 CoT。训练分为两阶段：先进行空间对齐，使潜在 token 锚定与目标相关的视觉证据；再通过 GRPO 和分割奖励进行优化。另提出极端优势采样、解耦探索—稳定性更新、潜在多样性增强，分别用于筛选信息量高的训练信号、学习互补表征及防止表征坍塌。

Result: 相较 VisionReasoner，LIRSeg 在 ReasonSeg、MUSE 和 MMR 上的 gIoU 分别绝对提升 4.9、7.1 和 4.7 个百分点；推理 token 数量约减少至原来的 1/16。

Conclusion: 结果表明，紧凑潜在推理表示能够兼顾推理分割精度与效率；信息导向的训练机制有助于提升潜在 token 的有效性。

Abstract: Reasoning segmentation aims to interpret implicit textual queries and enable fine-grained visual perception, which is critical for applications such as human-computer interaction and embodied agents. Existing methods typically generate explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) by multimodal large language models (MLLMs) before localizing the target. Although intuitive, such explicit verbal reasoning introduces substantial attention interference: redundant textual tokens disrupt attention during perception-token generation and also increase the effective distance between visual tokens. To address this issue, we propose LIRSeg, which fully replaces explicit CoT with a compact set of learnable latent tokens for reasoning segmentation. LIRSeg is trained in two stages: spatial alignment grounds the latent tokens in object-relevant visual evidence, and GRPO further optimizes them with segmentation rewards. To make these compact latent tokens more informative, we introduce three complementary mechanisms from an information perspective: extreme-advantage sampling for selecting informative training signals, decoupled exploration-stability updates for learning complementary representations, and latent diversity amplification for preventing representational collapse. Extensive experiments on benchmarks demonstrate that LIRSeg consistently improves both segmentation accuracy and reasoning efficiency. Compared with the VisionReasoner baseline, LIRSeg achieves absolute gIoU improvements of 4.9% on ReasonSeg, 7.1% on MUSE, and 4.7% on MMR, while achieving a approximately 16x reduction in reasoning tokens. Code is available in supplementary materials.

</details>


### [29] [Motion Style Slider: Endpoint-Supervised Continuous Style Control for Human Motion Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30795)
*Chen-Chieh Liao,Yichen Peng,Yiyi Cai,Yûi Ono,Hiroki Hanaoka,Erwin Wu,Hideki Koike,Shuichi Kurabayashi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Motion Style Slider，通过风格嵌入空间中的风格方向与标量强度条件，实现动作风格迁移的连续控制；利用端点监督和潜变量强度正则化，避免依赖中间强度动作真值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作扩散模型生成质量较好，风格迁移模型也能注入目标风格，但风格强度的细粒度连续控制仍不足。由于艺术家和导演对强度的判断具有主观性，实际更需要可靠、单调的相对控制轴，而非通用的绝对强度单位。

Method: 给定内容动作与风格动作，在学习到的动作风格嵌入空间中构造风格方向，并以标量强度控制扩散生成。训练目标结合扩散去噪损失与潜变量强度正则化，以端点监督促进平滑、单调的风格缩放，无需中间强度的真实动作。方法兼容预训练动作扩散骨干，并支持异构风格数据集；另加入少量真实采集的过度反应动作，用于评估对训练范围外强风格目标的泛化。

Result: 摘要说明实验评估了强度可控性、插值与外推行为、内容保持和动作真实感，并对风格方向构造及损失设计进行了消融。但未提供具体数值结果或明确的比较结论。

Conclusion: 该工作将动作风格迁移从目标风格注入推进到连续强度控制，并重点解决缺少中间强度标注、强度尺度主观的问题。其核心主张是学习一个平滑且单调的风格控制轴；具体性能优势仍需结合论文正文中的实验数据判断。

Abstract: Existing human motion diffusion methods provide strong motion generation quality, and recent style transfer models can inject target style cues, but fine-grained continuous control of style intensity remains underexplored. In production, style intensity is subjective across artists and directors, so the practical requirement is not a universal absolute unit, but a reliable monotonic control axis. We propose Motion Style Slider, a motion-to-motion style transfer framework for endpoint-supervised continuous control. Given a content motion and a style motion, we construct a style direction in a learned motion-style embedding space and condition diffusion generation with a scalar intensity. The training objective combines diffusion denoising with latent intensity regularization to encourage smooth and monotonic style scaling without requiring intermediate-intensity ground-truth motions. Our framework is compatible with pretrained motion diffusion backbones and supports heterogeneous style datasets, including the multi-actor style motion dataset. To test out-of-range usability, we additionally introduce a small real-capture over-reaction extension and evaluate large-intensity behavior against these unseen targets. Experiments measure controllability, interpolation/extrapolation behavior, content preservation, and motion realism, with ablations on direction construction and loss design.

</details>


### [30] [MDSkin-Net: Multi-Task Skin Lesion Analysis Driven by Pattern Analysis Priors and Spatial Alignment Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30855)
*Yijian Li,Saad Bedros,Paul Bigliardi,Mei Bigliardi Qi,Vassilios Morellas,Nikolaos Papanikolopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MDSkin-Net，将皮肤镜模式分析线索融入分割与分类联合网络，并以空间对齐正则化约束分类证据。模型仅用 ISIC 2017 训练集训练，在跨数据集零样本评估中表现稳健；域内分类有竞争力，但分割仍逊于专用单任务模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮损分割和分类对计算机辅助诊断很重要，但现有多任务方法通常缺乏临床知识引导；已有知识注入方法多借用宏观 ABCD 规则，而皮肤镜诊断更依赖针对微观皮肤镜特征的模式分析。因此，论文旨在把模式分析线索更直接地融入联合建模。

Method: 构建混合 CNN-Transformer 网络 MDSkin-Net。其模式分析引导注意力模块 PAGAM 包含三类先验：改进的高效通道注意力 iECA、多尺度空间注意力 MSSA、偏置不对称注意力 BAA，分别对应不同皮肤镜线索。另提出多尺度空间对齐正则化 MSAR，以分割真值掩膜提供层级软监督，使分类头关注病灶区域，并通过共享空间先验耦合分割与分类路径。训练仅使用 ISIC 2017 训练集，不使用外部皮肤镜数据。

Result: 集成模型在零样本 PH2 评估中取得 92.38% DSC 和 97.84% 黑色素瘤 AUC；在 ISIC 2018 Task 1 测试集取得 88.92% DSC。ISIC 2017 域内评估中，两项分类任务的平均 AUC 为 91.60%，分割 DSC 为 84.72%。分类表现与基线具有竞争力；域内分割则落后于单任务专用模型。

Conclusion: 将皮肤镜模式分析先验与分割掩膜引导的空间对齐结合，可形成具有跨队列泛化能力的联合表示，尤其在不同规模数据集间表现稳定。结果显示该方法提升了跨数据集应用的可信度，但并未在域内分割上超越专用分割模型。

Abstract: Reliable skin lesion segmentation and classification are central to dermoscopic computer-aided diagnosis. Existing multi-task frameworks couple the two tasks architecturally without clinical knowledge, while knowledge-injecting approaches rely on the macroscopic ABCD rule, which was not designed for dermoscopy. Dermoscopic diagnosis is grounded in Pattern Analysis, a microscopic framework structured around dermoscopic features. We propose MDSkin-Net, which incorporates cue-level Pattern Analysis priors into a hybrid CNN-Transformer architecture. At its core is a Pattern Analysis-Guided Attention Module (PAGAM) comprising three priors motivated by distinct dermoscopic cues: an improved Efficient Channel Attention (iECA), a Multi-Scale Spatial Attention (MSSA), and a Biased Asymmetry Attention (BAA). We further introduce a multi-scale spatial alignment regularization (MSAR) that uses the segmentation ground-truth mask as hierarchical soft supervision, confining the classification head to lesion-localized evidence and coupling both task pathways through a shared spatial prior. Trained exclusively on the ISIC 2017 training split without external dermoscopy data, the MDSkin-Net ensemble transfers robustly under zero-shot evaluation, reaching a Dice Similarity Coefficient (DSC) of 92.38% and a melanoma AUC of 97.84%on PH2, and a DSC of 88.92% on the ISIC 2018 Task 1 test set. On the in-domain ISIC 2017 benchmark, the ensemble attains a mean Area Under the Curve (AUC) of 91.60% across the two classification tasks (melanoma and seborrheic keratosis vs. rest), and a DSC of 84.72% for segmentation. Classification remains competitive with baselines; in-domain segmentation trails single-task specialists, yet the proposed priors and alignment regularization yield representations that generalize consistently across cohorts of different scales.

</details>


### [31] [Reliability-Regulated Trajectory Optimization for Progressive COLMAP-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30865)
*Zijian Wu,Jinliang Wang,Zidian Lin,Ying Song,Ziqian Lu,Hanjie Ma,Zhen Ye,Mingfeng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向无 COLMAP 的渐进式 3D Gaussian Splatting 的可靠性调节轨迹优化框架，通过双向循环一致性信号同时改善后续帧初始化与历史轨迹校正，提升相机轨迹精度和新视角渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐进式相机位姿跟踪容易累积误差：早期帧间跟踪偏差会影响后续位姿初始化，并被固化进场景表示。现有无位姿基线缺乏对误差可靠性的统一处理；依赖外部神经先验或零散启发式修补也未能系统解决漂移。

Method: 构建自监督的双向循环一致性机制，产生在线可靠性信号，并将其用于两个时间尺度：一是前向运动传播，按可靠性自适应门控刚体运动的一阶运动学热启动，为后续帧间配准提供方向性搜索先验，并拦截不可靠转移；二是回溯轨迹校正，在滑动窗口内动态调整相邻相机位姿的相对位姿一致性约束权重。两者由同一可靠性调节器统一控制，不需要外部先验或离线预处理。

Result: 在 Tanks and Temples 与 CO3D-V2 基准上的实验表明，该方法提升了相机轨迹精度和新视角渲染质量，并优于现有无位姿基线。摘要未提供具体指标或数值。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是把在线可靠性评估同时用于未来位姿初始化和历史轨迹整合，从而针对误差传播与累积漂移进行统一优化；实验结果支持其在无 COLMAP 渐进式 3DGS 场景中的有效性。

Abstract: COLMAP-free 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) bypasses computationally expensive structure-from-motion (SfM) pipelines, yet progressive camera pose tracking remains fundamentally vulnerable to error compounding---early pairwise tracking inaccuracies both corrupt subsequent frame initializations and remain permanently frozen in the scene representation. Rather than relying on heavyweight external neural priors or treating progressive tracking through isolated heuristic fixes, we propose a unified reliability-regulated trajectory optimization framework for progressive COLMAP-free 3DGS. At its core, our framework establishes an intrinsic, self-supervised bidirectional cycle-consistency mechanism that systematically regulates progressive camera trajectory estimation across two complementary temporal horizons: (1) Forward Motion Propagation, where the online reliability signal adaptively gates first-order kinematic warm-starts of rigid motion into upcoming pairwise registrations, supplying informed directional search priors while safely intercepting untrusted transitions; and (2) Retrospective Trajectory Correction, where the same reliability signal dynamically weights relative-pose consistency constraints within a sliding window of neighboring camera poses. By governing both prospective state initialization and retrospective trajectory consolidation through a unified reliability regulator, our self-contained framework resolves progressive drift without external priors or offline preprocessing. Extensive evaluations on Tanks and Temples and CO3D-V2 benchmarks show that our method substantially improves camera trajectory accuracy and novel-view rendering quality, outperforming existing unposed baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/Zijian1026/RRTO-CF3DGS.

</details>


### [32] [UltraG-Bench: A Multi-task Benchmark for assessing Large Vision-Language Models on Pixel-level Evidence Grounding in Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30928)
*Quanhao Zhu,Bo Xu,Rui Lin,Chenyuan Wang,Yu Shao,Boling Zhu,Jiuyan Sun,Liang Zhao,Hongfei Lin,Feng Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 UltraG-Bench，用于评估视觉语言模型在超声图像上的像素级证据定位能力，并提出 UltraG-Agent 结合语义推理与专用分割模型，以改善语义预测和视觉定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型虽已具备一定的超声图像理解能力，却难以为语义预测提供对齐的像素级视觉证据；其在超声场景中的细粒度定位能力也缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建 UltraG-Bench：基于 40 个公开超声分割数据集，覆盖 13 类解剖结构，设置逐步递进的三项任务——指令引导分割、证据支撑的视觉问答、证据支撑的报告生成，分别包含 331,125、666,779 和 138,832 条标注。对 14 个先进模型进行综合评测，并提出 UltraG-Agent，将视觉语言模型的语义推理能力与 UltraSAM3 的超声专用分割能力结合。

Result: 评测显示，模型的语义理解与细粒度像素定位之间存在显著差距。实验还表明，UltraG-Agent 能显著提升语义预测和像素级视觉证据定位表现。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了面向超声像素级证据定位的大规模、多任务基准，并通过结合通用语义推理与专用分割能力，展示了改善超声视觉 grounding 的可行路径。数据集与代码已公开。

Abstract: Ultrasound is one of the most widely used medical imaging modalities, and recent large vision-language models(VLMs) have shown increasing capabilities in ultrasound image understanding. However, these models fail to provide pixel-level visual evidence aligned with their semantic predictions, and their fine-grained grounding capability in ultrasound remains largely unclear. We introduce UltraG-Bench, a large-scale multi-task benchmark for evaluating pixel-level evidence grounding in ultrasound. UltraG-Bench is built by annotating 40 public ultrasound segmentation datasets spanning 13 anatomical categories, and comprises three progressive tasks: instruction-guided segmentation, evidence-grounded VQA, and evidence-grounded report generation, with 331125, 666779, and 138832 annotations, respectively. Comprehensive evaluation of 14 state-of-the-art models reveals a substantial gap between semantic understanding and fine-grained pixel-level localization. We further propose UltraG-Agent, which combines the semantic reasoning capabilities of a VLM with the ultrasound-specific segmentation capability of UltraSAM3. Experiments show that UltraG-Agent substantially improves both semantic prediction and pixel-level visual grounding. Our dataset and code are available at https://github.com/zhuqh19/UltraG-Bench.

</details>


### [33] [ManiVid: Unified and Explainable Forensic Analysis of Manipulated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30934)
*Hengrui Kang,Zhonghao Yan,Yuxuan Yang,Ruoyan Jing,Yuncheng Guo,Hao Chen,Kongming Liang,Zhanyu Ma,Conghui He,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 ManiVid 任务与数据集 ManiVid-38K，并设计 ManiVidLens 框架，统一处理视频伪造检测、伪造区域定位和异常解释。实验显示其定位与解释能力明显优于对比方法，检测性能接近专用分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成视频操纵风险上升，而局部篡改视频保留大部分原始内容，难以通过传统检测发现。现有研究一方面缺少面向局部操纵视频的高质量数据集和基准，另一方面多模态大语言模型难以有效利用底层取证线索，也难以精确定位像素级伪造区域。

Method: 提出 ManiVid 统一任务，涵盖伪造检测、伪造区域定位及异常解释；构建 ManiVid-38K，包含约1.9万组经人工核验的真实—伪造视频配对，并提供真实性标签、伪造掩码和异常解释。另从中抽取1,000组构建 ManiVidBench。提出 ManiVidLens：通过 Forensic Evidence Router 提供共享低层取证证据；通过 Prompt Distill Module 将定位状态转为语义与几何提示，并蒸馏空间先验以支持掩码解码和全视频传播。

Result: 相较最强对比方法，ManiVidLens 的伪造区域定位提升：mIoU 相对提高21.1%，J&F 提高21.3%；异常解释提升：ROUGE-L 提高131.3%，CSS 提高9.9%。伪造检测达到0.914准确率和0.913 F1，与专用分类器表现相当。

Conclusion: 该工作将数据、基准与统一分析框架结合，推动视频取证从真假二分类扩展到定位和解释。结果表明，向多模态推理引入低层取证证据并利用空间先验，有助于提升局部篡改分析能力；其检测能力则保持竞争力。

Abstract: Rapid advances in AI-generated video (AIGV) have increased the risks posed by deceptive video manipulation. Unlike fully synthetic videos, manipulated videos retain most source content and alter only localized regions, making forensic analysis particularly challenging. Existing video forgery research faces two limitations in both data and methodology: (1) High-quality datasets and benchmarks tailored for manipulated videos remain scarce. (2) Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend forgery analysis beyond binary classification but struggle to use low-level forensic cues and provide precise pixel-level grounding. Specifically, we introduce ManiVid, a unified forensic analysis task covering forgery detection, artifact grounding, and anomaly explanation for manipulated videos. We construct ManiVid-38K, the first dataset to combine paired, open-vocabulary localized manipulations of general videos with authenticity labels, forgery masks, and anomaly explanations. It comprises about 19K manually verified real-fake video pairs, mostly at 1080P resolution, generated under 2 paradigms with 15 powerful generation models. We sample 1K pairs for ManiVidBench, balanced across six manipulation types and generation models for fair evaluation. We further propose ManiVidLens, a unified framework for explainable video forgery analysis. Its Forensic Evidence Router supplies shared low-level forensic evidence for multimodal reasoning and video segmentation. Its Prompt Distill Module converts grounding states into semantic and geometric prompts and distills spatial priors for mask decoding and full-video propagation. ManiVidLens achieves relative gains over the strongest comparison methods in artifact grounding (+21.1% mIoU; +21.3% J&F) and anomaly explanation (+131.3% ROUGE-L; +9.9% CSS). Its forgery detection remains comparable to dedicated classifiers (0.914 Acc; 0.913 F1).

</details>


### [34] [Spackle: Completing Large View Single Image NVS with Adaptive Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30941)
*Xuanzhi Liu,Yuhe Zhou,Xinyi Wu,Zhenyao Wu,Jinghao Chen,Ruize Han,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spackle通过残差式3D高斯补充，缓解单图新视角合成中大视角偏移导致的容量竞争，在保持推理效率的同时提升遮挡区域重建和整体画面质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图新视角合成需要从单张输入图像生成未观测视角，核心挑战是补全被遮挡区域并保持较高推理效率。现有结合前馈3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）与扩散模型的混合方法，在目标视角偏离较大时会受固定高斯数量限制：模型将资源从原本可见区域转移到新显露区域，造成输入场景原有内容的保真度下降。

Method: 提出轻量级残差学习框架Spackle，分三步进行：先根据输入视图预测基础3DGS属性；再自动检测重建效果较差的区域；最后仅针对这些区域训练残差3DGS。推理时将基础高斯与残差高斯合并，用于新视角合成。

Result: 论文报告，Spackle在大视角偏移场景中取得当前最优性能，同时旨在维持较高推理效率。摘要未提供具体数据集、指标或定量结果。

Conclusion: 通过让额外的高斯表示专门补偿重建薄弱区域，Spackle缓解了固定表示容量引发的资源竞争，有望兼顾遮挡区域补全与原场景保真度，适用于大视角偏移的新视角合成。

Abstract: Single-image novel view synthesis (NVS) enables photorealistic rendering of un- observed viewpoints from a single input.
