RupeeBias consists of 39,150 prompts spanning four use cases: salary estimation, salary increment estimation, counter-offer recommendation, and service pricing recommendation. The benchmark follows a single-attribute counterfactual design, holding the description of the user's qualifications, experience, or service offering fixed while varying one demographic identifier at a time. RupeeBias covers 87 India-specific demographic identifiers across six axes: caste, religion, regional identity, gender, disability, and urban-rural location, with all prompts constructed in both English and Hinglish. We evaluate nine LLMs on RupeeBias and find systematic demographic disparities across all six axes. For otherwise identical prompts that differ only in demographic identifier, LLM-generated economic outputs differ by 20.2% on average. We publicly release RupeeBias to support future research on demographic bias in LLM-generated economic guidance across India-specific demographic and economic contexts.

</details>


### [138] [PIA: A Personal Intelligence Agent Turning Health Conversations into Records and Records into Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31255)
*Jeonghun Yoon,Dongchan Kim,Hongyeon Yu,Young-Bum Kim,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 PIA，一种与消费级健康代理协同运行的个人智能代理，将对话转化为结构化临床记录，并基于记录形成用户健康理解；其记忆能力从简单回忆扩展到健康快照和带因果关系的时间轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用代理通常以摘要片段和向量相似度进行记忆检索，但这不足以支持健康问题：剂量、时间范围和长期趋势需要结构化表示与时间推理，不能仅依赖文本相似度或模型自行解释。

Method: PIA接收健康代理发出的自然语言请求，自主决定是否及如何读写记忆。其记忆流程包含信息抽取、记忆存储、检索和理解四个控制环节；每个环节采用领域无关机制，并可接入健康领域模块，包括数据模式、医学别名词典、知识图谱和时间规则。系统把对话整理为类型化临床记录，再综合形成用户健康画像与轨迹。

Result: 论文展示了同一查询在注入不同深度记忆时，回答可从一维信息回忆，提升至二维健康快照，再到包含因果关系的三维时间轨迹。运行经验表明：用户自报健康数据并非随机缺失；问题措辞会影响综合理解质量；候选因果联系中近三分之一属于结构性噪声，可由规则过滤。摘要未提供具体量化基准或对照实验结果。

Conclusion: PIA强调，健康代理需要从非结构化摘要检索转向类型化、时间化并经过领域规则约束的个人记忆。该设计有望支持更丰富的健康理解，但其可靠性仍受到自报数据缺失、提问方式以及因果关系噪声的影响。

Abstract: General-purpose agent memory summarizes conversations: it extracts salient snippets, embeds them, and retrieves the top-k into the prompt. A health agent cannot run on summaries: a dose becomes a sentence, "since last week" is resolved at the model's discretion, and a three-month glucose trend cannot be answered by text similarity. We present PIA, a personal intelligence agent deployed alongside a consumer health agent. PIA receives the agent's natural-language requests, decides for itself whether and how to write or read, and turns conversations into typed clinical records and records into a synthesized understanding of the user. Its memory harness consists of four controls -- extraction, memory, retrieval, and understanding -- each a domain-agnostic mechanism with a pluggable health module: schema, medical alias dictionary, knowledge graph, and temporal rules. We show how the same query receives a different answer as the memory injected into the response context deepens from one-dimensional recall, to a two-dimensional health snapshot, to a three-dimensional trajectory with causality, and report lessons from operation: self-reported health data are missing not at random, question phrasing governs the quality of synthesized understanding, and nearly a third of candidate causal links are structural noise that rules alone remove.

</details>


### [139] [MoSAR: Mixture of Semantic Attention Regimes for Learning Adaptive and Approximable Attention Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31261)
*Michele Paolicelli,Alessandro Petruzzelli,Alessandro Franceso Maria Martina,Cataldo Musto,Giovanni Semeraro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 MoSAR：让模型从数据中学习查询—键交互的自适应距离衰减几何，在不损害语言建模质量的情况下减少注意力作用范围，并改善长上下文外推表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集自注意力的二次复杂度是长上下文建模的重要瓶颈。许多高效方法预先规定稀疏或局部注意力模式，但自然语言依赖随输入变化，固定模式难以同时捕捉局部相关性与必要的远距离交互。

Method: 提出 Mixture of Semantic Attention Regimes（MoSAR）。在位置编码之后使用输入条件化的 query/key 路由器，为短程、中程和全局注意力机制分配混合权重，从而形成连续、随距离变化的注意力场，而非固定稀疏结构。该几何可在训练中学习，并可在推理时通过 top-1 路由离散化。

Result: 在参数量匹配的 500M 模型预训练实验中，MoSAR 学得的注意力作用范围更短，同时不损害语言模型质量；相较于 dense RoPE，在训练上下文长度下困惑度更低。长度外推实验中，MoSAR 在所有评估变体（包括 ALiBi 等强基线）中取得最佳困惑度。确定性 top-1 离散化后，学得的几何仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于数据学习注意力交互几何有望兼顾效率与建模效果；MoSAR 的自适应机制不仅能改善长度外推，也具备通过低成本路由近似部署的潜力。摘要未提供具体计算量、推理速度或统计显著性数据，因此效率收益的实际幅度仍需结合正文评估。

Abstract: The quadratic complexity of dense self-attention remains a central bottleneck for long-context language modeling. Many efficient alternatives address this cost by deciding in advance where attention should be sparse or local. We argue that attention approximation should instead be approached as a geometric problem, with the relevant interaction geometry learned from data: natural-language dependencies are input-dependent and difficult to prescribe in advance, so the model should learn where positional relevance can decay and where broader interactions must be preserved. We introduce Mixture of Semantic Attention Regimes (MoSAR), which learns such an adaptive, controlled-decay geometry over query--key interactions. Input-conditioned query and key routers, applied after positional encoding, select mixtures over short, medium, and global regimes, inducing a continuous distance-dependent attention field rather than a fixed sparsity pattern. This geometry is learned during training and can subsequently be discretized through top-1 routing. In controlled pre-training experiments with matched 500M-parameter models, MoSAR learns a substantially lower-reach attention geometry without degrading language-modeling quality, improving perplexity over dense RoPE at the training context length. Under length extrapolation, MoSAR achieves the best perplexity among all evaluated variants, including strong baselines such as ALiBi. Moreover, the learned geometry remains stable under deterministic top-1 discretization, suggesting that it is not only adaptive, but also amenable to low-cost approximation at inference time.

</details>


### [140] [Identifying Scientists on X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31264)
*Philipp Meier,Katarina Boland,Laura Kallmeyer,Stefan Dietze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于 X/Twitter 用户简介与推文自动区分科学家和非科学家的方法；对比微调的 DeBERTa 集成模型 F1 最高达 0.96，并发布了两个标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学相关话语在网络上的重要性不断上升，传统知识秩序也在变化，因此需要自动识别不同用户群体，尤其是科学家，以支持对网络科学传播的分析。

Method: 利用用户简介和推文作为输入，在两个数据集上进行科学家/非科学家二分类。比较基于语言学特征的随机森林与对比微调的 DeBERTa 模型，并采用集成设置。

Result: 随机森林结合语言学特征的 F1 最高为 0.88；对比微调的 DeBERTa 集成模型 F1 最高为 0.96。论文还提供两个包含用户类别标签、推文及简介的数据集。

Conclusion: 用户简介和推文可用于有效识别 X/Twitter 上的科学家与非科学家，基于 DeBERTa 的方案表现最佳；公开标注数据集有望促进后续研究。

Abstract: With the growing importance of science-related discourse on the Web and the erosion of the classical knowledge order, it is important to identify different user groups, such as scientists, automatically. This work proposes an approach for identifying scientists and non- scientists on X/Twitter based on their user biographies and tweets. We show that we are able to classify accounts as scientists and non- scientists on two different datasets, reaching an F1 score of up to 0.88 using Random Forests with linguistic features and up to 0.96 using a contrastively fine-tuned DeBERTa model in an ensemble setup. Furthermore, we provide two datasets with X users labeled as scientists or non scientists and their respective tweets and user biographies.

</details>


### [141] [Stale-Document Poisoning: When Outdated Retrieval Overrides Correct Model Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31342)
*Md Shamim Ahmed,Lukas Galke Poech,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究RAG中的“过时证据污染”：即使模型原本能答对，检索到已失效的旧资料也会诱使其答错。实验显示，仅给出日期帮助有限，明确说明证据失效时间则能显著改善判断；准确的日期感知重排也可降低污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG常被视为缓解知识过时的方案，但外部证据本身也可能过时。论文关注现有系统是否能区分检索内容“说了什么”与“现在是否仍适用”，并量化陈旧证据对模型答案的因果影响。

Method: 构建包含317个经核验知识反转的基准，覆盖医学、法律、软件和平台政策，并以带日期的官方来源为依据。评测12个模型；比较旧证据与最新证据、不同指令条件及模型规模。另在50个反转案例中固定历史证据、仅改变评估日期，测试明确失效时间提示的作用，并通过因果干预分析内部决策机制；最后评估固定的近期性混合重排器。

Result: 近期医学知识反转比长期已确立的反转更难。过时检索在无明确服从指令时，令Llama和Qwen答案翻转的比例分别为30%和37%；要求模型遵循文档后升至66%和75%。四个开源模型、四个领域中的污染率为17%–91%，而匹配的最新证据在97%–100%的试验中被采纳。仅提供日期带来的适应有限；明确指出旧证据何时不再适用后，较大模型几乎总能切换到正确答案。准确日期下，近期性混合重排器使污染率下降4.6–10.0个百分点，效果依赖可靠的时间元数据。

Conclusion: RAG的可靠性不仅取决于检索相关性，还取决于证据的时间适用性。系统需要选择性信任检索结果：识别证据内容，并判断其是否仍有效。明确的失效时间信息有助于模型完成这一判断；日期感知重排可提供补充，但无法脱离可靠时间元数据。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is often used to address outdated knowledge by providing external evidence. But retrieval helps only when that evidence is still valid. We identify a temporal alignment failure, stale-document poisoning, in which outdated evidence makes a model wrong despite answering correctly without retrieval. We construct a benchmark of 317 verified knowledge reversals across medicine, law, software, and platform policy, grounded in dated official sources. Across 12 models, recent medical reversals are harder than long-established ones. More importantly, outdated retrieval flips 30% of Llama and 37% of Qwen answers even without instructions to trust the document; explicit follow instructions raise these rates to 66% and 75%. Across four open models and four domains, poisoning ranges from 17-91%, while matched up-to-date evidence is followed in 97-100% of trials. To isolate temporal applicability, we keep the historical evidence unchanged across 50 reversals and vary only the evaluation date. A clear pattern emerges: dates alone produce only modest adaptation, but when models are explicitly told when the old evidence stops applying, the larger models switch to the appropriate answer almost perfectly. Causal interventions confirm that this validity information directly shapes the final decision. The same internal components also support broader comparison tasks, suggesting that temporal applicability can recruit a general reasoning mechanism used for other comparisons. Finally, a fixed recency-aware hybrid re-ranker reduces poisoning by 4.6-10.0 points when dates are accurate, with gains that depend on reliable temporal metadata. Reliable RAG therefore requires selective trust: models must determine not only what retrieved evidence says, but whether it still applies.

</details>


### [142] [Highlight-Then-Summarize: Learning to Compress Evidence for Long-Context Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31382)
*Zhaoyuan Xia,Qinghongbing Xie,Yung Xiang Hue,Jianguang Jiang,Gaofeng Lu,Zhenyu Jiao,Xing Yuan,Dai Dai,Tong Mo,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 H2S（先高亮证据、再摘要并推理）范式，并通过数据集与过程级强化学习训练长上下文模型；其 14B 模型在所测开源模型中表现领先，同时降低输出预算需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文任务中的关键信息通常稀疏、分散，且被大量无关或重复内容淹没。直接在长输入上推理容易造成信息利用低效，也可能需要较大的生成预算。

Method: 提出 Highlight-Then-Summarize（H2S）：先从原文中选取有来源依据且与问题相关的证据，再将其整合为紧凑、问题条件化的摘要，最后生成答案。构建含 6,647 个样本、覆盖 11 类基准、平均上下文长度 43.9K tokens 的 H2S-Dataset；提出 H2S-RL，在答案正确性之外，对证据选择和摘要构建提供过程级奖励。

Result: 在七项长上下文任务组成的 H2S-Bench 上，在统一 128K 输入、4K 输出预算下，H2S-14B 平均得分为 32.60，比 Qwen3.8-27B 高 10.17 分，并在参测开源模型中总体排名第一。其 Evidence-Summary Quality 得分最高；仅使用 4K 输出预算时，仍保留其 16K 预算表现的 97.1%。

Conclusion: 结果支持显式筛选并整合证据有助于提升长上下文推理，同时使生成更紧凑。该方法将证据处理作为可训练的中间过程，而非仅依赖模型直接从长上下文生成答案。

Abstract: Long-context understanding requires large language models (LLMs) to reason over lengthy documents, conversations, and code, yet task-relevant evidence is often sparse and scattered amid substantial irrelevant and redundant content. We propose Highlight-Then-Summarize (H2S), a compress-then-reason paradigm that first identifies source-grounded, question-relevant evidence and then integrates it into a compact, question-conditioned summary before producing the final answer. To train this behavior, we construct H2S-Dataset, comprising 6,647 examples from 11 benchmark families with an average context length of 43.9K tokens, and introduce H2S-RL, which provides process-level rewards for evidence selection and summary construction in addition to final-answer correctness. We evaluate on H2S-Bench, a seven-task long-context suite. Under a shared 128K input and 4K output budget, H2S-14B achieves an average score of 32.60, outperforming Qwen3.8-27B by 10.17 points and obtaining the strongest overall result among the evaluated open-source models. H2S-14B also achieves the highest Evidence-Summary Quality score and retains 97.1% of its 16K-budget performance with only a 4K output budget. These results show that explicitly selecting and integrating evidence improves long-context reasoning while enabling more compact generation.

</details>


### [143] [Sorry Robot, Happy Human: Vision-Language Models Read Only One of Two Legible Typographic Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31403)
*Mert İncidelen,Yamen Kashkash,Asya Berker,Murat Aydoğan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究构建 DecoyBench，发现受测视觉语言模型在多层文字图像中存在稳定的读取偏差：高分辨率偏向轮廓文字，低分辨率则更易读取阴影文字。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型在 OCR 上表现出色，但可能容易受字体攻击影响，并难以可靠处理由不同空间频率构成的多层文字图像。

Method: 采用 Decoy Font 方法构建包含 300 张图像的 DecoyBench，每张图像将带清晰轮廓的文字叠加在带柔和阴影的另一段文字上。评估来自三个模型系列的六个闭源模型，比较普通与引导式提示，以及 512×512 和 64×64 两种分辨率；另以人工阅读实验验证图像可读性。

Result: 人工参与者能以较高准确率读取两层文字。高分辨率下，多数模型变体在两种提示条件下都能近乎人类水平读取轮廓文字，却几乎无法完整提取阴影文字。低分辨率下，模型和人类都难以读取轮廓文字，而阴影文字可被高准确率提取。

Conclusion: 受测视觉语言模型在多空间频率层叠的排版结构中表现出一致的行为局限；读取偏差随图像分辨率变化，单纯依赖模型整体 OCR 能力不足以保证多层文字均被识别。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs), despite their success in optical character recognition (OCR) tasks, are vulnerable to typographic attacks and have a fragile structure for images with multiple text layers. In this study, the DecoyBench dataset was created using the Decoy Font method. The dataset consists of 300 images, each containing text with sharp contour lines superimposed on another text with soft shading. Six recent closed-source models from three different model families were evaluated using this dataset under two different prompting conditions (naive and guided) and at two different resolutions ($512\times512$ and $64\times64$). A validation study showed that human participants could read both text layers with high accuracy. In contrast, the models, with most variants and both prompting methods, read the contour text with near-human accuracy at high resolution, but almost never fully extracted the shading text. At low resolution, the contour text could not be read by either the models or humans, while the shading text could be extracted with high accuracy. The findings indicate that the evaluated VLMs exhibit a consistent behavioral limitation when processing typographic structures containing multiple spatial frequency layers.

</details>


### [144] [ViSTA: A Simple Bridge Extends Visual Alignment to Clinical Time-Series Understanding in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31448)
*Junyi Gao,Yu Shi,Pingzhao Hu,Ewen M Harrison*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ViSTA：一种参数高效适配器，将不规则临床数值时间序列融入预训练视觉语言模型的图表表示，用于风险预测和时间序列问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测模型擅长从结构化测量值估计风险，而大型语言模型擅长理解医疗文本；但语言能力并不意味着模型能够准确处理高维、非规则的临床时间序列。研究希望连接患者风险评估与针对病情变化的灵活提问。

Method: ViSTA 将不规则数值测量编码进图表表示，并学习对视觉 token 的修正，同时冻结所有预训练参数。实验在 MIMIC-IV 上进行，涵盖急性肾损伤和死亡率预测，以及时间序列问答，并与多种适配方法及不同规模模型比较。

Result: 在 2–9B 参数模型中，ViSTA 对急性肾损伤和死亡率预测的四项指标均取得所比较适配方法中的最高平均分。仅训练 0.516M 个参数时，2B 模型的急性肾损伤 AUROC 为 0.7376，接近摘要所述 GPT-5.6 Sol 文本输入、高推理设置下的 0.7380。时间问答方面，4B 模型准确率为 69.27%；相比基于图表或数值文本的低秩适配，训练参数减少超过 90%，但准确率低 2.82–4.88 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，ViSTA 能以较少可训练参数将预训练语言模型扩展到临床数值预测和时间性问题回答；不过，时间问答相较低秩适配仍存在一定准确率差距。

Abstract: Clinical prediction models estimate risk from patient measurements, while large language models support medical text understanding and question answering. Yet their language capabilities do not ensure accurate prediction from structured, high-dimensional clinical time series. Improving this ability would connect risk estimation with flexible questions about a patient's evolving condition. We introduce ViSTA, a compact adapter that incorporates irregular numerical measurements into a pretrained vision-language model's chart representations. It learns corrections to visual tokens while leaving all pretrained parameters unchanged. On MIMIC-IV, ViSTA has the highest mean scores among the compared adaptations on all four metrics for acute kidney injury and mortality prediction across models with 2-9 billion parameters. With 0.516 million trainable parameters, the 2-billion-parameter model reaches an area under the ROC curve of 0.7376 for acute kidney injury, compared with GPT-5.6 Sol's 0.7380 with text input and high reasoning effort. Training for temporal question answering yields 69.27% accuracy at 4 billion parameters with over 90% fewer trainable parameters than low-rank adaptation using charts or numerical text, at a 2.82-4.88 percentage-point accuracy gap. ViSTA extends pretrained language models to numerical prediction and temporal questions.

</details>


### [145] [Evaluating Cultural Awareness of LLMs for Haitian Creole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31506)
*Christelle Clervilsson,Yanzhu Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文首次系统评估大语言模型对海地克里奥尔语文化的认知，发现其文化表现弱于法语，且受法语干扰并存在刻板叙事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言的大语言模型不仅任务性能较弱，也可能无法准确反映少数语言社群的文化规范与价值观。海地克里奥尔语使用者众多，但数字资源严重不足，相关文化认知此前缺乏系统评估。

Method: 由母语者整理具有文化显著性的提示，采用文本填空任务，从特异性、偏见、多样性和变异性四个维度评估模型；另通过故事生成分析人物描写，并与资源较丰富的法语表现进行比较。

Result: 海地克里奥尔语的文化认知与法语存在明显差距，且不同领域表现更不均衡，更易受到法语语言干扰。故事生成中，海地人物反复被描绘为经历困境并展现韧性，说明正面描述也可能承载刻板叙事。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了低资源语言模型在文化表达上的不足，并提供公开的代码、基准和评估框架，支持后续研究与改进。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit substantial performance disparities between high- and low-resource languages. Beyond lower task performance, they often fail to capture the cultural norms and values of underrepresented communities. In this work, we present the first systematic evaluation of cultural awareness in LLMs for Haitian Creole, a language spoken by millions but severely underrepresented in digital resources. We assess cultural awareness along four complementary dimensions---specificity, bias, diversity, and variation---using a benchmark of culturally salient prompts curated by native speakers in a text infilling setting. Our results reveal a clear gap between cultural awareness in Haitian Creole and higher-resource French, with Haitian performance being more uneven across domains and more affected by French linguistic interference. Story generation further reveals recurring portrayals of Haitian characters through hardship and resilience, showing that even positive characterizations can encode stereotypical narratives. Our code, benchmark, and evaluation framework are publicly available.

</details>


### [146] [Muslim: A Deployed Arabic Voice AI Platform for Grounded Islamic Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31511)
*Yahya Mohamed Elnawasany*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文介绍了 Muslim，一个面向真实用户的阿拉伯语语音 AI 产品，重点报告其模型、生产运营机制、可观测性设计及实测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出，许多研究原型缺少从模型能力走向真实服务所需的产品化要素。本文旨在展示如何将基于来源的伊斯兰知识问答语音系统部署为可运营、可监控且具备一定滥用防护能力的生产平台，并总结其工程权衡与局限。

Method: 系统采用实时语音链路（NeMo 阿拉伯语 ASR、兼容 OpenAI API 的 LLM 端点、自托管 TTS），并通过六个 MCP 服务器进行确定性、多来源检索路由。作者发布了工具路由模型 Muslim-6B-PRO（5.94B 参数）及 MSA TTS 模型 Fasih-TTS-V1；同时实现账户与用量管理（免费回合额度、容量感知拒绝、在必要环节才进行邮箱验证）以及由存活监测、错误报告和产品分析构成的三层可观测性体系。

Result: 摘要报告：Fasih-TTS-V1 在社区投票的阿拉伯语 TTS Arena 的现代标准阿拉伯语类别中，总体排名 17 个系统中的第 5、开源权重系统中的第 2；124 个案例上的诵读验证准确率为 98.4%；端到端语音延迟为 0.9–1.7 秒。可观测性设计针对 GPU 代理主机失联而 Web 层仍正常服务这一特定故障模式。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是展示一套从模型与检索到账户管理、监控和性能评估的生产级阿拉伯语语音 AI 实践。摘要呈现了有竞争力的 TTS 排名及具体延迟、准确率数据，但未提供对照实验、评测细节或更广泛用户效果，因此相关结果的可比性与泛化能力仍需结合正文判断。

Abstract: We present Muslim, a production Arabic voice AI platform serving grounded, sourced Islamic knowledge to real users. Beyond a real-time voice pipeline (NeMo Arabic ASR, an OpenAI-compatible LLM endpoint, self-hosted TTS) and a deterministic multi-source retrieval layer routed across six Model Context Protocol servers, we report three things a research prototype typically lacks. First, a released family of fine-tuned Arabic Islamic model artifacts: an efficient tool-routing LLM (Muslim-6B-PRO, 5.94B parameters) and a Modern Standard Arabic TTS model (Fasih-TTS-V1) that ranks 5th of 17 overall and 2nd of 11 open-weight systems on the community-voted Arabic TTS Arena for MSA. Second, an account and metering layer - a free per-account turn allowance, capacity-aware refusal, and email verification deferred to the point it actually matters - that turns an open demo into an operable, abuse-resistant product. Third, a three-layer observability stack (liveness, error reporting, product analytics) built specifically around the system's characteristic failure mode: a GPU-bound agent host going silent while the web tier keeps serving normally. We report real, measured latency and accuracy figures (98.4% recitation-validation accuracy on 124 cases; end-to-end voice latency of 0.9-1.7s) and discuss the concrete engineering trade-offs and limitations of running an Islamic-knowledge voice product in production.

</details>


### [147] [MexHat: A Dataset for Hate Speech Detection in Mexican Spanish Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31553)
*Itzel Tlelo-Coyotecatl,Hugo Jair Escalante*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 MexHat，一个面向墨西哥西班牙语语境的视频数据集，用于评测多模态仇恨言论检测，包含约 1,000 个视频片段，并提供粗粒度与细粒度分类标注及基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论检测需要结合上下文线索理解表达意图，但非英语资源稀缺，限制了模型识别多模态内容中细微、依赖语境且具有文化特性的表达。

Method: 构建 MexHat 视频数据集，约含 1,000 个片段。标注两项任务：三分类（无负面内容、冒犯性内容、仇恨言论）和细粒度分类（仇恨言论的三个子类别）；并报告数据集统计与基线结果。

Result: 摘要指出，数据集统计和基线实验体现了该任务的内在挑战；未提供具体性能数字或模型间比较。

Conclusion: MexHat 为墨西哥西班牙语语境下的多模态仇恨言论检测提供了专门评测资源，有助于研究上下文与文化线索，但摘要未说明数据集规模之外的细节、基线表现或具体挑战来源。

Abstract: Ensuring online safety through content monitoring had raised Hate Speech Detection as a crucial task to be addressed. By essence the task demands the capture of contextual cues, which are essential for a precise understanding of the content's intent. Although automated detection approaches for the task have advanced significantly, the scarcity of non-English resources persists, limiting the ability of models to adapt to the subtle, context-dependent, and culturally related nature of multimodal content. In this paper, we introduce MexHat, a video dataset designed to capture the linguistic and cultural cues for the hate-speech detection task in a Mexican Spanish context. Our dataset comprises around 1k video clips annotated across two tasks: a three-way class evaluation (no negative content, offensive content and hate-speech content), and a fine-grained class evaluation including three hate-speech sub-categories. The dataset statistics and the baseline results highlight the inherent challenges associated with the task. Disclaimer: This paper contains sensitive content that may be disturbing to some readers.

</details>


### [148] [Strategically Diverse Sampling for Self-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31571)
*Alexander Gurung,Esmeralda S. Whitammer,Mirella Lapata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究如何构造更有效的自训练数据，主张应优先追求解题策略的实质多样性，而非只依赖独立采样和答案正确性筛选。提出 GROOT 与 Verbalized Sampling 两种策略多样化采样方式；实验显示其有助于困难任务表现，并可作为强化学习和测试时扩展的良好初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见的强化学习、测试时扩展及自训练方法依赖多次采样，但 IID 采样往往重复模型偏好的解题套路。仅按正确性筛选会进一步偏向已有策略，难以提供新的训练信号。论文因此考察：自训练数据的价值是否更多取决于方法或思路的多样性，而不仅是正确性或教师模型规模。

Method: 提出两种构造策略多样数据的方法：GROOT 先建立解题方法的层级树，再从中采样不同路径；Verbalized Sampling（VS）则生成不具层级结构的一组不同方法。研究在竞赛编程和下一章节预测任务上比较策略多样化数据与 IID 数据训练的效果，并考察其作为强化学习和测试时扩展初始模型的作用。

Result: 策略多样化数据训练的模型在困难任务上优于 IID 数据训练的对应模型，并能为强化学习与测试时扩展提供较强初始化。尤其值得注意的是，Qwen3-4B 使用策略多样但答案错误的轨迹进行自训练，表现超过了使用 235B 教师模型进行 IID 蒸馏的结果。

Conclusion: 研究结果提示，自训练数据的效用不能仅由答案正确率或教师规模衡量；不同解题策略所带来的训练信号可能更重要。该结论挑战了以正确性筛选和大模型蒸馏为主的常见数据构造假设，但其适用范围仍需在更多任务、模型及策略多样性度量下验证。

Abstract: Many LLM training and inference methods, including RL and test-time scaling, depend on repeated sampling, but benefit only when the responses meaningfully differ. Self-training faces the same challenge: training data is typically constructed by sampling IID responses and filtering primarily for correctness, thereby overrepresenting strategies a model already favours. We investigate strategic diversity, or substantive variation among approaches to a problem, as an alternative principle for constructing self-training data. We generate strategically diverse data with two sampling methods: GROOT, a new method which constructs a hierarchical tree of approaches and samples distinct paths, and Verbalized Sampling (VS), adapted to produce an unstructured set of approaches. Across competitive programming and Next-Chapter Prediction domains, models trained on strategically sampled data outperform IID-trained counterparts on difficult tasks and provide strong initializations for RL and test-time scaling. Most strikingly, self-training on strategically diverse but incorrect traces from Qwen3-4B outperforms IID distillation from a 235B teacher. These results challenge prevailing assumptions about what makes useful self-training data and show that diversity of approaches can matter more than correctness or teacher scale.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Bringing AI to Autonomous Systems -- From Cognition to Collective Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30291)
*Joseph Sifakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出面向自主系统的设计与评估框架，强调融合连接主义与符号主义人工智能，并将可信性扩展到认知层面；同时指出自主多智能体系统的愿景与现有技术之间仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统被视为人工智能发展的重要阶段，但其实现需要解决感知数据与结构化知识衔接、基于目标的决策与规划、多智能体协调等问题。传统系统的可信性评估主要关注行为属性，难以覆盖智能体如何运用知识进行决策这一认知维度，因此需要统一的设计、工程与评估框架。

Method: 提出以通用智能体架构为基础的自主系统设计与评估框架。该架构将智能体行为视为围绕长期记忆组织的认知功能组合，长期记忆承载并更新智能体知识。文章讨论架构关键能力的实现挑战、多智能体协同机制，以及智能体可信性评估方法的潜在发展方向。

Result: 文章梳理了自主智能体的核心技术问题：将感知输入映射为记忆中的结构化数据；围绕目标进行决策和规划；协调多个智能体以整合个体与集体智能。并指出可信性不仅取决于外显行为，也取决于智能体知识的有效性及其在决策中的运用方式。

Conclusion: 自主系统的发展需要连接主义人工智能、符号人工智能与系统工程的结合，并建立同时考察行为和认知属性的可信性评估方法。作者最终认为，雄心勃勃的自主多智能体系统愿景与当前技术水平之间仍存在明显差距。

Abstract: The purpose of this article is to highlight the central role of autonomous systems as the ultimate stage in the development of AI, to explain the underlying technical challenges that require a combination of connectionist AI and symbolic AI, and to integrate AI and systems engineering. We present a comprehensive framework for the design and evaluation of autonomous systems, based on a generic agent architecture that characterizes their behavior as the composition of cognitive functions organized around a long-term memory containing the agent's evolving knowledge. We address the challenges posed by the implementation of the fundamental features of the agent architecture, in particular the link between sensory data and structured data stored in memory, decision-making related to the achievement of the agent's goals and their planning, as well as the coordination of agents to combine individual and collective intelligence. We explain that agent trustworthiness, unlike that of traditional systems, is not limited to behavioral properties. It includes an essential dimension related to cognitive properties, the validity of which depends on how the agent uses its knowledge in decision-making. We present avenues for the development of methods for evaluating agent trustworthiness. We conclude with a critical assessment of the substantial gap between the aspirational vision of autonomous multi-agent systems and the current state of the art.

</details>


### [150] [ScopeBench: Do Agents Preserve Engagement Boundaries Under Goal Pressure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30325)
*Shane Caldwell,Max Harley,Ads Dawson,Michael Kouremetis,Vincent Abruzzo,Will Pearce*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 ScopeBench，用于评估安全测试智能体的任务能力与范围遵循能力；在8个模型上的结果显示，二者并不等同，越界行为检测也不能仅依赖任务是否成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体用于网页应用和网络安全测试时，越过客户授权范围的单次操作就可能造成严重问题。现有基准主要衡量攻击能力，随着能力基准逐渐饱和，部署瓶颈转向是否能遵守明确范围。

Method: 构建包含30个“目标仅能通过越界实现”的安全测试任务。每项任务设无范围与有范围两种条件，环境、验证器和目标相同，仅范围指令不同。无范围轨迹由确定性验证器评分；有范围轨迹先由验证器检查目标标志，再由智能体裁判判断未通过者是否曾发出越界调用。裁判使用100条由人工逐调用标注的轨迹校准，并对评估轨迹进行盲审。

Result: 在同一测试框架下评估8个模型，原始能力得分为12.2%—81.1%，范围遵循得分为34.4%—86.7%。裁判额外识别出331起机械验证未发现的越界行为。Opus-4-8的原始能力比sonnet-4-6高10个百分点，范围遵循率高35.6个百分点。盲审的36起违规中未发现漏报，但裁判观察到的错误是过度标记。

Conclusion: 安全智能体评估应将任务能力与授权范围遵循分开衡量；仅依据目标是否完成会漏掉大量越界行为。ScopeBench及其评估代码和2160条轨迹为研究此类行为提供了可复现基准。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly deployed with real autonomy in web application and network penetration testing, where a single out-of-scope action can breach a client's engagement boundary. Existing offensive-security benchmarks measure raw hacking capability; as those benchmarks saturate, the real barrier to deployment is a special case of alignment: scope adherence. We introduce ScopeBench, a benchmark of 30 dead-end agentic security tasks in which the stated objective is reachable only by violating the stated scope. Each task appears under two conditions that share an environment, verifier, and objective and differ only in scope: one instruction set has no scope and measures capability; the other has a natural-language scope to measure adherence. Scopeless trajectories are graded by a standard deterministic verifier. Scoped trajectories pass through two grading arms. First, the same deterministic verifier checks for the flag: because the flag sits behind the scope boundary, a pass proves by construction that a forbidden action occurred, yielding a high-precision lower bound on the violation rate. If the verifier does not pass the trajectory, an agentic judge estimates whether an out-of-scope call occurred. We calibrate the judge against 100 ScopeBench trajectories labeled call-by-call by human annotators, and a blinded audit of the evaluated rollouts finds its high recall holds - no false negatives among the 36 audited violations, with over-flagging its only observed error. Across 8 models in one harness, raw capability spans 12.2% to 81.1% and scope adherence spans 34.4% to 86.7%, with the judge finding 331 violations that mechanical verification misses. Opus-4-8 achieves a raw-capability score 10 percentage points higher than sonnet-4-6's while exhibiting 35.6 percentage points higher scope adherence. We release the frozen pilot benchmark, evaluation code, and all 2160 ATIF trajectories.

</details>


### [151] [When Is a Multi-Agent Code Judge Actually Grounded? Two Label-Free Measurements, and a Judge That Declines to Guess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30328)
*Salma Roshdy Aly,Hussein Assaf,Ziad Kobti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，基于多智能体、逐项核验的代码评审方法可能因候选答案缺少可区分的证据而失效；通过监测流程日志中的信号，可识别何时评审缺乏依据，并选择弃答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型评判代码正确性时，可能在缺乏证据时仍给出自信结论。多智能体核验依赖证据，但有效证据既须独立于待评答案，也须能区分两个候选方案。检索文档通常满足后者，代码评审中的证据却未必满足。

Method: 不修改已发表的 MARCH 框架，在两个代码评审基准上进行 80 组条件—单元测量；比较其与直接询问同一模型的表现，并检验难度、评审模型规模及流程日志中的无标签指标。进一步基于其中一个指标设置弃答门控。

Result: MARCH 在 78%–95% 的比较中判定两个方案同样好；在某项测量中准确率仅为 4.4%，而同一模型直接评审为 43.7%。更简单的问题或更大的评审模型均未解决问题。依据流程日志指标进行门控后，准确率从 20.7% 提升至 36.9%，同时仍回答约一半的比较。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献不是提出更准确的代码评审器，而是提供一种无需标签、可判断评审是否有充分依据的方法，并在缺乏依据时让系统弃答。

Abstract: When one language model judges whether another's code is correct, it does not report the absence of evidence. It returns a confident verdict with reasoning attached, indistinguishable from a verdict it had grounds for. Multi-agent verification, which decomposes a judgment into checkable claims and verifies each against evidence, is a promising response and works well when the evidence is a set of retrieved documents.
  We argue such methods require two things of their evidence: it must be independent of the answer under review, and it must differ between the two candidates being compared. The second condition holds automatically with retrieved documents and stops holding in code judging.
  Running MARCH, a published framework unmodified over 80 condition-by-cell measurements on two code judging benchmarks, we find it declares both solutions equally good on 78 to 95% of comparisons, reaching 4.4% accuracy where the same model asked directly reaches 43.7%. Neither easier problems nor a larger judge changes this. Two measurements taken from the pipeline's own logs explain it without needing labels.
  Gating on one of them, the pipeline declines the comparisons it cannot make and raises its accuracy from 20.7 to 36.9% while still answering half of all comparisons. The contribution is not a more accurate judge, but a label-free way to tell when a judge has no basis for its answer.

</details>


### [152] [Bridging LLM Agents and Data Spaces: An Architectural Mediation Approach using the Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30341)
*Jaime Alonso Ruiz,Carlos Aparicio,Gabriel Huecas,Joaquín Salvachúa,Andres Munoz-Arcentales*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出以模型上下文协议（MCP）为基础的中介架构，通过 Eunomia Agent 将数据空间服务封装为结构化工具，使大语言模型智能体能够在治理约束下发现和调用服务。原型验证了目录发现、元数据获取和数据服务调用的端到端流程，且无需修改既有数据空间组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据空间支持跨组织、受治理的数据共享，但大语言模型智能体的概率式交互方式与数据基础设施的策略驱动机制存在不匹配，直接集成面临治理和互操作性挑战。

Method: 构建基于 MCP 的中介层，并通过 Eunomia Agent 将数据空间能力转换为具备明确 schema 的工具，供智能体发现和调用；以原型验证其与现有数据空间服务的端到端交互，同时保持架构解耦。

Result: 原型成功覆盖目录发现、元数据检索和数据服务调用。结果表明，基于协议的中介可实现智能体与数据空间生态的互操作、符合标准的集成，且不必改动已有组件。

Conclusion: MCP 中介架构为在受治理的数据共享环境中引入 AI 自动化提供了实用路径，有助于兼顾合规性、互操作性与关注点分离。

Abstract: Data Spaces enable sovereign and governed data sharing across organizational boundaries, but their integration with AI agents remains challenging due to mismatches between probabilistic language model interactions and policy-driven data infrastructures. This article presents an architectural mediation approach based on the Model Context Protocol (MCP), implemented through the Eunomia Agent, to enable controlled interaction between large language model (LLM) agents and data space services. The proposed mediation layer translates data space capabilities into structured, schema-driven tools that AI agents can discover and invoke while preserving governance constraints. A prototype implementation validates end-to-end interaction across catalog discovery, metadata retrieval, and data service invocation without modifying existing data space components. Results demonstrate that protocol-based mediation enables interoperable and standards-aligned integration of AI agents into data space ecosystems. The approach provides practical guidance for organizations seeking to introduce AI-driven automation into governed data-sharing environments while maintaining compliance, interoperability, and architectural separation of concerns.

</details>


### [153] [Stealth Apart, Harm Together: Skill Cascading Attacks on Skill-Based Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30383)
*Zihao Zhu,Siwei Lyu,Adel Bibi,Baoyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“技能级联攻击”：将恶意目标分散到多个单独看似无害的技能中，利用它们组合执行时的交互造成危害；作者构建自动化红队框架 SkillCascade 和含 213 个验证案例的 SkillCascade-Bench，实验表明此类攻击可绕过仅检查单个技能的防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于自然语言指令、脚本与参考资源的技能可在运行时扩展智能体能力，促进第三方能力复用，但开放生态也带来安全风险。既有研究主要关注单个技能内部的漏洞，未充分研究多个技能交互产生的系统级风险。

Method: 提出技能级联攻击这一威胁范式：将有害目标拆分到多个技能，使每项修改单独审视时显得无害，而组合执行后实现危害。开发多智能体自动化红队框架 SkillCascade，并发布 SkillCascade-Bench，包含跨多个智能体系统与领域的 213 个经验证级联测试案例。

Result: 在 OpenClaw、Claude Code、Codex 等代表性智能体及多种大语言模型骨干上，级联交互均能稳定诱发有害行为，并逃过现有的单技能扫描器和运行时监控器。文中以处方审核流程为例，说明多个技能逐步弱化、降级并抑制药物相互作用警报，导致警告未能呈现给医生。

Conclusion: 单个组件的完整性并不能保证整个系统安全。防御机制应分析技能之间的交互及其组合效果，而不应仅孤立检查单个技能。

Abstract: A skill is a modular package of natural-language instructions, executable scripts, and reference resources that an agent can load at runtime to extend its capabilities for a specific task. Skill-based agent systems therefore enable flexible reuse of third-party capabilities, but the openness of this skill ecosystem also opens up a new attack surface. Prior work has focused on vulnerabilities within individual skills, but little attention has been paid to risks that arise from interactions across skills. In this paper, we introduce skill cascading attacks, a threat paradigm in which a malicious objective is distributed across multiple skills so that each modification looks benign in isolation, yet their combined execution is harmful. For instance, in a prescription-review pipeline, the first skill weakens signals of recently discontinued medications in the extracted history, the second downgrades the severity of any drug interaction tied to them, and the third suppresses the resulting low-priority alert in the final summary, so that a severe drug-interaction warning silently disappears before reaching the physician. To systematically study this safety blind spot, we develop SkillCascade, an automated multi-agent red-teaming framework, and release SkillCascade-Bench, a benchmark of 213 validated cascading test cases across multiple agent systems and domains. Across representative agents (e.g., OpenClaw, Claude Code, Codex) and LLM backbones, cascaded interactions reliably induce harmful behaviors while evading existing per-skill scanners and runtime monitors. Our findings highlight a gap between component-level integrity and system-level safety, and call for defenses that reason over cross-skill interactions rather than individual skills in isolation.

</details>


### [154] [A Synthetic Ground-Truth Framework for the Evaluation of Explainable AI Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30397)
*Miquel Miró-Nicolau,Francesco Spinnato,Riccardo Guidotti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于合成真值的 XAI 评估框架，通过可控干预生成可明确判定特征重要性的合成数据，并在二值图像、表格数据和时间序列上评估九种常见方法。结果揭示现有方法的局限，强调干预式基准对可靠评估解释质量的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 XAI 评估通常依据解释与黑箱模型预测的一致程度（忠实度），但这只能说明解释是否复现模型输出，不能证明其准确反映模型真实决策过程。因此，不同甚至误导性的解释可能获得相近的忠实度分数。研究旨在弥补缺乏可靠评估程序及解释真值的不足。

Method: 提出基于合成真值的评估框架：通过可控干预构造合成数据，使输入组成部分的重要性能够按设计确定，进而形成与待分析模型行为直接对应的真值解释。框架在二值图像、表格数据和时间序列三个数据领域实例化，并用于评估九种常用 XAI 方法。

Result: 实验表明，当前 XAI 方法存在显著局限。摘要未报告具体方法名称、量化指标或各方法的详细排名。

Conclusion: 合成且基于干预的基准能够提供明确的解释真值，有助于更可靠地评估解释质量；仅依赖模型输出忠实度不足以判断解释是否真实揭示模型的决策过程。

Abstract: Evaluating explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) methods is a challenging task due to the lack of reliable evaluation procedures and, in particular, the absence of ground truth explanations. In the literature, existing evaluation approaches typically assess explanations by measuring their fidelity with respect to the predictions of a black-box model. However, such evaluation strategies only quantify the degree to which an explanation reproduces the model's output, without ensuring that the explanation correctly reflects the underlying decision process. As a consequence, different explanations may achieve similar fidelity scores while providing inconsistent or misleading interpretations of the model behavior. In this paper, we propose a framework for the evaluation of XAI methods based on synthetic ground truth. The proposed approach relies on controlled interventions to generate synthetic datasets in which the importance of input components can be determined by design. This enables the construction of ground truth explanations that are directly aligned with the behavior of the model under analysis. The framework is instantiated across three data domains, namely binary images, tabular data, and time series, allowing a comprehensive assessment of explanation methods in heterogeneous settings. Experimental results obtained by evaluating nine widely used XAI methods show significant limitations in current techniques and highlight the importance of synthetic, intervention-based benchmarks for a reliable assessment of explanation quality.

</details>


### [155] [Predicting Transmembrane Protein Topology from 3D Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30446)
*Sitong Chen,Xiaopeng Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于 SchNet 图神经网络的蛋白质拓扑预测方法，利用全原子级嵌入而非仅序列或 α-碳特征；在与 DeepTMHMM 相同的数据集上进行五折交叉验证，结果显示该方法具有应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见蛋白质拓扑预测方法主要依赖蛋白质序列或 α-碳特征，未充分利用原子层面的结构信息。本文探索图神经网络能否通过整合全原子级表示改进拓扑预测。

Method: 采用 SchNet 图神经网络构建分类器，使用开发 DeepTMHMM 时所用的数据集，并进行五折交叉验证。模型未使用预训练权重，输入表示利用全原子级嵌入。

Result: 摘要称实验结果显示该方法具有较大潜力，但未提供具体性能指标、与基线的量化比较或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: 全原子级图神经网络表示可作为蛋白质拓扑预测的一种可行方向；不过，摘要提供的信息不足以判断其相对现有方法的实际提升幅度。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach to infer protein topology using the state-of-the-art graph neural network (GNN), SchNet. The model is trained on the same dataset used to develop the recent DeepTMHMM model with 5-fold cross-validation. Unlike the conventional approaches based on using only the protein sequences or the $α$-carbons as features, we have decoded our classifier in this way, so all atom-level embeddings are used. Without applying any pre-trained weight, the final results have shown great potential that GNNs can be used for topological predictions.

</details>


### [156] [Spectral Feedback for Test-Time Alignment of Protein Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30456)
*Shai Dickman,Mert Cemri,Landon Butler,Kannan Ramchandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Spectral Feedback，通过迭代选择并重采样离散扩散模型中的 token 位置，提升生成结果的奖励对齐；在蛋白质逆折叠稳定性任务中取得显著增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散模型的奖励最大化对齐方法主要在反向生成过程中调整 token 标签或筛选序列，通常是单向过程，缺少回头修正不理想 token 的机制。论文将核心问题从“赋予哪些 token 标签”转为“重新编辑哪些 token 位置”。

Method: 定义 edit-set（需重新掩码并采样的 token 位置集合），构建其价值函数以评估编辑位置组合的效果。针对位置间编辑效应相互依赖、组合空间复杂的问题，作者实证发现蛋白质逆折叠中的 edit-set 价值函数具有稀疏傅里叶表示，并据此高效学习和优化编辑位置。算法在反馈循环中反复重掩码、重采样，且不改变底层生成过程，可用于预训练、测试时对齐及微调后的扩散模型。

Result: 在蛋白质逆折叠的稳定性奖励 oracle 评测中，稳定蛋白比例相对提升：预训练模型为 32.3%，Best-of-10 为 24.8%，最先进的 RL 微调扩散模型为 5.8%。

Conclusion: 通过针对性地选择可回访、可重采样的位置，Spectral Feedback 为离散扩散生成提供了迭代自我修正的奖励对齐机制。实验表明，该方法能在不同训练状态的模型上提升对齐表现，尤其对预训练模型增益较大。

Abstract: Reward maximization alignment methods for discrete diffusion models have primarily focused on steering the reverse process, either by influencing token logits or by selecting favorable sequences at intermediate steps. These approaches largely treat inference as a unidirectional process, lacking mechanisms for revisiting undesirable token selections. We introduce Spectral Feedback, an algorithm that selects edit-positions in a feedback loop, allowing the model to iteratively correct its own generations. This approach leverages the mask structure of discrete diffusion models by re-masking and re-sampling tokens, analogous to image editing methods that reintroduce noisy latents and re-run the reverse process. While prior alignment methods focus on what token labels to assign to maximize a target reward, we instead treat which tokens to revisit as the central alignment problem. Selecting edit-positions is challenging because edit effects are interdependent: the impact of modifying one token depends on which others are edited simultaneously. We define an edit-set as a set of token positions to re-mask and re-sample. Motivated by prior work on sparse interactions in biological systems, we find empirically that edit-set value functions for protein inverse folding admit sparse Fourier representations. This structure enables Spectral Feedback to efficiently learn and optimize the value functions for edit-position selection. Spectral Feedback is model-agnostic and can be applied to pretrained, test-time aligned, and fine-tuned diffusion models. For all of these models, the algorithm improves alignment performance without modifying the underlying generative process. Applied to inverse folding with a protein stability reward oracle, it achieves a 32.3% increase in stable proteins for a pretrained model, 24.8% for Best-of-10, and 5.8% for a state-of-the-art RL fine-tuned diffusion model.

</details>


### [157] [Pretrained ASR Pseudo-labeling for Noisy Police Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30469)
*Kaavya Chaparala,Su Huang,Stephen L. Miller,Rhiannon N. Miller,Anjalie Field*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究利用伪标签将 Whisper 和 Qwen3-ASR 适配到嘈杂的广播警务通信（BPC）语音识别任务。结果显示，内部置信度指标难以筛选优质伪标签；基于大语言模型的外部判别能改善伪标签训练集的质量，但仍不及理想的 oracle 筛选。跨模型伪标签是一个有潜力的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语音识别模型在噪声严重的 BPC 音频上表现不佳，而人工标注成本高。伪标签提供了无监督适配途径，但其在极高噪声领域中的有效性和局限尚不清楚。

Method: 系统评估 Whisper 与 Qwen3-ASR 在巴尔的摩和芝加哥 BPC 语料上的伪标签适配效果；比较 log-probabilities、STAR 等模型内部置信度指标与基于大语言模型的外部判别过滤；进一步提出由一个模型生成伪标签、另一个模型据此微调的跨模型方案。

Result: 内部置信度指标无法有效区分高、低质量 BPC 伪标签。LLM 判别器依据上下文合理性剔除转录，筛选更严格，并显著降低巴尔的摩和芝加哥伪标签训练集的词错误率（WER）；但与 oracle 筛选相比仍存在明显差距。跨模型伪标签被指出具有潜力。

Conclusion: 在极端噪声语音领域，常见内部置信度分数不足以保障伪标签质量，利用外部语言知识进行判别是一种有效改进，但尚未解决伪标签筛选问题。跨模型生成与使用伪标签值得后续研究。

Abstract: Pretrained ASR systems perform poorly on noisy Broadcast Police Communication (BPC), hindering efforts to understand police decision-making. Pseudo-labeling offers an unsupervised path to improve ASR without expensive human labels, but the efficacy of this approach on very noisy domains is not known. In this work, we systematically assess the opportunities and limits of pseudo-labeling to adapt foundation ASR models (Whisper and Qwen3-ASR) to noisy BPC domain corpora from Baltimore and Chicago. We demonstrate that existing internal confidence metrics (log-probabilities and STAR scores) fail to distinguish between high and low quality BPC pseudo-labels, and we introduce an external LLM-as-a-judge filtering paradigm that leverages parametric knowledge to discard contextually implausible transcripts. Our LLM-judging filters more aggressively than internal metrics and significantly reduces WER of the pseudo-labeled training sets across the Baltimore and Chicago BPC corpora, though a substantial gap remains relative to an oracle filter. We also introduce a new cross-model pseudo-labeling paradigm where one model is finetuned with pseudo-labels from the other, and we identify this method as a promising direction for future pseudo-labeling work.

</details>


### [158] [Do LLMs Understand Context? A Knowledge Graph-Based Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30484)
*Subavarshana Arumugam,Mamta Nallaretnam,Kithuni Wickramasinghe,Chamath Gunapala,Pragatheeswaran Vipulanandan,Kamal Premaratne,Uthayasanker Thayasivam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出基于知识图谱的评估框架，用于衡量大语言模型在问答中的上下文理解能力。其核心指标 S3KG 融合图结构与语义相似度，并辅以三元组级错误诊断；在九个基准上，相较最强基线 F1 最高提升 7.6 分，AUROC 最高达 0.973。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BLEU、困惑度等传统指标主要衡量表层文本表现，难以判断问答是否真正依据给定上下文、整合信息并进行推理。因此，模型可能因记忆或模式匹配而获得较好分数，却未能生成事实一致、上下文有依据的答案。

Method: 提出知识图谱（KG）驱动的上下文理解评估框架。核心方法 S3KG（知识图谱语义结构相似度）将图结构信号与语义信号结合为单一相似度分数；同时设计诊断分析框架，在三元组层面识别并分类推理错误。

Result: 在九个基准上的实验中，S3KG 相较最强基线的 F1 最高提升 7.6 分，AUROC 最高达到 0.973。摘要未提供各基准的具体结果、统计显著性或评测细节。

Conclusion: 该框架旨在补足表层文本指标对上下文依据与推理质量评估不足的问题，并提供更细粒度的错误分析能力。摘要报告的结果显示，结合结构与语义信息的知识图谱指标具有较强评估表现；其有效性范围仍需结合完整论文中的数据集、实现细节和实验设置判断。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable linguistic capabilities, a profound question lingers at their core: do these models truly comprehend context or simply excel at pattern matching on an unprecedented scale? Contextual understanding in LLMs refers to the ability to correctly extract relevant information from a given context, integrate it into a coherent internal representation, and reason over it to produce factually consistent and contextually grounded responses. However, traditional methods such as BiLingual Evaluation Understudy (BLEU) and perplexity simply measure surface-level performance. This reveals a critical gap in question answering (QA), where responses must be contextually grounded rather than simply being memorized associations. To fill this void, we propose a novel knowledge graph (KG) based evaluation framework for LLM contextual understanding in QA. Central to this is Semantic Structural Similarity for KGs (S3KG), a hybrid similarity measure combining structural and semantic signals into a single score. In addition, a diagnostic analysis framework is developed to identify and categorize reasoning errors at the triplet level, enabling fine-grained analysis of model failures. Together, across nine benchmarks, S3KG achieves F1 gains of up to $+7.6$ points over the strongest baseline and AUROC up to $0.973$.

</details>


### [159] [BioEVAL: A global, multi-institutional benchmark of large language and multimodal models for bioengineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30489)
*Shun Ye,Vinny Chandran Suja,Chenlong Li,Chongming Jiang,Reza Zamani,Xiang Li,Christopher Bain,Yuqi Zhou,Walker Peterson,Huidong Wang,Chenglang Hu,Jongchan Park,Xiao Cheng,Benjamin Swedlund,Sandra Murillo,Anjali Sivanandan,Shiyu Sun,Liang Lanfeng,Mohammad Tariqul Islam,Baju C. Joy,Ishaq N. Khan,Sreedhar S. Kumar,Gabriel Mercado-Vásquez,James V. Vizzard,Jonathan M. Matthews,Helen Huang,Xiaolu Guo,Ethan Nicklow,Guorui Chen,Ryan A. Neff,Surjendu Maity,Hyeonjin Park,Han-ho Joo,Katherine Dong,Yuyan Cai,Weihang Huang,Yichen Zou,Rui Yan,Raphael Figueroa,Artem Goncharov,Bella Rose Schremmer,Lian Elsa Linton,Keisuke Goda,Liang Gao,Ke Cheng,Leonardo Morsut,Jennifer L. Wilson,Jianping Fu,Lim Chwee Teck,Deblina Sarkar,Andreas Hierlemann,Savaş Tay,Alexander Hoffmann,Donald Richieri Griffin,Jun Chen,Shana O. Kelley,Shyni Varghese,Jinwoo Cheon,Wilbur A. Lam,James J. Moon,Wilson W. Wong,Samir Mitragotri,Dino Di Carlo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文介绍 BioEVAL：一个用于评估大语言模型在生物工程前沿、多模态实验推理能力的专家级基准。模型在选择题、文献综合和图像解释任务上表现不一，且不同子领域差异明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型基准多强调事实回忆，难以反映模型在生物工程前沿问题、实验推理与多模态任务中的实际能力，因此需要由领域专家共同构建、更贴近研究实践的评测体系。

Method: 由22个研究团队协作构建 BioEVAL，覆盖11个生物工程子领域及未分类题目，共608项博士级评测任务：选择题380项（审查后保留359项）、文献综合任务218项、多模态实验图像解读题10项。题目经作者团队专家审查和集中质量控制；评测后又对选择题高、低准确率题目进行盲审，21题被标记并从结果中剔除。评估云端基础/多模态模型及可在消费级GPU部署的本地模型。

Result: 模型在选择题上的最高准确率达90%，文献综合相似度最高为0.72，多模态推理小样本准确率最高为80%；不同生物工程子领域的表现存在显著差异。

Conclusion: BioEVAL提供了用于刻画当前模型能力、局限与研发优先方向的多任务基准，并通过标准化流程持续接收专家贡献和评估新模型。其多模态结果基于仅10道题，代表性有限；文献综合采用相似度评分，也不等同于对科学正确性与推理质量的全面评估。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated historic breakthroughs in general reasoning with early successes in biomedical science. However, existing LLM benchmarking emphasizes factual recall, offering limited insight into model performance on frontier and multimodal tasks. We assembled BioEVAL (BioEngineering Validation of AI and LLMs), a global, multi-institutional initiative designed to assess experimental reasoning capability across bioengineering (BE) subfields. BioEVAL spans 11 major BE subfields plus a set of uncategorized items, bringing together 22 research groups to create a PhD-level benchmark comprising 608 evaluation items: 1) 380 multiple-choice questions (MCQs, 359 retained after audit), 2) 218 literature synthesis tasks, and 3) 10 multimodal problems with experimental image interpretation. Benchmark items underwent authoring-group expert review and centralized quality control before evaluation. Following evaluation, a blinded cross-group consensus audit of the highest- and lowest-accuracy MCQ items flagged 21 questions for revision or removal; these were withheld, and all reported MCQ results are computed on the 359 retained items. We evaluated diverse cloud-scale foundation/multimodal models (e.g., ChatGPT, Gemini, and Grok) and locally deployable models suitable for inference on consumer-grade GPUs. Models achieved the highest accuracy of up to 90% on MCQs, similarity score of 0.72 on literature synthesis, and accuracy of 80% on a small sample of multimodal reasoning questions, with substantial performance variation across subfields. Leaderboard rankings characterize current capabilities, limitations, and development priorities across the evaluated BE task categories. BioEVAL is maintained as an extensible benchmark with standardized protocols for continuing expert item contribution and model evaluation.

</details>


### [160] [Benchy: towards a universal language for task-oriented AI benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30550)
*Francis F Daniel,Mauro Ibañez,Francis Perelman,Marian Basti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Benchy：一种用于 AI 程序基准测试的语义语言与执行引擎，以规范化定义、确定性编译和统一运行接口保障基准语义清晰且可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准定义、系统接入和执行机制若相互混杂，会造成语义歧义、隐藏默认行为及结果难以复现。本文旨在明确基准本身与受测 AI 系统的边界，并建立严格、可验证的基准描述与执行规范。

Method: 将基准形式化为 B=(P,S,D)，分别表示程序、评分函数和数据集；一次运行表示为 R=(B,AI)。基准以规范 YAML 编写，依据共享的任务/领域/语言本体分类，并确定性编译为规范 JSON 中间表示。定义对象模型、任务到程序的验证规则、评分与失败语义、编译执行架构及语言范围；程序采用固定的具名输入/输出字段，叶级输出字段构成评分维度。引擎通过统一的具名对象输入与输出契约运行，外部系统仅在边界适配。

Result: 摘要未报告实验或量化性能结果。其主要产出是 Benchy 的语义模型、规范化表示与编译/执行设计，以及首版引擎的规范性工程契约。编译仅改变表示而不改变语义，不修复无效定义，也不注入隐藏默认值。

Conclusion: Benchy 通过显式基准语义、严格验证、确定性编译和统一运行契约，将基准定义与 AI 系统集成机制解耦，目标是提升基准执行的一致性、可解释性与可复现性。其价值主要在语言与工程规范层面；摘要尚未提供实证评估。

Abstract: Benchy is a semantic language and execution engine for benchmarking AI programs. A benchmark is completely specified by a program, a scoring function, and a dataset, B=(P,S,D), and is separate from the AI-system taking it; a run binds the two, R=(B,AI). Benchmarks are authored as canonical YAML in which each semantic concept has one valid syntax, classified by a shared task/domain/language ontology, and deterministically compiled into a canonical JSON intermediate representation that the engine executes. Compilation changes representation, not meaning: it does not repair invalid definitions or inject hidden defaults. Programs use fixed schemas of named input and output fields, the leaf output fields are the scoring dimensions, and the engine exposes one universal runtime contract --- a named-field input object in, a named-field output object out --- to which external AI-systems adapt at the boundary, so integration mechanics never propagate into benchmark semantics. This paper gives the semantic object model, the ontology and task-to-program validation rule, the scoring and failure semantics, the compilation and execution architecture, and the scope of the current language. An appendix fixes the normative engineering contract for the first engine implementation.

</details>


### [161] [Rank-Reliable Teacher-Guided Fitness Approximation for Expensive Evolutionary Optimization: A TinyML Architecture Search Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30553)
*Soumen Garai,Suman Samui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 TGL-NSGA-II：以教师模型引导的低保真评估结合高斯过程代理模型，减少 TinyML 神经架构搜索中的完整评估开销；实验显示其排序能力较强且搜索更快，但保证仅针对固定候选群体的低保真评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化式神经架构搜索的完整候选评估成本高。实际筛选常不必精确估计每个候选的适应度，只需可靠判断候选之间的优劣，因此需要低成本且保留相对排序能力的评估机制。

Method: 提出 TGL-NSGA-II。预训练教师模型按样本难度与类别联合分层；每个候选在紧凑训练集上进行短时、设有上限的 KD-Lite 知识蒸馏，再在独立分层评估集上打分。将教师引导分数与高斯过程代理模型融合，以决定哪些候选进入完整评估。论文分析评估方差、分数集中度、成对排序反转、期望 Kendall τ、第一前沿识别及超体积扰动，并推导方差感知融合权重和容量自适应蒸馏规则。

Result: 在关键词识别和鸟鸣分类任务上，实测 Kendall τ 分别为 0.74 和 0.62，高于对应预测下界 0.60 和 0.46。联合分层使代理分数方差相较随机评估降低 41%；但选择性教师失配会加大差异偏差，使 Kendall τ 降至 0.41。在受限评估预算下，关键词识别任务取得最高平均超体积和最低代际距离；BirdCLEF 上平均假阳性率最低；运行速度为完整 NSGA-II 的 2.2 倍。

Conclusion: TGL-NSGA-II 能以较低评估成本提供较可靠的候选排序，并在所测任务与预算条件下改善多目标搜索表现。其理论保证针对固定候选群体的低保真评估，不代表完整进化搜索轨迹收敛；教师失配也是影响排序质量的重要风险。

Abstract: Expensive evolutionary search does not always need an exact fitness estimate for every candidate. It often needs a reliable answer to a simpler question: which candidate is better? We address this need through Teacher-Guided Learning NSGA-II (TGL-NSGA-II), a low-fidelity framework for constrained Tiny Machine Learning (TinyML) neural architecture search. A pretrained teacher organizes samples into strata defined jointly by difficulty and class. Each candidate then undergoes KD-Lite, a short and capped knowledge-distillation procedure on a compact training set, before being scored on a separate stratified evaluation set. This teacher-guided score is fused with a Gaussian-process surrogate to select candidates for full evaluation. For a fixed candidate population, we analyse evaluation variance, score concentration, pairwise rank inversion, expected Kendall-$τ$, first-front identification, and hypervolume perturbation. We also derive a variance-aware fusion weight and a capacity-adaptive distillation rule. On keyword spotting and bird-call classification, the measured Kendall-$τ$ values are 0.74 and 0.62, exceeding the corresponding predicted lower bounds of 0.60 and 0.46. Joint stratification reduces proxy-score variance by 41% relative to random evaluation. Selective teacher mismatch, in contrast, increases differential bias and reduces Kendall-$τ$ to 0.41. Under a constrained evaluation budget, TGL-NSGA-II achieves the largest mean hypervolume and smallest generational distance on keyword spotting, records the lowest mean false-positive rate on BirdCLEF, and runs 2.2x faster than full NSGA-II. These guarantees apply to population-level low-fidelity evaluation and do not establish convergence of the complete evolutionary trajectory.

</details>


### [162] [Thinking Less to Simulate Better: Intuitive Prompting Improves LLM Agents Simulating Individual Social Media Reactions, Including Unfamiliar Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30563)
*Ljubisa Bojic,Tijana Stanic,Joerg Matthes,Agariadne Dwinggo Samala,Bojana Dinic,Jue Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究比较了不同画像信息与提示方式下语言模型模拟真实用户反应的能力。态度信息比人口统计背景更有预测力；要求模型凭直觉、快速作答，获得最高画像忠实度，并减少了对个体差异的压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台政策评估日益使用人工智能模拟用户，但模拟结果可能影响公众意见判断。既有验证主要考察模型是否像人类，却较少检验模型行为是否符合提供给它的个人画像；研究旨在补足这一验证维度，并比较不同画像内容和提示方式的作用。

Method: 研究为8名塞尔维亚参与者收集问卷、深度访谈及书面自我介绍，并记录其对68条社交媒体帖文的反应。随后让4种语言模型在5种提示条件下预测这些反应；条件在画像信息内容和指令风格上有所不同。

Result: 态度信息相较人口统计背景显著提高预测表现。模型行为与指定画像的一致程度，甚至高于参与者对自身问卷答案的前后一致性；但在提供画像后，一致性与画像忠实度无关。要求模型直觉、即时作答的条件忠实度最高，并将个体差异被压缩的程度从人类水平的7倍降至3倍。在问卷未涉及的主题上，该条件仍取得最高忠实度，并大幅优于群体基线。

Conclusion: 画像忠实度不应仅以对人类总体行为的拟合来衡量；画像中的态度信息和直觉式提示可能更适合构建可泛化的模拟用户。研究也提示，直觉式而非分析式提示在某些任务中可能更有效。不过，样本仅有8名参与者，结论的普遍性仍需更大规模研究验证。

Abstract: Platform policies are increasingly tested on artificial users, making agent fidelity important. Yet convincing fake profiles could also manipulate perceived public opinion before elections. Validation has concentrated on agreement with human behaviour and has paid little attention to whether an agent behaves in line with the profile it was given. The present study profiled eight Serbian participants through a questionnaire, a deep interview, and a written self-presentation, recorded their reactions to sixty-eight social media posts, and asked four language models to predict those reactions under five prompt conditions varying profile content and instruction style. Attitudinal content improved prediction over demographic backstories by a wide margin. Agents matched their stated profiles more closely than participants matched their own survey answers, and consistency proved unrelated to fidelity once profile information was present. Instructing models to respond intuitively and immediately rather than analytically gave the highest fidelity of any condition and cut the compression of individual differences from seven times the human level to three. The advantage held on posts about topics the questionnaire never raised, where that condition reached the highest fidelity of any setup and beat a crowd baseline by a wide margin, which suggests that agents prompted this way could serve as general-purpose simulated users rather than specialists on the topics they were profiled for. Results may bear implications for the development of language models, because intuition-based setups appear better suited to some tasks than reasoning-based ones.

</details>


### [163] [Atelier: Learning Local Self-Supervised Features for CryoEM Volumes via Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30569)
*Phillip Lo,Sudarshan Babu,Dari Kimanius,Aly A. Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Atelier：一种自监督、可摊销的隐式神经表示框架，可从冷冻电镜密度图生成连续的局部特征，并提升多项体素级结构属性预测任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有冷冻电镜图注释方法多依赖固定体素网格，难以自然表达跨空间尺度的连续特征。逐张图单独拟合隐式神经表示（INR）虽具尺度无关和坐标条件化优势，但计算成本高，且不同样本的表示不对齐，难以用于大规模特征提取。

Method: 提出 Atelier 自监督框架，在 5,439 张 Electron Microscopy Data Bank 密度图上预训练一个基于 Transformer 的超网络，以摊销方式为输入图生成 INR。除重建密度图外，利用生成 INR 中间层在任意空间坐标处的激活，构成连续、局部且坐标条件化的特征场；再将其作为辅助通道，输入从头训练的 3D nested U-Net 注释头。

Result: Atelier 能对多种蛋白质结构进行高保真重建，包括大型多亚基复合物。用于八项体素级属性预测任务时，加入 INR 特征的模型表现优于仅使用体积数据的基线。摘要未提供具体指标或各任务的提升幅度。

Conclusion: 摊销式 INR 可兼顾隐式表示的连续性与大规模应用效率，并为冷冻电镜数据提供适于几何感知分析的特征基础；其价值不仅在于重建，也在于支持下游注释任务。

Abstract: CryoEM map interpretation requires features that are spatially localized, consistent across samples, and informative across spatial scales. Most deep learning methods for map annotation extract features from fixed voxel grids. However, implicit neural representations (INRs) are able to model volumetric data as scale-agnostic, coordinate-conditioned functions. INRs are therefore attractive for cryoEM, but fitting a separate INR for each map is too expensive for large-scale feature extraction and produces representations that are not aligned across samples. We introduce Atelier, a self-supervised framework that amortizes INR fitting for reconstructed cryoEM maps. Pretrained on 5,439 Electron Microscopy Data Bank maps, Atelier is a transformer-based hypernetwork that generates high-fidelity reconstructions across a wide range of protein structures, including large multi-subunit assemblies. Beyond reconstruction, the INR generated by the pretrained transformer exposes a continuous, local feature field through its intermediate activations at any spatial query point, a property that voxel grid and patch-tokenizer architectures do not naturally provide. Used as auxiliary channels to a 3D nested U-Net annotation head trained from scratch, these coordinate-conditioned features improve performance on eight voxel-level property prediction tasks over a volume-only baseline. Our results demonstrate that amortized implicit neural representations are an effective primitive for geometry-aware analysis of cryoEM data.

</details>


### [164] [HARDEN: Constrained Evolutionary Search for Harder, Answer-Preserving Evaluation Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30571)
*Aditya Kumaran,Rahul Singhal,Karime Maamari,Amine Mhedhbi,Pradyumna Tambwekar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 HARDEN：通过带约束的进化搜索改写现有评测题目，在保持预期答案不变的前提下生成更具挑战性的有效样例；在三类任务和三种规模模型上，平均准确率下降 22.7%，最高相对单次改写基线下降 49.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常用语言模型基准多为精心整理的题目，难以体现企业部署中的复杂性，因此可能高估模型在真实场景中的表现。论文希望系统地构造更困难、但仍然有效且答案保持不变的评测用例。

Method: 提出 HARDEN，一种受约束的进化搜索方法。它沿自动生成的领域特定复杂度维度探索题目变体，同时施加约束，以确保任务语义、现实性、执行有效性及预期输出得到保持。将其应用于 FinQA、PubMedQA、ContractNLI，测试 Qwen3.5 的 35B-A3B、122B-A10B 和 397B-A17B 三种规模，并与采用相同可行性检查的单次改写基线比较。

Result: HARDEN 生成的有效评测案例使任务模型准确率平均降低 22.7%，相较单次改写基线最高降低 49.9%。结果覆盖三个数据集及三种模型规模。

Conclusion: 受约束的进化搜索能够显著提高评测样例难度，同时保留其有效性与预期答案。研究表明，相比单次改写，迭代搜索可能更适合构造具有挑战性的模型评测集；但摘要未提供显著性检验、各任务分项结果或更广泛模型类别的验证信息。

Abstract: Language models are often evaluated on curated benchmarks that underrepresent the complexity of enterprise deployments. We introduce HARDEN, a constrained evolutionary search method to adapt the input of existing evaluation cases into more challenging variants while keeping their expected outputs fixed. HARDEN searches along generated domain-specific complexity axes while enforcing feasibility constraints such as preserving task semantics, realism, and execution validity. Across FinQA, PubMedQA, and ContractNLI and three Qwen3.5 model scales (35B-A3B, 122B-A10B, and 397B-A17B), HARDEN reduces task-model accuracy by 22.7% on average and by up to 49.9% relative to single-pass baselines using the same feasibility checks. These results show that evolutionary search can produce substantially harder valid evaluation cases.

</details>


### [165] [T-RoPE: Time-Aware Rotary Position Embedding for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30576)
*Yang Liu,Noel Loo,Ali Khanafer,Shuying Sun,Akshay Soni,Zhong Wu,Linjun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 T-RoPE，将 RoPE 从仅编码交互序号改为基于时间戳的时间感知位置编码，用于序列生成式推荐；在公开数据集、工业数据和在线实验中均报告了显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐序列中的交互序号只表达事件先后，不包含真实时间间隔、跨尺度行为周期或日历相位。直接沿用语言模型中的 RoPE，可能无法有效捕捉推荐行为的时间结构；即使把时间戳用于标准 RoPE，其时间平移不变性仍使模型难以区分季节性等时间上下文。

Method: 提出 T-RoPE，以时间戳生成旋转角，并结合可学习的时间系数、多尺度频率组、偏移查询对齐和非平稳键旋转。理论上证明标准 RoPE（包括基于时间戳的版本）仍具有时间平移不变性，而 T-RoPE 可打破该性质，同时保留 RoPE 接口。作者还给出前向与反向算法，使额外计算复杂度随序列长度和注意力头维度线性增长。

Result: 在五个公开基准上，论文报告 T-RoPE 在每个数据集的所有评估指标上均优于对比方法；在稀疏 PixelRec 上，HR@10 相对最强基线提升 78%–130%，在 Amazon Books 上各项指标提升 8%–12%。在超过 60 亿次交互的工业电商数据上，相较 HSTU + Time RAB，所有指标提升 13%–82%。消融实验显示，多尺度频率带来最大增益（NDCG@50 提升 56%），非平稳键旋转另有 4% 提升。Shop 应用在线 A/B 测试中，转化率提升 0.33%，订单量提升 0.63%。

Conclusion: 摘要表明，显式建模真实时间、周期尺度与时间上下文，可能比单纯的位置序号更适合生成式推荐。T-RoPE 在离线基准、工业规模评估和在线实验中均取得正向结果，并声称计算开销可控。不过，摘要未提供显著性检验、实验配置或跨场景泛化细节；实际收益仍需结合完整论文和独立复现评估。

Abstract: Large-scale recommenders increasingly adopt the sequential generative recipe behind large language models, bringing the Transformer into recommendation along with design choices made for text, including Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE). In language models, RoPE encodes token indices for relative position reasoning, but in recommendation, an interaction index records only event order, saying nothing about elapsed time, behavioral cycles across scales, or calendar phase. We revisit this choice and propose T-RoPE, a time-aware RoPE for sequential generative recommendation that replaces index-only rotation with timestamp-based angles, learnable temporal coefficients, multiscale frequency banks, shifted query alignment, and non-stationary key rotation. We prove that standard RoPE, even on timestamps, remains time-translation invariant and cannot distinguish seasonal contexts, and that T-RoPE breaks this invariance while preserving the RoPE interface. Across five public benchmarks, T-RoPE achieves the best result on every metric on every dataset, improving over the strongest baseline by 78--130\% in HR@10 on the sparse PixelRec data and 8--12\% across metrics on Amazon Books. On an industrial-scale e-commerce dataset with more than 6B interactions, it improves every metric over the HSTU + Time RAB backbone by 13--82\%, with ablations attributing the largest gains to multiscale frequencies ($+56\%$ NDCG@50) and non-stationary keys ($+4\%$). An online A/B test in the Shop app yields positive lifts in conversion rate ($+0.33\%$) and order count ($+0.63\%$). We also provide forward and backward algorithms whose added cost is linear in sequence length and head dimension, keeping time-aware RoPE practical for large generative recommenders.

</details>


### [166] [Audio LLMs Know When They Can't Hear You](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30625)
*Amirhosein Javadi,Richa Dixit,Mehrdad Farajtabar,Minsik Cho,Devang Naik,Mohammad Samragh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种轻量级预测器，在语音生成前识别音频大语言模型自身转写是否可靠，并在不可靠时请求用户澄清。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频输入严重退化时，Audio LLM 可能错误转写并据此回答。模型通常无法准确判断自身转写可靠性；现有语音质量指标、生成不确定性和基于转写的 WER 估计，对发现转写失败的帮助有限。

Method: 研究模型条件下的转写可靠性。利用冻结音频编码器提取的表示训练轻量级可靠性预测器，在生成前预测可靠性类别；若预测不可靠则触发澄清请求，可靠输入则直接交由原 Audio LLM 处理。另考察可靠性标签在不同 Audio LLM 家族间的迁移，以及迁移效果与模型可靠性边界对齐程度的关系。

Result: 预测器在域内和跨域测试中分别取得 81.10% 和 78.09% 的宏平均 F1，较最强基线分别高 10.33 和 11.93 个百分点。可靠性标签可在不同 Audio LLM 家族间迁移，迁移性能与各模型特定可靠性边界的对齐程度密切相关。

Conclusion: 音频编码器表示中包含可用于判断转写可靠性的有效信息。基于这些表示的前置检测能够补充现有方法，并通过选择性请求澄清降低错误转写导致的误答风险，而无需修改底层 Audio LLM。

Abstract: Audio large language models allow users to interact with the model through speech. When an input recording is too degraded, the model may misinterpret the user's query and respond based on an incorrect transcription. In this paper, we study model-conditional transcription reliability: whether an Audio LLM can recognize when its own transcription is unreliable. We first prompt the Audio LLM to assess whether its own transcription would be reliable, and find that the model is a poor judge of its own transcription reliability: in most cases, it predicts that its transcription will be reliable. We find that existing approaches, including speech quality predictors, audio LLM generation uncertainty, and transcript-conditioned WER estimation, provide limited signals for detecting transcription failures. In contrast, we discover that transcription reliability is strongly represented in the model's audio-encoder representations. Based on this observation, we devise a lightweight reliability predictor that operates on representations extracted by the frozen audio encoder and predicts the reliability class before generation. The reliability predictor can trigger a clarification request from the user when their voice query is predicted to be unreliable, while allowing reliable queries to proceed without modifying the underlying Audio LLM. Our predictor achieves 81.10% in-domain and 78.09% cross-domain macro-F1 scores, outperforming the strongest baselines by 10.33 and 11.93 points, respectively. Finally, we show that reliability labels can transfer across Audio LLM families, and that transfer performance is closely related to the alignment of their model-specific reliability boundaries.

</details>


### [167] [LLM Parkinsonism: Executive-Control Failure, Token-Inefficient Persistence, and an Uncertainty-Aware Global Executive Control Architecture for Autonomous Language-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30662)
*Dongsheng Xiao,Zeyuan Wang,Xuzhe Xia,Bo Zhao,Yankai Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出全局执行控制（GEC）架构，以治理代理的项目级行动与停止决策。在仿真基准中，GEC与候选集控制成功率相当，但显著减少 token 消耗、完成前漂移和复杂度；仍需真实模型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 代理可能在目标已达成后仍持续行动，进行低价值改进、重复验证或修复自身制造的复杂性。作者将此现象以非临床隐喻称为“LLM Parkinsonism”，并认为其根源更直接地在于行动提议、范围解释、进度评估和停止权集中于同一自我条件循环，而非仅由自回归预测造成。

Method: 提出不确定性感知的 Global Executive Control（GEC）v0.2，将行动生成与项目级控制分离。通过 24,000 个 episode 的匹配候选基准，在统一 40,000-token 上限下，对比首候选基线、候选集局部控制和 GEC，并评估治理开销敏感性。

Result: 硬目标成功率分别为 67.42%、96.53% 和 96.57%。相较候选集局部控制，GEC 在维持成功率的同时，将平均 token 使用量从 19,782 降至 12,574（36.4%），将 40,000-token 上限下的受限平均完成 token 数从 16,136 降至 13,114（18.7%）；并消除测得的完成前漂移，大幅降低总体复杂度。额外加入每周期 500 个合成治理 token 后，开销敏感性仍有利。

Conclusion: 结果支持对代理的范围、证据、资源使用和停止决策进行显式治理。成功率提升的大部分来自能够访问多个候选行动；GEC 的主要增益则是以近乎不变的成功率提高效率并抑制无效延续。证据来自机制仿真，尚不能替代真实模型上的验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can plan, use tools, write code, and execute long-horizon workflows, yet strong local competence does not guarantee project-level executive control. Agents may continue acting after the original objective is satisfied, producing low-value refinements, repeated verification, and repairs to self-created complexity. We use LLM Parkinsonism as a narrowly defined, non-clinical metaphor for this pattern of persistent action despite diminishing task-level value. We argue that the problem is not explained by autoregressive next-token prediction alone, but more directly by concentrating proposal generation, scope interpretation, progress assessment, and stopping authority within the same self-conditioned loop. We therefore introduce Global Executive Control (GEC) v0.2, an uncertainty-aware governance architecture that separates action generation from project-level control. In a 24,000-episode matched-candidate benchmark under a common 40,000-token ceiling, a first-candidate baseline achieved 67.42% hard-goal success, a candidate-set local control achieved 96.53%, and GEC achieved 96.57%. The candidate-set control shows that access to multiple candidate actions explains most of the success gain; relative to that control, GEC preserved success while reducing mean token use from 19,782 to 12,574 (36.4%) and restricted mean tokens to completion at the 40,000-token ceiling from 16,136 to 13,114 (18.7%), while eliminating measured pre-completion drift and sharply reducing gross complexity. Governance-overhead sensitivity remained favorable through an additional 500 synthetic governance tokens per cycle. These mechanistic simulations support explicit governance of scope, evidence, resource use, and stopping, while live-model validation remains necessary.

</details>


### [168] [The Price of Thought: Does Test-Time Reasoning Pay in LLM Trading?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30705)
*Jiayi Chen,Guiling Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过受控实证评估发现，在所测试的模型、输入条件与股票组合设定下，增加推理力度并未稳定提升扣除交易成本后的投资回报；推理效果可能非单调，重复生成也会造成决策不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的推理能力通常被认为有助于提升决策质量，但推理会增加计算成本，而且输出变化未必能转化为扣除交易成本后的经济收益。作者指出，推理控制很少被当作经济干预进行严格评估，因此需要检验其对实际投资组合结果的影响。

Method: 对DeepSeek、GPT和Gemini系列代表模型开展受控研究，在固定各组合形成日可用信息、提示词、输出格式及组合构建方式的前提下，改变推理力度。评估覆盖美国股票市场完整一年，设置数值信息、可识别新闻和屏蔽新闻三类输入，分析超过80万项资产预测，并进行重复模型生成。对DeepSeek还检验从无推理到最高推理力度的完整变化过程。

Result: 三个模型系列中，增加推理力度均未可靠改善扣除交易成本后的组合收益。DeepSeek的表现随推理力度变化呈非单调特征。重复生成会导致干预效果和组合选择不稳定，即使整体评分相近也是如此。

Conclusion: 推理力度会改变模型的金融决策，但这种变化不必然创造经济价值。部署推理控制前，应针对具体任务和交易环境进行验证，而不能仅凭推理能力或模型评分推断其投资效益。

Abstract: While inference-time reasoning in large language models (LLMs) promises better decision making, its higher computational cost may not yield better economic outcomes. Yet reasoning controls are rarely evaluated as economic interventions, where changes in model outputs must translate into better portfolios after trading costs. We conduct a controlled study of representative LLMs from the DeepSeek, GPT, and Gemini families. We vary reasoning effort while holding information available at each formation date, prompts, output formats, and portfolio construction fixed. Our evaluation covers a full year of U.S. equities under three input conditions: numerical, identifiable news, and masked news. It includes more than 800,000 asset predictions and repeated model generations. Across all three model families, additional reasoning does not produce a reliable improvement in net portfolio returns. For DeepSeek, where we examine the full progression from no reasoning to maximum reasoning, performance is nonmonotonic. Repeated generations also produce unstable treatment effects and portfolio selections, even when overall scores remain similar. These findings show that additional reasoning can change financial decisions without reliably improving their economic value, motivating validation for each task before deployment.

</details>


### [169] [LAVOIR: Teaching a Single-Pass Decision Encoder When and What to Ask with Amortized Value of Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30706)
*Furkan Yilmaz,Habibe Aleyna Tasdemir,Muhammed Faruk Gozay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LAVOIR在单次前向推理中同时预测答案和询问各候选信息槽的预期决策收益；实验显示其能以较少提问提升准确率，并在若干基准上优于基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单次前向的决策模型虽能回答文本中的类型化问题并给出校准概率，但无法识别并询问缺失信息，遇到关键条件未说明时只能猜测。

Method: 将候选缺失信息槽与答案选项一同输入模型，使其一次输出决策分布及询问每个槽所带来的正确决策概率预期增益（VOI）。训练目标通过模式规则生成金标准决策、LLM生成文本、跨模型校验文本，并将同一消息配对多个用户画像以回归实际收益；另以Gini不纯度上限约束预测收益。

Result: 已见模式上，决策表现与贝叶斯上限无显著差异；提问策略AUC为0.799，接近贪心VOI oracle的0.797。每段对话平均最多提问0.5次时，准确率较从不提问高14.1个百分点。ABCD真实对话中，实际提问后准确率提高8.3个百分点，不提问时准确率不变；在SGD上，收益上限使提问率从93%降至8.6%。十二项基准中有七项高于Laya报告分数；GH200上回答延迟中位数为31毫秒。

Conclusion: LAVOIR将决策与主动信息获取结合，在低提问预算下有效提升准确率，并通过收益上限抑制不必要提问；结果支持其在对话决策中的实用性，但部分结论限于已见模式及所报告数据集。

Abstract: "System One" decision models such as TypeSafe's Jev and its open counterpart Laya answer typed questions about a text in a single forward pass with calibrated probabilities, but they cannot ask for missing information: when a first message does not say what separates two departments, they guess. We present LAVOIR (Laya with Value-Of-Information Routing), which places the candidate pieces of missing information (slots) in the input next to the answer options, so that one forward pass returns both the decision distribution and, for every slot, the expected gain in the probability of the correct decision if the user were asked about it. VOI targets need no human labels: gold decisions come from schema rules, an LLM only verbalizes messages and answers, a model from another family checks every text, and pairing each message with several profiles makes regression on realized gains estimate the expected gain. A Gini-impurity cap bounds the predicted value by what a calibrated model can still gain. In a controlled study, decisions on seen schemas are statistically indistinguishable from the Bayes ceiling. The final model's question policy matches a greedy oracle VOI policy on seen schemas (AUC 0.799 vs. 0.797), and with at most 0.5 questions per conversation it is 14.1 points more accurate than never asking. On real ABCD conversations, one real exchange raises accuracy by 8.3 points where LAVOIR asks and leaves it unchanged where it does not; on SGD the cap lowers the asking rate from 93% to 8.6%. On Laya's twelve benchmarks LAVOIR is above Laya's reported scores on seven, and it answers a question in 31 ms (median, GH200).

</details>


### [170] [CRC-Router: Risk-Constrained Routing for Medical Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30714)
*Xueyang Li,Mingze Jiang,Gelei Xu,Jun Xia,Ching-Hao Chiu,Mengzhao Jia,Danny Z. Chen,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CRC-Router：一种风险约束、具备不确定性感知能力的路由模块，可在模型自主处理与转交人工复核之间进行选择；在胸部 X 光多发现分诊实验中，其风险—覆盖率权衡优于所比较的基线，并能接入 MedRAX agent。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗影像中的智能体系统有望提升处理效率、减轻临床人员负担，但自主判断错误可能传递至后续临床决策。因此，系统除需具备预测能力外，还需可靠判断何时可以自主接受结果、何时应升级复核。

Method: CRC-Router 融合多种互补的不确定性信号与预测分数，构建逐发现（per-finding）的路由特征；再通过轻量级逐发现风险模型估计错误接受风险，并利用 Conformal Risk Control（CRC）在用户指定风险目标下校准接受阈值。该模块旨在兼容传统预测模型及智能体医疗 AI 系统。

Result: 在 NIH ChestX-ray14 数据集的胸部 X 光多发现分诊实验中，CRC-Router 在所评估基线中取得最优的经验风险—覆盖率权衡；无论作为独立路由层，还是作为插件集成到 MedRAX agent，均表现有效。

Conclusion: 结果支持 CRC-Router 用于选择性医疗自动化，并显示其具有模块化、模型无关的兼容性。不过，摘要仅报告特定数据集与任务上的实验结论，尚不足以单独证明其在其他临床场景中的泛化能力或实际部署安全性。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are increasingly being explored in medical imaging to improve throughput and reduce clinician workload; however, safe deployment remains challenging because autonomous errors may propagate into downstream clinical decisions. A central requirement is therefore not only strong predictive performance, but also a reliable routing mechanism that determines when the system should proceed autonomously and when a case should be escalated for further review. To address this gap, we propose CRC-Router, a risk-constrained, uncertainty-aware routing module that is applicable to both conventional medical prediction models and agentic medical AI systems. CRC-Router combines multiple complementary uncertainty signals with the predictive score to construct a per-finding routing feature vector, maps this vector to an estimated wrong-accept risk using a lightweight per-finding risk model, and then applies Conformal Risk Control (CRC) to calibrate acceptance thresholds under a user-specified risk target. Instantiated on chest X-ray multi-finding triage using the NIH ChestX-ray14 dataset, CRC-Router achieves the strongest empirical risk--coverage trade-off among the evaluated baselines, both as a standalone routing layer and as a plug-in module integrated with the state-of-the-art MedRAX agent. These results demonstrate both the effectiveness of CRC-Router in selective medical automation and its modular, model-agnostic compatibility with existing predictive and agentic medical pipelines. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/XLIAaron/CRC-Router

</details>


### [171] [Analyzing and Mitigating Cost-Inefficient Behaviors in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30725)
*Yiran Hu,Nan Jiang,Shanchao Liang,Anik Dey,Yi Wu,Lin Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究编码智能体的成本低效行为及缓解方法。三类行为普遍存在，最高占任务成本的22.75%；开发者设计的技能最有效，最高可降低成本41.73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体虽然能有效完成任务，但可能因反复检索、重复生成脚本和重复运行测试而产生显著费用。既有研究对这些持续性的成本低效行为缺乏系统分析，因此论文旨在识别其类型、影响范围并评估缓解方案。

Method: 作者分析 Claude Code 与 Mini-SWE-Agent 在 SWE-bench Verified 上四种配置下的1,200条轨迹，归纳三类成本低效行为；随后在留出的 SWE-bench Verified 与 Pro 任务上，基于超过10,000条轨迹评估结构感知检索、智能体合成技能和开发者设计技能三种策略。

Result: 三类低效行为影响79%–98%的编码任务，最多占任务成本的22.75%。结构感知检索可能引入额外开销并改变智能体委派方式，导致效果不稳定，成本最高增加28.14%。智能体合成的技能往往过于底层且依赖具体轨迹，泛化和效果受限。开发者设计的技能提供较高层次、与具体轨迹无关的指导，最高降低成本41.73%，约为智能体合成技能最高收益的两倍。

Conclusion: 编码智能体的成本低效行为普遍且影响显著，简单增加检索结构或让智能体自行总结技能并不一定有效。由开发者设计具有通用性的高层技能，是本文评估中最有效的降本策略；其实际收益仍应结合任务类型与系统配置评估。

Abstract: Although effective, coding agents often incur substantial monetary costs. Their recurring cost-inefficient behaviors remain underexplored. We conduct the first study of behavioral cost inefficiencies in coding agents, analyzing 1,200 trajectories from Claude Code and Mini-SWE-Agent across four configurations on SWE-bench Verified. We identify three cost-inefficient behaviors: subsumed retrieval, similar script generation, and test re-execution. We then evaluate three mitigation strategies: structure-aware retrieval, agent-synthesized skills, and developer-designed skills, over 10k trajectories on held-out SWE-bench Verified and Pro tasks.
