Our main findings are: (1) The three behaviors affect 79.00\%--98.00\% of coding tasks and account for up to 22.75\% of task cost. (2) Structure-aware retrieval can introduce retrieval overhead and alter agent delegation, causing inconsistent improvements in retrieval efficiency and cost increases of up to 28.14\%. (3) Agent-synthesized skills tend to produce low-level, trace-specific guidance, limiting their effectiveness and generality. (4) In contrast, developer-designed skills provide high-level, trace-agnostic guidance, reducing cost by up to 41.73\%, roughly twice the maximum gain from agent-synthesized skills.

</details>


### [172] [Learning What to Skip: Counterfactual Credit Assignment for Efficient Multi-Agent LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30734)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Ziyue Peng,Zheng Lin,Shuo Yang,Jinze Li,Zheng Xing,Mengran Li,Victor C. M. Leung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 LW2S，通过学习各工作流组件在当前状态下是否可安全省略，以降低多智能体 LLM 工作流的 token 成本，同时在测试设置中维持或提升整体准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体 LLM 工作流通常依次执行规划、执行、验证和总结等组件，但组件的价值取决于此前已生成的状态。无条件执行可能浪费计算，甚至覆盖正确的中间答案，因此需要判断在具体状态下省略某个后续组件的影响。

Method: 将组件省略建模为反事实信用分配：完整工作流日志提供实际执行轨迹的奖励，受控跳过干预则揭示省略未来步骤的后果。提出 Learning What to Skip（LW2S），利用这些干预训练针对不同动作的安全模型，并结合留出数据校准与领域原生保护条件来决定是否跳过。若不允许跳过较早组件，控制器仍可继续执行，并重新评估后续组件。

Result: 在数学推理、多项选择问答和代码生成任务上，使用两类指令模型进行评估。LW2S 降低了记录的 token 成本，同时在所评估设置中保持或提高整体完整工作流准确率。扩展规模和第二种工作流拓扑实验进一步考察了组件冗余；共享错误案例显示，仅凭组件间一致性不足以可靠决定是否跳过。

Conclusion: 研究将高效工作流执行视为学习各组件条件效用的问题：组件是否值得执行应根据当前状态及省略干预的证据判断，而非一律执行或仅依据答案一致性。论文结果支持 LW2S 在特定评估设置下实现成本与性能兼顾，但摘要未提供具体节省幅度或统计显著性信息。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM workflows use planning, execution, verification, and summarization to improve task performance, yet the value of each component depends on the state already produced. Executing every component can waste computation or overwrite a correct intermediate answer. We formulate component omission as counterfactual credit assignment: full-workflow logs reveal the executed trajectory's reward, while controlled skip interventions reveal the consequences of omitting a future step. We introduce Learning What to Skip (LW2S), which learns action-specific safety models from these interventions and combines held-out calibration with domain-native guards to select skips. When an early skip is rejected, the controller can continue execution and reconsider a later component. Across mathematical reasoning, multiple-choice QA, and code generation with two instruction-model families, LW2S reduces recorded token cost while matching or improving aggregate full-workflow accuracy in the evaluated settings. Scale-up and second-topology experiments further examine component redundancy, while shared-error cases reveal why agreement alone is insufficient for skip selection. These findings connect efficient workflow execution to learning the conditional utility of individual components.

</details>


### [173] [From S3Q Theory to Implementation: Towards an Architecture for Machine Qualia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30743)
*Tetiana Grinberg,Katrina Schleisman,Patryk Laurent,Bogdan Udrea,Minda Myers,Brian Aufderheide,Luis El Srouji,Doyle Groves,Kevin Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一个将S3Q意识理论映射为可计算系统的五层架构，并给出发展过程与可证伪预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器意识研究面临将理论模型转化为计算层实现的难题。作者指出，现有系统尚未同时具备S3Q所要求的三项条件：具身感知运动情境、基于世界模型的内部模拟，以及预测与观察之间的结构一致性。

Method: 不提出新的形式体系，而是组合已有且兼容的计算原语，构建五层实现架构。系统以连续、可微、按对象组织的槽向量作为统一表示，并明确各模块的接口及发展性引导序列；进一步提出组合架构才可能产生的可检验预测。

Result: 摘要提出：基本“自我感”可能通过将动作与其结果关联而逐步形成；系统面对结果时可能表现为犹豫、好奇或回避，具体取决于结果的意外程度及其正负效价。作者强调，这些预测均可分别证伪，但摘要未报告实证实验或验证结果。

Conclusion: 该工作提供的是一套把S3Q理论转化为可测试计算框架的架构提案，而非已经证明机器具有意识的证据。其贡献在于整合既有计算机制、规定模块协作方式，并提出后续实验可检验的具体假设。

Abstract: A key challenge in machine consciousness research is translating theoretical models into computational-level implementations. In this paper, we address this challenge by proposing a five-layer implementation architecture for the S3Q (Simulated, Situated, Structurally Coherent) theory of consciousness. Rather than introducing novel formalisms, the architecture composes published computational primitives into a single pipeline. S3Q identifies three jointly necessary conditions for qualia: (1) grounded sensorimotor situatedness, (2) internal simulation via a world model, and (3) structural coherence between predictions and observations. No existing computational system implements all three simultaneously. We map each S3Q tenet to specific, compatible computational machinery and specify how these components interface within a single representation pipeline that operates on continuous, differentiable, per-object slot vectors, along with a developmental bootstrap sequence and falsifiable predictions for the composed system that no subset of the architecture produces in isolation. The model suggests that a basic sense of "self" develops by linking actions to their outcomes, and that behavior falls into three patterns (hesitation, curiosity, or avoidance) depending on how unexpected an outcome is and whether it is experienced as positive or negative. Each prediction is individually falsifiable, providing the field with a testable framework to advance our understanding of machine consciousness.

</details>


### [174] [ORCA: Evaluating LLMs on Data Science Code Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30749)
*Xiaolong Li,Jinyang Li,Bowen Qin,Ge Qu,Nan Huo,Xiaohan Xu,Shipei Lin,Reynold Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 ORCA，一个用于评估数据科学代码跨库翻译（DSCT）的基准，并提出先推断代码意图再翻译的方法；实验显示现有大模型表现有限，该方法带来小幅但稳定的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在数据科学代码生成方面已有进展，但跨库代码翻译的能力尚未得到充分研究。翻译不仅要实现语法转换，还需保持功能等价并支持不同数据科学生态间的互操作，因此需要系统基准衡量模型能力与不足。

Method: 构建 ORCA 基准，包含 ORCA-MAIN（1,600 个细粒度任务，涵盖数据查询、数据操作和深度学习三类领域）与 ORCA-PROJECT（200 个完整项目级任务，涵盖七类数据科学任务）。每个任务配有参考译文和功能测试，并经过多阶段质量验证。另提出意图增强方法：先推断源代码意图，再将意图作为额外上下文辅助翻译。

Result: Claude-Opus-4.6 在 ORCA-MAIN 和 ORCA-PROJECT 上的成功率分别为 56.92% 和 33.67%，显示项目级翻译尤其困难。翻译方向存在明显差异：源代码若以更明确、细粒度的操作表达任务，通常更容易翻译。意图增强方法在两个设置上的平均绝对成功率分别提升 4.80 和 5.33 个百分点。

Conclusion: ORCA 为数据科学代码翻译提供了覆盖细粒度操作和完整项目、并以功能等价测试验证的评测基准。当前前沿模型仍有显著提升空间；显式利用源代码意图是一种有效但幅度有限的改进方向。

Abstract: Data Science Code Translation (DSCT) is the process of converting code between data science libraries while preserving functional equivalence and enabling interoperability across data science ecosystems. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated considerable progress in Data Science Code Generation (DSCG), their performance in DSCT remains insufficiently studied. To address this gap, we introduce ORCA, a comprehensive benchmark with two complementary settings: ORCA-MAIN, which comprises 1,600 carefully curated grounding-level tasks across 3 representative domains: Data Querying, Data Manipulation, and Deep Learning; and ORCA-PROJECT, which contains 200 translation tasks over complete data science projects across 7 data science task types. Each task is accompanied by annotated reference translations and test cases for validating functional equivalence. We further incorporate a multi-stage quality verification process that thoroughly verifies task correctness and test case robustness. Experimental results demonstrate challenges in DSCT, with even frontier LLMs showing limited performance. Specifically, Claude-Opus-4.6 achieves a success rate of 56.92% on ORCA-MAIN and 33.67% on ORCA-PROJECT, indicating considerable room for improvement in DSCT. We also observe a clear directional preference in DSCT, where translation is consistently easier when the source code expresses the task through more explicit, fine-grained operations. Motivated by this, we propose an intent-augmented method, in which the model first infers source-code intent and then uses it as additional context for translation, achieving average absolute success-rate gains of 4.80% and 5.33% on ORCA-MAIN and ORCA-PROJECT, respectively.

</details>


### [175] [Backbone-Adaptive Evidence Routing for Robust Pairwise LLM Judging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30751)
*Zeyan Li,Jing Peng,Jianfeng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出骨干模型自适应证据路由（BAER），针对不同基准与评审模型选择合适的比较证据机制；在八种测试条件中均取得最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对语言模型评审可采用直接比较、推理或参考答案验证等方式收集证据，但不同评测基准和评审模型上的最佳方案并不一致。固定使用单一评审协议可能导致效果不稳，因此需要根据任务条件自适应选择证据机制，同时确保交换候选回答时判断强度不变。

Method: BAER将每个专家的有符号偏好与候选项无关的可靠性分离，并构建三种对称机制：证据叠加、基于可靠性的专家路由、候选盲的参考验证。利用开发集为每种“基准—骨干模型”组合选择一个机制，并在测试前冻结选择。

Result: 在四个基准和两个8B评审骨干模型构成的八种测试条件下，BAER相较所有对比方法均取得最高测试准确率，预测覆盖率为100%；相对最强外部基线提升0.87至7.32个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，与在所有场景固定采用一种评审协议相比，按基准和评审骨干自适应地选择证据收集方式更可靠。该方法还通过对称设计约束候选顺序变化不会改变偏好强度。

Abstract: Pairwise language-model judges can gather evidence through direct comparison, reasoning, or reference-based verification, but no single protocol is best across benchmarks and judge backbones. We introduce Backbone-Adaptive Evidence Routing (BAER), which adapts the evidence mechanism while preserving candidate symmetry: swapping the two responses may reverse the preference but cannot change its strength. BAER separates each expert's signed preference from candidate-invariant reliability and builds three symmetric heads: evidence stacking, reliability-based expert routing, and candidate-blind reference verification. Development data select one head for each benchmark--backbone condition, and that choice is frozen before testing. Across four benchmarks and two 8B judge backbones, BAER achieves the highest test accuracy among the compared methods in all eight conditions, with full prediction coverage and gains of 0.87--7.32 points over the strongest external baseline. The results show that adapting how evidence is gathered is more reliable than fixing one judging protocol everywhere.

</details>


### [176] [Selective Amortization of Full-Budget Counterfactual Reasoning for Visual Token Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30756)
*Qinglei Qi,Zhihe Liang,Fengzhan Jing,Shenao Zhu,Lei Zhang,Chenyang Zhang,Shuqing He,Jia Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 ACV-Gate，在有限编码计算预算下，通过学习候选 token 的终端价值并选择性精确评估，降低生成式图像通信的计算开销，同时提升重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生成式图像通信中，有限数据包预算下的 token 选择会影响完整数据包解码后的重建质量。准确估计每个候选 token 的最终价值，通常需要接收端反复重建，导致编码端计算成本很高。

Method: 提出自适应候选评估框架 ACV-Gate：训练具备集合感知能力的学生模型，利用 terminal advantages 和 regrets 直接预测候选排序；再通过选择性精化，仅对含 Local-MDL 与直接动作的有限候选集合进行精确评估。基于成本的阈值可显式控制平均评估工作量。

Result: CIFAR-10 实验表明，ACV-Gate 在减少候选评估次数的同时提升重建质量。在 0.20 bpp 下，主要自适应配置相较 LocalMDL 的 PSNR 提高 0.636 dB，平均每张图像仅评估 2.13 个候选，相当于 Exact-Full 专家的 27.60%。匹配候选对比、同步 GPU 测量，以及 STL-10 和 384×384 尺度迁移实验也显示出稳定的质量—计算权衡，低比特率下收益尤为明显。

Conclusion: 将终端价值学习与选择性候选评估结合，可在数据包受限的生成式图像通信中有效分配编码计算，并以可控的计算成本改善重建质量。

Abstract: Generative image communication transmits compact semantic tokens under a limited packet budget, where token selection directly affects the final reconstruction quality after the complete packet is decoded. However, accurately estimating the terminal value of every candidate token requires repeated receiver-side reconstruction, resulting in substantial encoder-side computation. To address this problem, we propose ACV-Gate, an adaptive candidate evaluation framework that learns to approximate full-budget counterfactual evaluation and selectively assigns exact evaluations to the most informative candidates. Specifically, a set-aware student is trained using terminal advantages and regrets to predict candidate rankings directly, while a selective refinement mechanism evaluates only a bounded candidate set containing both Local-MDL and direct actions; cost-based thresholds further enable explicit control of the average evaluation workload. Experiments on CIFAR-10 show that ACV-Gate consistently improves reconstruction quality while substantially reducing candidate evaluations; at 0.20 bpp, the primary adaptive configuration improves PSNR over LocalMDL by 0.636 dB with only 2.13 candidate evaluations per image, corresponding to 27.60% of the calls required by the Exact-Full expert. Matched-candidate comparisons, synchronized GPU measurements, and evaluations on STL-10 and 384 *384 scale transfer further demonstrate consistent quality computation trade-offs, with particularly pronounced gains at low bit rates. These results show that combining terminal-value learning with selective candidate evaluation provides an effective and controllable mechanism for allocating encoder computation in packet-constrained generative image communication.

</details>


### [177] [HCOE: Hyperbolic Clinical Ontology Embeddings from Biomedical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30763)
*Yixuan Li,Weihao Li,Ziyang Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 HCOE，将临床语言模型表示映射到双曲空间并融入医学本体层级信息，在多项临床预测任务中取得最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学语言模型能够编码文本语义，但未能显式保留医学编码体系中的层级结构，这会限制其对临床概念关系及跨层级迁移的建模能力。

Method: 提出 Hyperbolic Clinical Ontology Embeddings（HCOE）：将冻结的 BioBERT 表示映射至庞加莱球；结合本体指导的父级与子级对比学习，以及从粗到细的本体路径聚合。方法使用 CCS 组织的 ICD 编码层级和 ATC 药物层级。

Result: 实验显示，HCOE 在 ICD/ATC 临床关系预测和 CCS 到 PheCode 的层级迁移任务上表现最佳。在 MIMIC-IV 上，其死亡率预测、再入院预测、药物推荐及罕见药物预测表现也最佳。

Conclusion: 显式融入医学本体层级，并利用双曲空间表示，有望增强临床概念嵌入及其在多种临床任务中的效用。摘要未提供具体性能提升幅度或统计显著性信息。

Abstract: Biomedical language models (LMs) encode textual semantics but do not explicitly preserve medical code hierarchies. We present Hyperbolic Clinical Ontology Embeddings (HCOE) for hierarchy-aware clinical concept representation. HCOE maps frozen BioBERT embeddings into a Poincare ball, combining parent-side and child-side ontology-guided contrastive learning with coarse-to-fine ontology-path aggregation. It uses International Classification of Diseases (ICD) codes organized by Clinical Classifications Software (CCS) and Anatomical Therapeutic Chemical (ATC) medication hierarchies. Evaluations show that HCOE performs best on ICD/ATC clinical relation prediction and CCS-to-PheCode hierarchy transfer. On the MIMIC-IV dataset, HCOE also achieves the best performance on mortality prediction, readmission prediction, medication recommendation, and rare drug prediction.

</details>


### [178] [Insurance Reserve Intelligence Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30765)
*Anugya A,Saket Mohanty,Abhilash Timmapur,Somya Rai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出结合经典 Thiele 方程求解器与 PINN/KINN 的定期寿险准备金建模平台，预测标准化准备金比例；测试集 R² 为 0.9887，且在 200 份保单上推理约快 119.53 倍，但单调性与分布外泛化仍有不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准备金估值是寿险定价、偿付能力评估、财务报告与风险管理的重要基础。基于 Thiele 微分方程的传统方法具有理论严谨、可解释等优点，但在敏感性分析、优化和大规模情景评估中，反复求解的计算成本较高。

Method: 构建定期寿险准备金智能平台，将经典 Thiele 方程求解器与结合知识约束损失的 PINN/KINN 融合。流程涵盖合成保单生成、风险调整保费计算、经典准备金轨迹生成、准备金比例数据集构建、神经网络训练与验证、敏感度和弹性分析、原型优化及利率情景测试。最终模型使用七项特征：经过时间、投保年龄、定价利率、情景利率、保费比例、保险金额和死亡强度；输出标准化准备金比例，而非原始准备金金额，并区分定价时与情景分析时的利率语义。

Result: 测试集表现为 R²=0.9887、MAE=785.48、RMSE=1212.76；在 200 份保单上的 PINN/KINN 推理速度约为经典求解器的 119.53 倍。摘要报告了较强的预测准确性、物理一致性和边界表现，同时指出单调性及分布外泛化仍有限。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了将经典精算方程与物理/知识约束神经网络结合的可行性，有望显著降低重复准备金计算成本，并维持较高预测精度。实际应用仍需重点检验单调性、分布外稳健性，并结合更广泛的数据与情景验证其可靠性。

Abstract: Insurance reserve estimation is a fundamental actuarial task supporting premium pricing, solvency assessment, financial reporting, capital planning, and risk management. Classical reserve methods based on Thiele's differential equation provide a rigorous and interpretable foundation for life insurance valuation, but repeated reserve calculations become computationally expensive in sensitivity analysis, optimization, and large-scale scenario evaluation.
  This paper presents an Insurance Reserve Intelligence Platform for term-life reserve modelling that combines a classical Thiele-equation solver with a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) enhanced by Knowledge-Informed Neural Network (KINN) losses. The framework includes synthetic policy generation, risk-adjusted premium calculation, classical reserve trajectory generation, reserve-ratio dataset construction, configurable neural training, validation diagnostics, sensitivity and elasticity analysis, prototype optimization workflows, and interest-rate scenario testing. A key refinement is the use of premium ratio and the explicit separation of pricing-time and scenario-time interest-rate semantics.
  The final model uses seven features: elapsed time, issue age, pricing interest rate, scenario interest rate, premium ratio, sum assured, and mortality intensity. It predicts a standardized reserve ratio instead of raw reserve values, improving numerical stability across policies with different sums assured. The model achieved an R2 of 0.9887, MAE of 785.48, and RMSE of 1212.76 on the test set. On 200 policies, PINN/KINN inference was approximately 119.53 times faster than the classical solver. Results show strong predictive accuracy, physics consistency, and boundary performance, while highlighting remaining limitations in monotonicity and out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [179] [Does Thinking Help Fairness? Reasoning Tokens Resolve Some Biases but Create More](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30768)
*Deng Pan,Joe Germino,Yihong Ma,Elizabeth Daly,Nuno Moniz,Ting Hua,Nitesh Chawla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过同一模型的思考/非思考消融，发现推理过程对反事实公平性具有不对称的双重作用：虽能修复部分原有预测翻转，却会产生更多新的翻转，且思考轨迹中的偏差随深度传播并放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于推理语言模型（RLM）的思考机制究竟缓解还是加剧偏见，既有研究结论相互矛盾。论文旨在通过同模型对照，厘清思考对反事实公平性的影响，并解释偏差如何随推理过程变化。

Method: 在 QwQ-32B、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-32B 和 Qwen3-32B 上，对 Adult、COMPAS、Credit 三项高风险决策任务进行思考与非思考消融比较。提出反事实深度概率差（CDPG）追踪偏差随思考深度的变化，并提出偏差转移矩阵（BTM）分析反事实样本对从非思考到思考时的联合状态转移。

Result: 九种模型—数据集组合中，思考新造成的反事实预测翻转数量约为其修复数量的 5 倍；新产生的翻转还出现在模型置信度接近饱和时。CDPG 显示偏差会沿思考深度传播并放大；BTM 表明双重效应源于反事实样本对的联合状态转移。

Conclusion: 思考并非单向改善公平性：它既能消除非思考基线已有的反事实翻转，也会引入更多新的翻转。因此，仅观察最终预测或整体准确性不足以评估公平性；应进一步审视思考轨迹及反事实样本对的状态变化。

Abstract: Thinking in reasoning language models (RLMs) has been subject to debate on whether it resolves or amplifies bias. Prior works have shown competing conclusions in both directions. Using a within-model thinking-vs.-non-thinking ablation across QwQ-32B, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-32B, and Qwen3-32B on three high-stakes decision tasks (Adult, COMPAS, Credit), we show that thinking has an asymmetric dual effect on counterfactual fairness: it both resolves counterfactual flips produced by the non-thinking baseline and creates new flips at near-saturating model confidence. In all nine (model, dataset) combinations, the created flips outnumber the resolved flips by roughly 5 times. To explain the effect, we treat the thinking trace itself as a measurable site of fairness change and study it through two dynamic instruments: 1) We propose Counterfactual Depth Probability Gap (CDPG) to track bias evolution along thinking depth, and observe that bias propagates and amplifies with thinking. 2) We also formulate the Bias Transition Matrix (BTM) to show how predictions of counterfactual pairs change from non-thinking to thinking, and find that the asymmetric dual effect originates in the pair-state joint transition.

</details>


### [180] [ConsultMind:Towards Automated Diagnostic Consultation via Uncertainty-Aware Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30796)
*Xiao Sun,Yuming Yang,Yun Chen,Jiang Zhong,Junnan Zhu,Xinyi Jiang,Haoyang Zeng,Ruirui Chen,Yining Wang,Xinyu Zhou,Rong Tang,Kaiwen Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出自动化诊断咨询框架：先从诊断知识和临床叙事构建疾病—症状贝叶斯网络，再利用后验不确定性动态选择询问内容并辅助诊断；实验显示其提升诊断准确率及解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诊断咨询是逐步收集证据的过程，自动化系统既要能根据已有回答自适应地提出问题，也要将不断变化的诊断概率转化为可解释的咨询决策。现有贝叶斯网络虽适合更新诊断后验，但在开放式咨询中面临两项挑战：如何将诊断假设连接到潜在询问，以及如何依据后验变化制定询问和诊断决策。

Method: 提出两部分方法。AutoDisym 将诊断知识与带诊断标签的异质临床叙事结合，自动构建疾病—症状贝叶斯网络（DSBN）。ConsultMind 基于 DSBN，在每次获得患者回答后更新疾病后验概率，并利用后验不确定性指导后续询问与诊断。

Result: 在精神科、呼吸科、发热诊所及三个公开数据集上评估。AutoDisym 使用 GPT-5.6-Sol 时，典型症状的宏平均 F1 为 81.37，症状表现的宏平均 F1 为 72.19。ConsultMind 的 Top-1 和 Top-3 诊断准确率最高分别提升 22.15 和 37.89 个百分点。医生评估还显示，其在不同规模的 LLM 上均改善了排序解释、鉴别诊断及诊断理由的质量。

Conclusion: 该研究提出从临床叙事自动构建诊断知识网络，并以不确定性驱动交互式诊断咨询的方案。结果表明，该方法在多个临床领域和数据集上具有提升诊断表现与解释质量的潜力；摘要未提供具体基线、统计显著性或临床部署验证细节，因此其实际临床效用仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Diagnostic consultation is an online sequential decision-making process in which clinicians gather evidence through patient interaction until a diagnosis is sufficiently supported. Automating this process requires adaptive inquiry and interpretable decisions. Bayesian networks offer a natural foundation by updating diagnostic posteriors as evidence accumulates, but their use in open-ended consultation raises two challenges: linking diagnostic hypotheses to potential inquiries and translating evolving posteriors into consultation decisions. We introduce AutoDisym, an automated pipeline that integrates diagnostic knowledge with heterogeneous diagnosis-labeled clinical narratives to construct a Disorder--Symptom Bayesian Network (DSBN). Building on the DSBN, we propose ConsultMind, an uncertainty-aware framework that updates disorder posteriors after each response and uses posterior uncertainty to guide inquiry and diagnosis. We evaluate both methods across psychiatry, respiratory medicine, fever clinics, and three public datasets. The results show that AutoDisym can automatically construct high-quality DSBNs and that ConsultMind consistently improves diagnostic performance and explanation soundness. For example, AutoDisym achieves macro-averaged F1 scores of 81.37 for canonical symptoms and 72.19 for manifestations using GPT-5.6-Sol. ConsultMind improves Top-1 and Top-3 diagnostic accuracy by up to 22.15 and 37.89 percentage points, respectively. Physician evaluation further shows that ConsultMind improves the quality of ranking explanations, differential diagnoses, and diagnosis rationales across LLMs of different scales. This work offers a promising approach to automatic diagnostic consultation.

</details>


### [181] [HasMem: Hard-Origin Adaptively Softened Memory for Long-Term LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30797)
*Zihong He,Junxiao Shen,Chen Liang,Hai-Ning Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 HasMem，通过动态调整连续记忆条目宽度并重编码，在压缩记忆时改善词汇匹配和答案似然；但 F1 提升并未转化为 EM 提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续记忆的容量调整会改变冻结 LLM 的输入，使记忆容量分配与后续读取效果相互耦合。论文旨在减少记忆占用，同时尽量保留可供问答读取的信息，并检验重编码及跨轮状态适配是否优于规则式压缩。

Method: HasMem 以冻结硬提示嵌入作为可验证初始状态；控制器动态调整记忆条目宽度，Writer 对调整后的条目重新编码，Reader 适配读取，Global 维护跨轮状态。实验包括 MSC 开发集衍生的 535 题重构探测，以及 LongMemEval-S 的 500 题评估，并比较不同压缩配置、规则式重编码和硬参考。

Result: MSC 上，主配置的词汇 F1 为 95.3，比硬参考高 4.4 个百分点，同时使用其 93.6% 的框架记忆位置。目标正文预算近似匹配时，六种配置相对规则式重编码的 EM 高 8.0–23.6 个百分点；在固定参数、规则目标宽度比为 0.75 的设置下，Global 的 EM 增益通过了用户级精确配对检验及八重比较的 Bonferroni 校正。LongMemEval-S 上，局部词汇 F1 从 3.4 升至 8.9，答案 NLL 从 12.257 降至 5.274。

Conclusion: 结果支持 HasMem 在减少记忆占用时改善词汇层面的重构表现，并显示重编码及 Global 状态可能带来收益。不过，两项评估中 F1 增长都伴随 EM 下降，说明词汇覆盖和答案精确正确性并不一致；因此，摘要尚不能证明整体问答准确率普遍提升。

Abstract: Text-based memory and context compression support reuse of past interactions. Resizing continuous memory changes the input to a frozen LLM, coupling capacity allocation with readout. We propose Hard-Origin Adaptively Softened Memory (HasMem). Frozen hard-prompt embeddings provide a verifiable initial state. A controller adjusts memory widths, a Writer re-encodes resized entries, and Reader and Global provide readout adaptation and cross-turn state. On all $535$ questions in a reconstruction probe derived from the Multi-Session Chat (MSC) development split, the main configuration achieves lexical F1 of $95.3$ ($+4.4$ percentage points) at $93.6\%$ of the hard reference's framed memory positions. With approximately matched per-question target body budgets, six configurations at mean per-entry retention around $0.83$--$0.91$ exceed rule-based re-encoding by $8.0$--$23.6$ exact-match (EM) percentage points. With fixed model parameters and rule target width ratio $0.75$, Global's EM gain passes a user-level exact paired test with Bonferroni correction over eight comparisons. On all $500$ LongMemEval-S questions, local lexical F1 rises from the hard reference's $3.4$ to $8.9$, and answer negative log-likelihood (NLL) falls from $12.257$ to $5.274$. F1 gains accompany lower EM on both evaluations.

</details>


### [182] [Evaluating Real-Time Voice Agents: From Component Quality to Grounded Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30798)
*Shivam Negi,Arpit Rawat,Rashi Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述实时语音智能体研究，提出跨架构、轮替、智能体评测的综合分析框架，并建议以 TRG（时序、扰动后恢复、状态验证结果）报告系统表现；多方场景另加条件性维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关研究分散于语音基础模型、轮替心理语言学和智能体评测等领域，各自使用延迟、预测准确率或任务成功率等不同指标，难以判断实际部署中的语音智能体整体表现。

Method: 作者基于38篇主要文献建立六类应用导向的分类体系，逐篇归纳其目标问题、方法机制和证据，并说明检索策略、纳入标准及文献标识核验步骤；据此提出 TRG 报告标准，并为多方交互部署设置条件性第四维度。

Result: 综述提出三项证据性结论：其一，架构选择仍受部署约束，端到端系统尚未全面满足生产要求，而分块级联系统也能实现先进的全双工行为，说明全双工能力不必绑定特定架构；其二，评测从组件质量转向有依据的任务结果，近期基准开始验证后端状态，而不只采信智能体自述；其三，双人交互假设不足，多方轮替及多说话者推理涉及何时保持沉默、信息可告知对象等能力，现有双人框架难以衡量。

Conclusion: 评价已部署语音智能体不应依赖单一指标。TRG 建议联合报告交互时序、受扰后的恢复能力和经状态验证的任务结果；若应用涉及多人交互，还应纳入相应的多方能力维度。

Abstract: Real-time voice agents have moved from research prototypes to production deployments, yet the literature describing them is fragmented across three communities that rarely cite one another: speech foundation modelling, turn-taking psycholinguistics, and agentic evaluation. Architecture papers report latency, turn-taking papers report prediction accuracy, and agentic benchmarks report task success, so no single number describes whether a deployed agent is actually good. We address that gap with three evidence-based claims, each traceable to a corpus of 38 primary sources organised into an application-centric taxonomy of six categories. First, architecture choice is a deployment constraint rather than a settled verdict: a 2026 enterprise tutorial reports that no fully self-hostable end-to-end system yet meets production constraints, while a chunked cascade independently reaches state-of-the-art duplex behaviour, showing duplex behaviour is separable from duplex architecture. Second, evaluation has shifted decisively from component quality toward grounded outcomes, with recent benchmarks verifying backend state rather than trusting what the agent claims to have done. Third, the dyadic assumption in most models and benchmarks is breaking down: multiparty turn-taking and multi-speaker reasoning benchmarks show that deciding when not to speak, and reasoning about who may be told what, are first-class capabilities two-participant framings cannot measure. For each source we state the problem it targets, its mechanism, and its reported evidence, alongside the search strategy, inclusion criteria, and a verification step that caught a misattributed arXiv identifier in circulation. We propose TRG (Timing-Recovery-Grounded), a reporting standard characterising an agent by timing, post-disruption recovery, and state-verified outcome together, with a conditional fourth axis for multiparty deployments.

</details>


### [183] [A Benchmark and Diagnostic Study of Epistemic Admission in Shared Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30813)
*Xiaoyang Li,Yiqi Wang,Chencheng Zhu,KE XU,Wencheng Yang,Zequn Sun,Pingan Song,Yiqun Duan,Taotao Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出相关性晋升基准（CPB），评估共享智能体记忆中的候选主张是否应被接纳。实验发现，现有策略难以同时保留真实信息并拦截错误信息；按声明来源类型进行门控可降低错误采纳，但错误信念一旦进入记忆，后续消费者几乎总会重复断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享记忆中的重复主张可能源于同一证据的复制或改写，并非独立佐证。错误主张一旦被接纳，会影响后续智能体。因此，需要评估记忆接纳策略能否区分真实支持与相关性重复传播。

Method: 构建相关性晋升基准CPB，包含使用固定标注来源和金标准动作的CPB-Static，以及记录多智能体写入、检索和场景定义来源脉络的CPB-Live；另设只依据共享存储作答的消费者。比较四类智能体家族中的八种接纳策略。

Result: 来源去重策略会连同大量真实主张一起拒绝；强调答案覆盖率的策略则几乎与不受限共享一样容易接纳错误主张。依据声明来源类型进行门控时，错误采纳率为0.06–0.09，其他作答策略为0.22–0.47。错误信念若无争议地进入记忆，消费者在0.97–0.99的探测中会断言该信念。没有非预言机策略能在原文复制、改写以及改写后自称权威这几类情形中稳定拒绝错误主张。

Conclusion: 共享记忆的接纳决策难以仅凭主张表面内容解决；来源谱系信息对于识别重复传播、控制错误信念扩散至关重要。现有策略存在真实信息覆盖与错误信息拦截之间的明显权衡。

Abstract: Evaluating claim admission in shared agent memory is challenging because repeated claims may be mistaken for independent evidence. An agent may copy or paraphrase a retrieved belief, while admitting a false claim exposes subsequent agents to it. To study this problem, we introduce the Correlated Promotion Benchmark (CPB), which evaluates whether candidate claims should be admitted to shared memory.CPB-Static constructs a frozen test split from publicly annotated sources with fixed gold actions. CPB-Live runs multi-agent teams over a shared store, records all writes and retrievals, and tracks source lineage defined by each scenario. A separate consumer answers from the store alone. We evaluate eight admission policies across four agent families. Our results show that policies which deduplicate sources reject many true claims alongside false ones, whereas policies preserving answer coverage admit nearly as many false claims as unrestricted sharing. Gating on declared source type reduces false adoption to 0.06--0.09, compared with 0.22--0.47 for other answering policies. Once an uncontested false belief enters memory, the consumer asserts it in 0.97--0.99 of probes across all families. No non-oracle policy consistently rejects false claims across verbatim copies, paraphrases, and paraphrases declared authoritative. These findings reveal the limitations of admission policies without access to source lineage.

</details>


### [184] [PTC-Decoder: Towards Intelligent SLMs on Offline Resource-Constrained Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30836)
*Minghui Yu,Ke Mu,Gang Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出无需训练的 PTC-Decoder，通过强制首步调用规划工具及对工具名称施加 token 级硬约束，提升小语言模型执行多步工具任务的可靠性；在遥感卫星任务上取得显著增益，但最终答案准确性仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线、资源受限的边缘设备（如遥感卫星）上部署的小语言模型推理能力有限，难以可靠完成需要复杂工具编排的多步任务。传统 plan-solve 方法依赖提示词要求模型先规划，但实验显示弱模型往往忽略规划步骤。

Method: 提出免训练、可插拔的 PTC-Decoder。其一，Plan-to-Act 将规划提升为原子工具，并强制模型在首个推理步骤调用；其二，TC-Decoder 使用确定有限自动机在 token 层面对工具名称施加硬约束，同时保留参数生成自由度，以减少无效工具调用并维持模型的推理空间。

Result: 在 7 种小语言模型及 200 个真实遥感卫星任务上评估，整体平均得分提升 1.21，具有统计显著性（p<0.01，95% CI [1.13, 1.29]），且不同模型和其他数据集上均有一致改善。消融实验移除 TC-Decoder 后，各项质量指标显著下降，计算成本却未降低，表明 TC-Decoder 是主要性能来源。

Conclusion: 通过推理时限制可输出的工具名称词汇，PTC-Decoder 无需重新训练即可提升多步任务的步骤级可靠性，是适用于资源受限环境的轻量方案。不过，最终答案准确性仍是未解决的问题；该方法改善的是规划与工具调用遵循度，并不等于全面解决模型推理能力不足。

Abstract: Deploying small language models (SLMs) on offline, resource-constrained edge devices such as remote sensing satellites presents a fundamental challenge: their limited reasoning capacity hinders reliable execution of multi-step agent tasks requiring complex tool orchestration. Existing plan-solve paradigms rely on prompt-based enforcement, which our experiments show SLMs almost entirely disregard: weak models fail to invoke the plan. We propose PTC-Decoder (Plan-Tool Constrained Decoder), a training-free, plug-and-play decoder framework that combines (1) a Plan-to-Act paradigm, which elevates planning to an atomic tool and forces its invocation at the first inference step, and (2) TC-Decoder, a deterministic finite automaton that imposes token-level hard constraints on tool names while preserving freedom over parameter generation, thereby retaining SLM reasoning capability. Evaluated on 200 real remote-sensing satellite tasks across 7 SLMs, PTC-Decoder yields a statistically significant mean overall score gain of +1.21 (p<0.01), 95% CI [+1.13, +1.29]), with consistent improvements across models and other datasets. An ablation study that removes TC-Decoder causes substantial performance degradation across all quality metrics without reducing computational cost, confirming TC-Decoder as the primary driver. PTC-Decoder thus offers a lightweight yet effective solution for improving step-level reliability, with final-answer accuracy remaining an open challenge. In essence, we enforce plan adherence by constraining the permissible output vocabulary during inference, without requiring retraining.

</details>


### [185] [Why Jailbreaks Succeed in Diffusion Language Models: An Energy Landscape Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30841)
*Thong Bach,Dung Nguyen,Thao Minh Le,Truyen Tran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出基于扩散能量景观的 dLLM 越狱检测框架，利用初始安全倾向和去噪轨迹动能构造三种无需训练的互补检测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对扩散式大语言模型（dLLMs）的攻击与防御往往聚焦特定漏洞，缺少统一理论来解释攻击为何成功，也难以形成覆盖多种攻击方式的检测方案。

Method: 将安全对齐解释为塑造去噪能量景观：安全输出与有害输出区域之间存在能量屏障。越狱要么在初始化时掩盖查询的安全属性，要么在去噪过程中干预轨迹、迫使其跨越屏障。基于掩码扩散模型去噪时最小化动能这一性质，提出三种免训练检测信号：生成前读取初始 logits 安全倾向的 step-0 ratio，以及在 logits 空间两个互补子空间中跟踪轨迹速度（动能）的两种信号。

Result: 在 LLaDA-8B、LLaDA-1.5、Dream-7B 三种稠密 dLLM 和 LLaDA-MoE-7B 稀疏混合专家模型上评估，结果支持三种信号具有互补性。在已知攻击的压力测试中，所有逃过检测的配置也未能生成有害内容。

Conclusion: 该框架把越狱检测归结为监测攻击在初始安全倾向或去噪轨迹动能上的代价；三种信号按构造覆盖彼此盲点。实验暗示检测阈值与成功跨越安全屏障的阈值难以分离，但结论目前基于所测试的模型与攻击范围。

Abstract: Existing attacks and defenses for diffusion-based large language models (dLLMs) target specific vulnerabilities but lack a shared framework explaining why attacks succeed. We propose one by interpreting safety alignment as shaping the denoising energy landscape: a well-aligned model routes harmful queries toward safe outputs through an energy barrier that separates the two regions. Current jailbreak attacks reduce to two strategies for circumventing this barrier: obscuring the query's safety disposition at initialisation, or intervening mid-trajectory to force the denoising path across the energy barrier. From this perspective and the result that masked diffusion models minimise kinetic energy during denoising, we derive three complementary, training-free detection signals: a step-0 ratio that reads the initial safety disposition from the logit distribution before generation begins, and two trajectory-velocity signals that track kinetic energy in complementary subspaces of the logit space. An attack must either reveal its intent at initialisation or expend kinetic energy to cross the barrier in at least one monitored subspace, so the three signals cover each other's blind spots in the energy budget by construction. Evaluation across three dense dLLMs (LLaDA-8B, LLaDA-1.5, Dream-7B) and a sparse mixture-of-experts dLLM (LLaDA-MoE-7B) confirms this complementarity. In stress tests of known attacks, every configuration that evades detection also fails to produce harmful content, suggesting that the detection and barrier-crossing thresholds are hard to separate.

</details>


### [186] [SkillEvoReg: Regularizing Agent Skill Evolution Against Overfitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30861)
*Guanyu Nie,Fangzhou Zhu,Shixiong Kai,Xiongwei Han,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 SkillEvoReg，用正则化减轻语言模型智能体在持续更新外部技能时的过拟合，在控制技能状态增长的同时保持并改善部分下游能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体通过执行经验不断更新可复用技能，但局部有效的编辑可能累积为冗余或过度依赖特定任务的指令，也可能破坏此前有效的行为。论文将这种现象定义为技能演化过拟合，指出需要对技能更新过程本身进行约束。

Method: 提出通用框架 SkillEvoReg，包含三项机制：训练阶段技能 dropout，通过扰动更新生成过程提升稳健性；复杂度感知的局部正则化，抑制不必要的技能结构增长；因果反例验证（CCV），针对候选更新检查其是否导致行为回退。该框架应用于多种技能演化系统，并保留各系统原有的技能更新器与任务评估器。

Result: 在 SkillOpt、SkillEvolBench 和 ContinualSkillBench 上，SkillEvoReg 均能控制技能状态增长，同时保持有竞争力的下游能力；在若干迁移和后期演化指标上有所改善。CCV 还能发现仅靠结构指标无法识别的更新级行为回退。摘要未提供具体数值或显著性检验信息。

Conclusion: 显式正则化可作为更强技能更新器的有效补充，帮助降低技能演化中的冗余增长与行为退化风险。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly improve by converting execution experience into reusable external skills. Yet repeated skill updates form a learning process of their own: locally useful edits can accumulate into redundant or task-specific instructions, while new updates can disrupt behavior that previously worked. We study this problem as skill-evolution overfitting and introduce SkillEvoReg, a general regularization framework for skill evolution inspired by anti-overfitting techniques in neural-network training. SkillEvoReg combines training-time skill dropout, which perturbs update generation, and complexity-aware local regularization, which controls unnecessary structural growth, with causal counterexample validation (CCV), which provides targeted behavioral validation of candidate-specific regressions. We instantiate the framework across heterogeneous skill-evolution systems while retaining each system's native skill evolver and task evaluator. Across SkillOpt, SkillEvolBench, and ContinualSkillBench, SkillEvoReg consistently controls skill-state growth while preserving competitive downstream capability, improves several transfer and later-stage evolution outcomes, and identifies update-level regressions that structural metrics alone cannot reveal. These results suggest that explicit regularization is a useful complement to increasingly capable skill updaters.

</details>


### [187] [TISD: On-Policy Self-Distillation with Trajectory Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30878)
*Taeckyung Lee,Rinat Amankos,Jeonghye Kim,Hyungjun Yoon,Woogyeol Jin,Sung-Ju Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，按学生自身采样轨迹进行蒸馏会限制教师监督的覆盖范围；提出 TISD，由教师选择分支动作、学生续写，再对完整轨迹蒸馏，在编码和科学任务上较 SDPO 获得小幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy self-distillation（OPSD）只在学生采样到的轨迹上评估教师目标。当教师在已访问前缀处偏好另一动作时，学生若未采样该动作，教师便无法监督该分支所产生的后续上下文，形成训练数据收集瓶颈。论文进一步提出，教师—学生分歧可能更适合用于提出轨迹分支，而非仅用于定位局部修正。

Method: 作者通过受控 token 干预诊断发现，在分歧峰值处采用教师偏好的 token，可能提升学生后续生成成功率，但其局部纠错作用有限。据此提出 Trajectory-Intervention Self-Distillation（TISD）：强制采用教师选定的分支动作，将后缀生成交还学生，并在特权上下文条件下由教师对完整轨迹进行蒸馏。

Result: 编码模型实验中，TISD 相较 SDPO 的平均 Avg@4 提高 1.2 个百分点。科学领域实验中，在相同步数预算下平均 Avg@128 提高 0.8 个百分点；在相同时间预算下提高 0.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果支持教师引导的分支探索能够暴露有用的后续上下文，从而改善自蒸馏。报告的提升幅度较小，且随任务及预算设定而变化。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) provides dense teacher targets, but evaluates them only along student-sampled rollouts. When the privileged teacher favors an alternative action at a visited prefix, OPSD can provide a target for the branch decision but cannot supervise the successor contexts induced by that action unless the student samples it. This creates a training-time data-collection bottleneck and suggests a different role for teacher-student disagreement: proposing a trajectory branch rather than identifying a sufficient local repair. Our diagnostic framework using controlled token interventions reveals that a teacher-preferred token at peak disagreement can improve student continuation success, while its local corrective value is limited. Motivated by this finding, we introduce a simple branch-regenerate-distill algorithm, Trajectory-Intervention Self-Distillation (TISD). TISD forces a teacher-selected branch action, returns suffix generation to the student, and distills the full trajectory under the privileged-context-conditioned teacher. Across the coding models, TISD improves average Avg@4 over SDPO by 1.2 percentage points. Across the science domains, it improves average Avg@128 by 0.8 points under an equal-step budget and by 0.3 points under an equal-time budget. These results support teacher-guided branching as a way to expose useful successor contexts for self-distillation.

</details>


### [188] [EXAONE Demand 1.0: A Time Series Foundation Model for Demand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30880)
*Seunghan Lee,Sangjun Han,Jun Seo,Junhyeok Kang,Jaehoon Lee,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 EXAONE Demand：针对需求预测构建专用时间序列基础模型，在 22 个留出数据集上优于 36 个 TSFM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用时间序列基础模型的预训练语料中需求序列占比很低，难以覆盖需求数据的短历史、频繁零值、缺货导致的删失，以及序列未记录的外生事件等特性。

Method: 构建包含 1,130 万条序列、484 亿个观测、来自 73 个来源的需求语料，并用合成生成器补足公开数据较少呈现的行为。模型采用冻结的通用领域骨干网络，添加四类需求模式（平滑、间歇、波动、块状）的低秩适配分支；路由器依据输入序列的八项尺度无关统计量，决定各分支的贡献。训练两个版本：真实与合成需求数据联合训练，以及仅用合成数据训练。

Result: 在 22 个留出数据集上，两个 EXAONE Demand 版本均优于 36 个时间序列基础模型；联合使用真实需求数据还能较仅用合成数据带来额外提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明，面向需求数据特性设计专用语料与适配机制，能够提升需求预测表现；真实需求数据仍可为合成数据训练提供补充收益。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are pretrained on series from diverse domains, where demand series make up only a small fraction. Demand data has properties that such corpora rarely contain: Short histories, frequent zeros, censoring by stock-outs, and exogenous events that the series does not record. To this end, we propose EXAONE Demand, built on 1) a demand-specific corpus and 2) a demand-aware adapter. For the corpus, we assemble 11.3M series and 48.4B observations from 73 sources, and a synthetic generator supplies the behaviour that open demand data under-represents. For the adapter, we attach low-rank branches to a frozen general-domain backbone, one for each of the four demand classes (smooth, intermittent, erratic, and lumpy), and a router that reads eight scale-free statistics of the input series decides how much each branch contributes. We build EXAONE Demand in two versions, one trained on real-world and synthetic demand together and one trained on the synthetic corpus alone. On 22 held-out datasets, both versions outperform 36 TSFMs, and real-world demand adds a gain over synthetic data alone.

</details>


### [189] [From Tapping to Hopping: Augmenting Mobile GUI Agents with App-Native Deeplinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30887)
*Yuchen Sun,Chenglin Cai,Gongjie Zhang,Tianyu Xia,Quyu Kong,Panrong Tong,Zhengwen Zeng,Long Chen,Steven Hoi,Chongyang Zhang,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 GUI-Hopper：将 deeplink 直接导航与常规 GUI 操作结合，并通过经验证的 deeplink 目录提升移动端任务完成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动 GUI 智能体通常依靠逐屏点击、滑动到达目标界面，过程较长且易出错；deeplink 可用一次调用直达界面，但需要可靠发现并确认其行为。

Method: 先通过静态分析发现候选 deeplink，再在真实设备上验证，并记录实际落地界面的描述，构建经过验证且有界面语义标注的 deeplink 目录。随后提出 GUI-Hopper：导航时使用 deeplink，其他屏幕操作仍使用 GUI 动作，并保留 GUI 操作作为回退方式。

Result: 摘要称 GUI-Hopper 在真实设备上的商业应用中提高了任务成功率，证明混合交互具有优势；摘要未给出具体实验数值或比较细节。

Conclusion: 将 deeplink 与 GUI 动作结合，可减少到达目标界面的逐步操作，同时保持对非导航操作和异常情况的处理能力。经验证并带有落地界面描述的 deeplink 目录是实现该方案的重要基础。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents complete tasks using GUI actions like taps and swipes. These actions are broadly applicable across applications, but reaching a navigation interface. A single deeplink call can replace a sequence of screen-by-screen GUI actions. We therefore introduce hybrid interaction, using deeplinks for direct navigation and GUI actions for other on-screen operations and fallback. To enable this, we discover candidate deeplinks through static analysis, validate them on real devices, and describe their observed landing screens. This process creates a verified and grounded deeplink catalog that pairs each working deeplink with a description of its landing screen. Using this catalog, we introduce GUI-Hopper, a improves task success in commercial applications on real devices, further demonstrating the benefits of hybrid interaction.

</details>


### [190] [Training Graph Foundation Models on The Web Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30894)
*Ryoma Sato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Acacia，一种仅用 Common Crawl 网页图从头训练的图基础模型，无需额外训练即可适配多种特征、标签和图任务，并展现上下文学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型往往需要针对新图、新标签训练分类头或特征投影器；不少模型还依赖预训练大语言模型组合而成。因此，模型能否仅凭图结构数据从头获得通用能力仍待验证。

Method: 在 Common Crawl 网页图上从头训练 Acacia，不依赖预训练 LLM。模型设计目标包括兼容任意维度与语义的节点特征，并支持多种图任务及上下文学习，而无需额外训练。

Result: 摘要声称 Acacia 无需额外训练即可执行节点分类、链接预测、节点聚类和图生成，并适配任意特征维度与语义，同时具备上下文学习能力。摘要未提供具体实验设置、数据指标或基线比较数值。

Conclusion: 作者认为，Acacia 展示了仅基于图数据训练的模型也可能获得跨任务的涌现能力，为图模型从头习得通用能力提供了证据。

Abstract: We introduce Acacia, a graph foundation model, trained on the web graph. Acacia (i) supports arbitrary feature dimensionalities and semantics without additional training, (ii) supports a wide range of tasks, including node classification, link prediction, node clustering, and graph generation, without additional training, (iii) has in-context learning capabilities, and (iv) does not rely on pretrained LLMs. In particular, existing graph foundation models often require training additional classification heads or feature projectors to accommodate new graphs or new labels, whereas Acacia does not. Moreover, existing graph foundation models often gain their capabilities by being stitched together with pretrained LLMs, whereas Acacia is trained from scratch using only the Common Crawl web graph. This is also an important result because it provides evidence that graph models can acquire emergent capabilities from scratch like LLMs.

</details>


### [191] [JevSoup: System-One Routing for Training-Free LoRA Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30922)
*Xiuying Wang,Jiahua Cheng,Shuotian Li,Yufan Cheng,Bowen Deng,Zhexuan Bai,Yichen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JevSoup 是一种无需训练的 LoRA 专家组合框架：根据输入及专家描述选出两个专家，再通过正交投影处理第二个专家的更新并等权合并。在 14 项 PorTAL 任务和三种 Qwen3 规模上，较所评估的最强外部基线，任务宏平均准确率最高提升 1.19 个百分点，样本微平均准确率最高提升 1.21 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LoRA 专家路由方法可能依赖辅助数据或额外训练，或需要自回归解码，增加了系统适配与推理的成本。论文旨在构建一种无需训练、能协调专门能力并适用于多任务的路由及执行方案。

Method: 框架将流程分为 System One 路由与 System Two 执行。路由阶段仅使用输入和专家描述，通过结构化概率选择两名专家；执行阶段保留排名第一专家的更新，将第二名专家的更新投影到第一名更新的行空间正交补上，再以相同权重合并。

Result: 在 14 项 PorTAL 任务及三种 Qwen3 模型规模的评测中，JevSoup 相较所评估的最强外部基线，任务宏平均准确率最高提升 1.19 个百分点，样本微平均准确率最高提升 1.21 个百分点。摘要未提供各任务结果、统计显著性或计算开销数据。

Conclusion: 摘要显示，JevSoup 通过训练免费的专家选择与更新正交化组合，在所测任务和模型规模上取得了相对基线的准确率提升。结果支持其作为 LoRA 专家组合方案的潜力，但提升幅度及适用范围仍需结合完整实验和更广泛评测判断。

Abstract: Building adaptable AI systems requires effective coordination of specialized capabilities across diverse tasks. Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) enables modular expertise, but existing routing approaches may require auxiliary data, additional training, or autoregressive decoding. We propose JevSoup, a training-free framework separating System One expert routing from System Two execution. Using only the input and expert descriptions, Jev selects two experts through structured probabilities. JevSoup retains the leading expert's update, projects the second onto the orthogonal complement of the first update's row space, and combines them with equal weights. Across 14 PorTAL tasks and three Qwen3 scales, JepSoup achieves absolute gains of up to 1.19\% in task-macro and 1.21\% in sample-micro accuracy over the strongest evaluated external baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/Leowang980/JevSoup.

</details>


### [192] [Self-Play Search Distillation for Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30936)
*Lorenzo Molfetta,Wai-Chung Kwan,Giacomo Frisoni,Luca Ragazzi,Gianluca Moro,Pavlos Vougiouklis,Jeff Z. Pan,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出自我博弈搜索蒸馏（SPSD），将棋类游戏中 MuZero 类网络的搜索记录转化为结构化推理链，用于训练大语言模型；实验显示其改善数学基准成绩和未见棋类游戏表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升大语言模型推理能力需要包含困难决策、备选方案及其后果的高质量训练数据，但合成数据质量不高，人工标注成本也较高，造成数据稀缺。

Method: 在可执行的棋类环境中训练 MuZero 类网络并进行自我博弈搜索。对每个状态，记录专家偏好的决策、合理备选项、可能的对手回应及价值估计，再将搜索记录转化为结构化推理链，以环境为依据监督训练大语言模型。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B-Base 上，仅使用自我博弈搜索记录训练后，六项数学基准的平均分从 24.1 提升至 36.6；留出棋类游戏的胜率从 15% 提升至 45%。摘要称该方法还可迁移至未见过的数学任务。

Conclusion: SPSD 利用可执行环境中的搜索结果生成高质量合成推理数据，减少对人工标注的依赖，并在摘要所述实验中改善了数学推理和棋类泛化表现。

Abstract: Improving reasoning abilities in Large Language Models (LLMs) requires high-quality data that exposes difficult decisions, competing alternatives, and their consequences. Data scarcity is driven by the low quality of synthetic data and the cost of human labeling. We introduce Self-Play Search Distillation (SPSD), a framework for generating superhuman synthetic data via self-play of MuZero-like networks trained on board games. SPSD uses executable environments to turn search into structured reasoning problems. At each state, the expert identifies a preferred decision, plausible alternatives, plausible opponent replies, and value estimates. By converting the self-play search records into superhuman chains-of-thought, we train LLMs with environment-grounded supervision. Although trained only on self-play search records, SPSD transfers to unseen mathematics. On Qwen3-4B-Base, it raises the mean over six mathematics benchmarks from 24.1 to 36.6 while increasing the held-out-game win rate from 15% to 45%. SPSD offers an annotation-efficient way to create high-quality synthetic data for improving LLM performance in reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [193] [MACBT: A Multi-Agent Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Decision Support System with Longitudinal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30939)
*De Jiang,Shuo Zhang,Weiwei Liao,Jianying Zhang,Chuanhui Yu,Hongen Liao,Kehong Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向临床工作者的AI辅助系统，将多智能体认知行为疗法框架与跨会话记忆结合，以减轻准备、记录和长期追踪负担；初步评估显示其在专业性、临床真实性及连续性等指标上优于基线系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBT虽是抑郁症的一线循证疗法，但治疗师需投入大量时间进行会前准备、会后记录和跨会话认知问题追踪，限制了服务规模。

Method: 构建五智能体MACBT，对应评估、苏格拉底式提问、认知重构、行为实验和治疗监测五阶段；设计CD Memory，跨会话记录认知扭曲的类型、频率、严重程度及重构效果，并生成会前报告和干预优先级建议。研究者通过双角色大语言模型模拟构建中文CBT对话语料，并对Qwen3-14B进行监督微调和直接偏好优化。

Result: GPT-4评估显示，MACBT在专业性（2.62）和临床真实性（2.25）方面优于MeChat、SoulChat、PsyChat和CPsyCounX。加入记忆模块后，会谈质量进一步提升12.6%；跨会话连续性、干预进展和个性化的纵向均值为2.29。摘要未说明评分量表范围、样本规模或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 该系统尝试把结构化CBT流程与纵向记忆结合，为治疗师提供决策支持，有望改善会谈质量及跨会话个性化，并减轻部分工作负担。现有结果主要来自模型裁判评估，临床效用仍需在真实治疗场景中验证。

Abstract: Cognitive behavioral therapy (CBT) is an evidence-based first-line treatment for depression, yet its scale is constrained by the time clinicians spend on pre-session preparation, post-session documentation, and longitudinal cognitive-pathology tracking. We present a clinician-facing AI decision-support system that combines a multi-agent CBT framework (MACBT) with a CBT-specific longitudinal memory module (CD Memory). MACBT encodes the five-stage CBT workflow (assessment, Socratic questioning, cognitive restructuring, behavioral experiments, and treatment monitoring) into five collaborative agents. CD Memory tracks cognitive-distortion type, frequency, severity, and restructuring efficacy across sessions to generate pre-session pathology reports and intervention-priority recommendations. We construct a Chinese CBT dialogue corpus via dual-role large language model simulation and train a Qwen3-14B backbone with supervised fine-tuning and direct preference optimization. Evaluation with GPT-4 judges shows MACBT outperforms MeChat, SoulChat, PsyChat, and CPsyCounX in professionalism (2.62) and clinical authenticity (2.25). The full memory-augmented system further improves session quality by 12.6% and achieves a longitudinal mean of 2.29 on cross-session continuity, intervention progression, and personalization.

</details>


### [194] [Financial Fragility in Societies of LLM Agents: Coordination Failures and Stabilizing Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30940)
*Zhenhao Fu,Ruipeng Xu,Qibing Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过 FRAIL 框架研究 LLM 智能体在动态金融环境中的系统性脆弱性，发现个体理性不保证集体稳定；三类协调机制均有助于改善结果，而早期广泛承诺是成功稳定的共同特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 智能体参与金融决策，单个智能体的安全性不足以保证其共同构成的金融系统安全。论文旨在考察智能体互动如何导致可避免的集体失败，以及何种协调机制有助于缓解风险。

Method: 提出受控实验框架 FRAIL，在银行挤兑、债务续借和奖励型众筹三种动态金融环境中，让智能体决策改变其他参与者所面对的金融条件。实验覆盖七种主流 LLM，并比较基于补偿承诺、集中式承诺协议和参与者主导联盟的三种互动机制。

Result: 在没有要求任何智能体破坏系统的基线条件下，银行挤兑实验中 77% 的回合、债务续借实验中 83% 的回合以失败告终，显示出普遍的集体脆弱性。三种互动机制都改善了整体结果，但不存在适用于所有金融结构的最佳单一机制。成功稳定通常遵循相似的时间模式：在防御性行为形成自我强化之前，尽早建立广泛承诺。

Conclusion: LLM 智能体具备个体决策能力，并不意味着它们能自然形成安全稳定的金融系统。金融 AI 安全需要进行系统层面的评估，并针对具体互动结构设计协调机制；及时形成广泛承诺可能是稳定系统的重要条件。

Abstract: Individually protective decisions can produce avoidable collective failures. As large language model (LLM) agents take on greater roles in financial decision-making, financial AI safety must therefore be considered not only at the level of individual agents, but also at the level of the systems they jointly create. We study this problem with FRAIL, a controlled experimental framework that places LLM agents in three dynamic financial environments---bank runs, debt rollover, and reward crowdfunding---where agents' decisions reshape the financial conditions faced by others. Across seven leading LLMs, we find widespread collective fragility even when no agent is instructed to destabilize the system: 77\% of baseline bank-run episodes and 83\% of debt-rollover episodes end in failure. We then compare three interaction mechanisms based on compensated commitments, centralized commitment agreements, and participant-led coalitions. All three improve aggregate outcomes, but no single mechanism performs best across all financial structures. Across mechanisms, successful stabilization shares a common temporal pattern: broad commitment forms early, before defensive behavior becomes self-reinforcing. Our findings show that individually capable agents do not automatically form safe financial systems, highlighting system-level evaluation and interaction design as central problems for financial AI safety. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/FinFrail-CF26.

</details>


### [195] [LogicTree-RAG: Logic Tree-guided Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Long-form Patent Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30943)
*Jiaqi Zhu,Naili Xing,Hexiang Pan,Haotian Gao,Jianwei Yin,Xiaokui Xiao,Beng Chin Ooi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 LogicTree-RAG，通过证据引导构建层级逻辑树，并将其映射到专利各章节，以提升长篇技术文档生成的整体连贯性、内容质量和格式合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇技术文档生成要求跨章节维持逻辑一致、技术信息完整且推理可靠。专利撰写尤其需要全面、合规的整体文档，但既有方法多局限于单独章节生成，或依赖人工设计提纲，难以规模化应用。

Method: 提出 LogicTree-RAG 框架：以检索增强方式获取证据，通过递归生成构建层级逻辑树，每个节点对应一个技术要素；再采用混合遍历机制，将逻辑树映射至专利章节，实现可控且章节分布均衡的生成。该方法不依赖专家预先定义的撰写先验。

Result: 实验表明，相较于强 LLM 基线，LogicTree-RAG 持续提升内容质量与语言规范性，并能以较高 token 效率生成更长、更有结构的文本。摘要未提供具体数据或评测细节。

Conclusion: 逻辑树可作为长篇技术文档的全局组织骨架，帮助模型协调技术披露内容与章节结构；实验结果支持逻辑中心化生成用于复杂技术文档撰写的有效性。

Abstract: Long-form technical text generation underpins knowledge-intensive workflows, yet remains challenging for large language models (LLMs) due to the need for globally consistent logical structuring and faithful technical reasoning beyond local coherence. Patent drafting is a canonical instance of this challenge, demanding holistic generation of a legally compliant and technically exhaustive document through sustained multi-expert collaboration. Existing approaches often focus on partial section generation or rely on manually crafted outlines, limiting scalable automation in realistic settings. In this work, we propose LogicTree-RAG, a logic tree-guided retrieval-augmented generation framework that induces a hierarchical logic tree as a global organizational backbone to organize and ground technical disclosures, without relying on expert-defined drafting priors. Each node in the logic tree represents a technical element and is constructed through evidence-guided recursive generation. A hybrid traversal mechanism then maps the logic tree into patent sections, enabling controllable and section-balanced generation. Extensive experiments show that LogicTree-RAG consistently improves content quality and language conformity over strong LLM-based baselines and achieves longer structured generation with high token efficiency, demonstrating the effectiveness of logic-centric generation for complex technical document drafting.

</details>


### [196] [FTB Graph: Determining and Validating First-token Broadcasters and Language-Identity Head Circuits in Multilingual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30954)
*Arjun Pillai,Christian Hoang,Anjelo Laroza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过跨模型结构因果分析，研究多语言模型生成首个 token 时如何确定输出语言；结果显示语言路由电路因模型而异，梯度近似与精确因果干预常有偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言模型需要在生成初期确定目标响应语言，但首 token 语言身份决策由哪些内部组件及连接驱动，尚未得到充分刻画。

Method: 分析 GPT-2、BLOOM-560M、Pythia-1B/2.8B 与 Qwen2.5-1.5B Base/Instruct 六种模型。先使用 FP16 主动钳制的 Edge Attribution Patching（EAP）提取候选有向无环电路，再在最多 2,000 条候选边的搜索范围内，以精确激活补丁验证因果作用。

Result: 独立模型通常呈现位于中层或中后层的广播枢纽；Pythia-2.8B 与 BLOOM-560M 的证据较强，而 GPT-2、Pythia-1B 可用于对照的图外注意力头较少，两个 Qwen 变体则未通过必要性检验。Pythia 从 1B 扩至 2.8B 后，参与节点增多而验证边数预算相近，电路因而更稀疏。Qwen Base 与 Instruct 电路的 Jaccard 相似度为 84.7%，且都包含第 27 层枢纽。多数模型中，EAP 分数与精确补丁效应的相关性较弱。

Conclusion: 首 token 语言路由可由跨层结构电路驱动；Qwen 结果提示该路由在预训练期间已大体形成，并在指令微调后保留。由于梯度线性近似可能偏离 FP16 下的因果干预结果，可靠的电路发现应以精确激活补丁进行验证。

Abstract: Large language models operating in multilingual contexts must resolve target response languages early in generation, yet the causal circuitry governing first-token language identity decisions remains poorly mapped. We present an end-to-end structural circuit analysis across six model architectures spanning four families: GPT-2, BLOOM-560M, Pythia-1B/2.8B, and Qwen2.5-1.5B Base/Instruct. Using Edge Attribution Patching (EAP) with FP16 active clamping, followed by exact activation patching verification with a 2,000-candidate-edge search ceiling, we extract directed acyclic graphs driving first-token language broadcasting. Across the standalone models, we observe deep or mid-to-deep broadcasting hubs, though the evidence is strongest for Pythia-2.8B and BLOOM-560M because GPT-2 and Pythia-1B leave few out-of-graph heads for comparison, while both Qwen2.5-1.5B variants invert the necessity check. Scaling from Pythia-1B to 2.8B expands node participation while maintaining a similar verified edge budget, producing sparser topology. The Qwen2.5-1.5B base and instruct circuits retain 84.7% Jaccard similarity, including the Layer 27 hub, indicating that first-token routing is largely established during pretraining and preserved by instruction tuning. Finally, EAP scores correlate weakly with exact patching deltas across most models, showing that linear gradient approximations can diverge from causal interventions in FP16 and motivating exact-patching verification for reliable circuit discovery.

</details>


### [197] [MoMHa: Multi-Objective Optimization of LLM Harnesses over Accuracy, Safety, and Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30967)
*Subhojyoti Mukherjee,Md Mehrab Tanjim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Meta-Harness，将大语言模型周边的 Python harness 作为可优化对象，在准确性、行为安全性和 token 成本之间进行多目标优化；其单阶段联合奖励方法 MoMHa 在合成与真实基准上整体优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型改进工作往往只关注准确率，但 harness 还会影响安全行为与推理成本。只追求准确、却放任不安全响应或显著增加 token 消耗的 harness 并不理想，因此需要将 harness 设计视为多目标优化问题。

Method: 提出 Meta-Harness，在每个领域同时优化准确性、行为安全性和 token 成本。系统使用具备代理能力的提议器 Claude Code，并向其提供既有 harness 源码、执行轨迹及评分材料，以搜索和改进 harness。核心方案 MoMHa 采用单阶段联合奖励；实验将其与两阶段“先准确率、后 token”、仅标量反馈和准确率单目标等基线比较。评测覆盖 17 个领域、12 个模型（来自 4 个模型系列），包括合成能力套件、公开真实基准及用户特定安全领域。

Result: 合成评测中，MoMHa 联合均值为 0.482，高于十个基线的 0.198–0.422，并在 10 个领域列中赢得 7 个；真实基准中得分为 0.461，高于最强基线 DSPy 的 0.377，并在 7 个领域列中赢得 5 个。其 U-SafeBench 行为安全综合分最高，为 0.781；相较两阶段方案，每个样例减少 95 个 token。作者还报告，方法在 8 个未参与训练的目标模型上体现迁移能力。

Conclusion: 研究表明，harness 是影响模型准确性、安全性和成本的重要设计层，将三者纳入单阶段联合优化，比单目标或分阶段优化更有效。结果也提示，学到的 harness 策略可迁移至未见过的真实基准及部分目标模型。作者计划公开代码、评测基础设施和跨模型日志。

Abstract: Most work on improving large language models treats accuracy as the sole objective. We argue that the harness, the Python code surrounding the model that constructs prompts, routes calls, and parses outputs, is a first-class design surface whose quality is inherently multi-objective: an accurate harness that refuses no unsafe request, or that consumes an order of magnitude more tokens, is not a good harness. We present Meta-Harness, a system that casts harness design as search over three per-domain objectives (accuracy, behavioural safety, and token cost) solved by an agentic proposer (Claude Code) with full filesystem access to prior harness source, execution traces, and scoring artifacts. Our central finding is that a singlephase joint-reward proposer (MoMHa) outperforms every alternative, including a two-phase "accuracy then tokens" ablation, scalar-only feedback, and an accuracy-only baseline. We evaluate on seventeen domains: seven synthetic capability suites, seven real-world public benchmarks (HumanEval, MBPP, Spider, FEVER, MMLU-Pro, LawBench, NuminaMath), and three U-SafeBench-derived user-specific safety domains, using a 12-model fleet spanning four families. On the synthetic track MoMHa achieves a joint mean of 0.482 versus 0.198-0.422 for ten baselines, winning $7 / 10$ per-domain columns; on the real-world track it scores 0.461 versus 0.377 for the strongest baseline (DSPy), winning 5/7 columns, demonstrating that harness strategies transfer to unseen benchmarks without retraining on 8 of 12 target models. MoMHa attains the highest measured behavioral safety composite (U-SafeBench, 0.781) and uses 95 fewer tokens per example than the two-phase alternative. We will release all harness code, evaluation infrastructure, and crossmodel logs.

</details>


### [198] [SciHorizon-eLab: An Agentic Protocol-to-Task Compiler for Scalable Benchmarking of Scientific Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30971)
*Maokai Qin,Chuan Qin,Qi Zhang,Dianyu Liu,Zirui Liu,Hongting Niu,Yuanchun Zhou,Hengshu Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 SciHorizon-eLab，将自然语言实验协议自动编译为可执行、可验证的具身实验任务，并据此构建包含 300 项认证任务的基准；当前最强策略平均成功率为 49.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体有望自动化科学实验，但缺少可靠、系统的评测环境。现有模拟实验室基准依赖大量人工设计，难以规模化地将多样实验流程转化为可执行且可验证的任务。

Method: 提出协议到任务的智能编译系统 SciHorizon-eLab，将任务构建视为编译过程，依次进行语义 grounding、可执行任务合成和多阶段仿真认证。系统生成语义对齐的环境、可执行操作程序及逐步成功判据，并支持可复现的专家示范与执行轨迹生成；基于该流程构建包含 300 项认证任务的基准。

Result: 基准覆盖多种实验室操作，支持人在环执行、专家示范生成及有序的逐步评估。代表性任务中，最强策略的平均成功率仅为 49.7%；进一步评估显示，人类与具身智能体的协同仍存在明显短板。

Conclusion: 该工作以自动化编译与仿真认证降低了实验任务基准构建对人工工程的依赖，并提供规模化、可复现的评测资源。结果也表明，现有具身策略及人机协同能力距离可靠执行实验室任务仍有较大差距。

Abstract: Embodied agents offer a promising route to automating scientific experimentation, yet their progress is constrained by the lack of reliable and systematic evaluation environments. Existing simulation-based laboratory benchmarks rely heavily on manual task engineering, making it challenging to systematically compile diverse scientific protocols into executable and verifiable embodied tasks at scale. To address this challenge, we introduce SciHorizon-eLab, an agentic protocol-to-task compiler that formulates scientific embodied task construction as a compilation problem. Given a natural-language protocol of scientific experiments, SciHorizon-eLab progressively compiles laboratory protocols into semantic-preserving embodied tasks through semantic grounding, executable task synthesis, and multi-stage simulation-based certification. The system generates semantically grounded environments, executable manipulation programs, and step-level success specifications, while enabling reproducible generation of expert demonstrations and execution traces. Using this pipeline, we further construct \BenchName, a ready-to-use benchmark comprising 300 certified tasks across diverse laboratory operations. It supports HIL task execution, reproducible expert-demonstration generation, and ordered step-level evaluation. Across representative tasks, the strongest policy attains an average success rate of only 49.7%, with further evaluations revealing pronounced weaknesses in human and embodied agent coordination. We publicly release the code, benchmark data, and evaluation toolkit at https://github.com/SciHorizon-elab/SciHorizon-elab.

</details>


### [199] [Factorized axis convolutional gated recurrent unit with dynamic adaptive pooling for remaining useful life prediction of rolling bearings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30972)
*Hanbyeol Park,Jungho Choo,Hyerim Bae*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向滚动轴承剩余寿命（RUL）预测的模型，通过方向敏感的时频特征提取、自适应聚合和时序建模提升预测效果，并用蒙特卡洛 Dropout 估计不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规 CNN 的各向同性卷积核不易捕捉时频图中沿时间轴或频率轴排列的退化特征；全局平均池化可能抹去显著响应的位置与集中程度，因此需要更具方向性且保留关键信息的特征处理方法。

Method: 提出因子化轴卷积-GRU架构：以多尺度各向异性卷积和双轴卷积块注意力模块提取、强化方向特征；用动态自适应池化（DAP）聚合时频轴信息；由 GRU 建模潜在表征的时间动态，并通过蒙特卡洛 Dropout 估计预测不确定性。

Result: 在两个公开轴承数据集上的实验显示，该模型在不同运行条件下优于已有 RUL 预测方法。消融实验表明，因子化轴向设计较各向同性卷积核具有更低的平均误差；DAP 在一个数据集上带来明显提升，在另一个数据集上与 GAP 表现相当。

Conclusion: 结果支持在时频表示的 RUL 预测中结合各向异性特征提取与自适应特征聚合。方法的整体优势得到实验验证，但 DAP 的收益具有数据集依赖性；蒙特卡洛 Dropout 提供不确定性估计能力。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNN) are widely used to predict the remaining useful life (RUL) of rolling bearings from time-frequency representations (TFRs) of vibration signals. However, during degradation, characteristic structures in TFRs align predominantly along the frequency or time axis, making it challenging for conventional CNN isotropic kernels to capture directional structure. Furthermore, global average pooling (GAP) averages across axes, potentially obscuring the locations and concentrations of salient activations. This study introduces a factorized-axis convolutional gated recurrent unit (GRU) that employs multiscale anisotropic convolution and dual-axis convolution block attention module to enhance directional features and highlight salient time-frequency regions. Dynamic adaptive pooling (DAP) adaptively aggregates the time-frequency-axis information from the extracted feature maps, whereas a GRU captures temporal dynamics in the latent representations and Monte Carlo dropout enables predictive uncertainty estimation. Experiments on two public bearing datasets demonstrate that the proposed model outperforms existing RUL prediction methods across operating conditions. Ablation experiments demonstrate that the factorized axis-wise design achieves lower mean errors than convolutional isotropic kernels. DAP yields clear improvements on one dataset while matching GAP on the other, highlighting the importance of anisotropic feature extraction and adaptive feature aggregation for TFR-based RUL prediction.

</details>


### [200] [Same Text, Different Numbers: The Divergence of LLM-Based Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31013)
*Hamid Boustanifar,Sasan Mansouri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文考察不同大语言模型生成企业文本变量时的一致性，发现测量结果具有明显模型依赖性，且模型选择会改变后续实证结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者日益用大语言模型将企业文本转化为实证变量，但不同模型是否对同一文本给出稳定、可比的测量，尚不明确；这种差异也可能影响后续统计推断。

Method: 选取13类文本构念（如情绪、管理层表达清晰度、不确定性、回答具体性，以及气候与政治风险），使用来自不同提供商的7个大语言模型，对标普500企业的业绩电话会议记录进行评分；比较模型间相关性、分歧来源及其对下游分析的影响，并考察跨模型平均的效果。

Result: 模型间排名相关系数平均仅为0.52；跨提供商普遍存在的文本层面差异仅解释总评分变异的34%。模型间分歧不能预测后续分析师或市场分歧。不同模型会使下游回归系数的大小、符号和显著性出现明显变化。跨提供商平均通常能提升文本排序稳定性，但评分水平仍取决于纳入集成的模型。

Conclusion: 基于大语言模型生成的文本变量应视为依赖具体模型的测量结果，而非天然客观且可互换的指标。实证研究应报告模型选择，并通过多个提供商的模型进行稳健性验证；若使用集成平均，也需检验模型组合对评分水平的影响。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use generative large language models (LLMs) to convert corporate text into empirical variables. We examine the extent to which LLM-based textual measures are invariant to model choice using thirteen measures, including sentiment, management clarity, uncertainty, answer specificity, and climate and political risk. Seven LLMs from different providers score earnings call transcripts of S&P 500 companies on these constructs. Cross-model rank correlations average only 0.52, and transcript-level differences common across providers account for only 34% of total score variation. Cross-model disagreement does not predict subsequent analyst or market disagreement, consistent with a substantial model-specific component rather than common ambiguity in the underlying disclosure. Model choice significantly affects downstream inference, with coefficient magnitudes, signs, and statistical significance varying substantially across models. Averaging across providers makes transcript rankings more stable for most constructs, but score levels remain sensitive to the models included in the ensemble. LLM-generated variables should therefore be treated as model-contingent measurements and validated across providers.

</details>


### [201] [Governed Deduction: Policy-Grounded Premise Authorization Beyond Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31029)
*Wesley Shu,Hsi-Ching Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“受治理演绎”（GD），将前提是否相关与前提是否获准用于某个具体推理转移区分开来。作者构建了配对授权基准并测试线性控制器；消除角色名称捷径后，控制器表现为随机水平，而符号策略预言机达到满分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见推理系统依据相关性选择前提，却未必区分“有助于推理”和“获准用于当前局部推理步骤”。论文旨在形式化这种授权约束，并检验学习模型能否识别由策略决定的前提使用权限。

Method: 定义转移局部准入谓词 admit(p, τ, S)，表示在给定前提 p、转移 τ 和策略状态 S 时是否允许使用该前提。基于独立生成的、加入 RBAC（基于角色的访问控制）的 Spider 基准，构造 4,461 组成对授权样本，使相同查询前提和策略状态对应允许与拒绝的消费转移。评估前提/状态输入、转移输入及联合线性控制器，并通过冻结、与标签无关的上下文局部角色置换消除角色名称捷径；同时设置符号策略预言机及单侧匹配控制和泄漏审计。

Result: 初始联合控制器在留出集上的准确率为 99.19%，但仅使用转移信息的控制器达到 100%，表明模型利用了角色名称捷径。角色置换后，前提/状态、转移及联合线性控制器在 1,856 条留出边上均为 50%；符号策略预言机仍为 100%。

Conclusion: 该基准实例化了超越相关性判断的、以策略为依据的授权问题；但实验中的冻结线性表示未能恢复前提、策略状态与具体转移之间的授权关系。论文强调，评估策略敏感推理时必须使用匹配的单侧控制并进行泄漏审计。

Abstract: Reasoning systems usually treat premise use as a question of relevance: if a fact is available and useful, it may be selected for inference. Authorization imposes a different constraint: a premise may be represented and logically usable but not permitted for a particular local transition. We formalize this distinction as Governed Deduction (GD), with a transition-local admission predicate admit(p, tau, S). From an independently produced RBAC-augmented Spider benchmark, we construct 4,461 matched authorization pairs in which the same query premise and policy state support permitted and denied consuming transitions. An initial joint controller reaches 99.19% held-out accuracy, but a transition-only control reaches 100%, exposing a role-name shortcut. After a frozen, label-independent context-local role permutation removes that shortcut, premise/state-only, transition-only, and joint linear controllers all score exactly 50% on 1,856 held-out edges, while a symbolic policy oracle remains at 100%. The result is a controlled negative finding: the benchmark instantiates policy-grounded authorization beyond relevance, but the frozen linear representation does not recover the relation. Matched one-sided controls and leakage audits are therefore essential for evaluating learned policy-sensitive reasoning.

</details>


### [202] [Cheap, open agents make LLM pollution harder to mitigate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31054)
*Raluca Rilla,Anne-Marie Nussberger,Rui Mata,Dirk U. Wulff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，开源自主调查代理已能以低成本、接近商业代理的表现自动填写问卷，构成新的大语言模型数据污染风险；单一检测手段不足，开放文本分析结合多层检测更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成回答可能污染旨在反映人类行为的调查数据。开源模型与代理框架降低了自主问卷代理的使用门槛，因此需要评估其表现以及能否与真人回答区分。

Method: 比较九种自主代理配置，涵盖完全开源与闭源商业方案。让各代理独立完成包含多种回答形式的问卷，并通过不同检测项评估其可检测性。

Result: 完全开源代理可在本地运行且无需使用费，表现与商业方案具有竞争力。开源代理和商业代理未通过的检测项各不相同；没有单一检测项能可靠识别所有代理。开放文本回答对区分代理与真人最有效。

Conclusion: 完全开源代理构成一种独立且值得关注的调查数据污染风险。应采用多层检测策略，并重点分析开放文本回答，而不能依赖单一检测项。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) pollution occurs when synthetic responses contaminate data intended to capture human behavior. High deployment costs have so far limited the risk posed by autonomous survey agents. However, open-weight models paired with open-source agentic frameworks may have removed this barrier. We compared the performance and detectability of nine agent configurations, ranging from fully open variants to closed commercial ones. Each agent autonomously completed a survey containing multiple response types yielding various detection checks. Fully open agents ran locally without usage fees and performed competitively with commercial alternatives. Open and commercial agents failed different sets of checks, and no single check reliably detected all agents, but open-text responses discriminated best between agents and humans. These findings identify fully open agents as a distinct risk for LLM pollution and support multilayered detection strategies emphasizing open-text analysis.

</details>


### [203] [Neuralyzing the Trace: Selective Representation-Level Unlearning with Contrastive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31056)
*Itai Zehavi,Fanny Jourdan,Ulrich Aivodji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 SCALPEL，一种对比式稀疏自编码器，用于识别更具目标选择性的内部特征，从而在遗忘特定信息时减少对背景知识的扰动；在 TOFU 多模型实验中优于 NMF 和标准 SAE 干预，并与 Gradient Difference、RMU 具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘需要删除特定目标信息，同时尽量保留模型的其他能力。仅观察行为层面的遗忘可能不够，直接干预内部表征或许更有效；但常见的基于重构的机制可解释性提取器存在能量偏置，容易捕获占主导的背景结构，而漏掉能量较低、但与目标相关的成分。

Method: 提出 SCALPEL：通过对比式训练的稀疏自编码器学习更具目标选择性的遗忘特征。理论分析说明对比训练有助于产生目标选择性特征，并提出一种选择分数，用来控制预期的背景知识扰动。

Result: 在 TOFU 基准及 Qwen、Llama、Gemma 模型上的实验显示，SCALPEL 明显优于 NMF 和标准 SAE 干预；其表现与 Gradient Difference 和 RMU 方法相当，具有竞争力。

Conclusion: SCALPEL 将机制可解释性中的内部特征提取与细粒度机器遗忘结合起来，为针对狭窄目标信息进行遗忘、同时保护其他知识提供了一种方法。摘要未提供具体数值或更广泛场景下的验证，因此其效果范围仍需结合全文评估。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove targeted information while preserving a model's other abilities. In realistic settings, such as privacy requests under the EU GDPR, the target may be narrow, for example information associated with a single person. Behavioral forgetting alone may be insufficient, motivating interventions directly on internal representations. However, standard mechanistic-interpretability extractors are poorly selective for such targets. We identify an energy bias in reconstruction-based extraction, which favors dominant background structure over low-energy target-specific components. We introduce SCALPEL, a contrastive sparse autoencoder designed to learn more selective forget features. We show theoretically that contrastive training promotes target-selective features and that our selection score controls expected background knowledge perturbation. We validate SCALPEL experimentally on TOFU across Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, where it substantially improves over NMF and standard SAE interventions and is competitive with Gradient Difference and RMU, bridging mechanistic interpretability and fine-grained unlearning.

</details>


### [204] [Externalized CPDAG Summaries Improve LLM Causal Deduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31071)
*Wentao Sun,João Paulo Nogueira,Dominique Verchere,Mathieu Acher,Alonso Silva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究因果判断任务中，模型是否能正确处理与观测数据相容的整类因果图，而非只做局部模式匹配。作者提出两轮结构化思考：先生成受约束的 CPDAG 表示，再依据该图作答；实验显示这一做法在多个模型和测试设置中带来提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Corr2Cause 的标签取决于一个因果断言是否对所有与观测相关性及条件独立性相容的 DAG 均成立。自由形式的链式思考可能把需要考察马尔可夫等价类的问题简化为局部模式匹配，因此作者希望显式呈现决定标签的潜在图结构，并减少推理过程中的结构遗漏或混淆。

Method: 提出 Structured Thinking 两轮流程：第一轮生成类型明确、受 schema 约束的 CPDAG 摘要；第二轮基于该图状态回答因果问题。比较对象包括强 PC 指令基线及“PC 脚手架＋自由文本中间表示”控制组，并在不同模型、分布外改写集和随机打乱 CPDAG 等条件下评估。

Result: 在 Corr2Cause 全测试集的主要配对实验中，Qwen3.5-27B 的 Yes 类 F1 从 73.0 提升至 86.4，增加 13.4 个百分点（McNemar p=2.4×10⁻⁶；bootstrap 95% CI 为 +8.4 至 +18.6）；三个完整 ID 随机种子的平均增益为 8.1±5.3 个百分点。自由文本中间表示控制组仅得 67.6 F1。相似趋势也出现在 Qwen3.6-27B、Paraphrase-OOD 和 GPT-5.4-mini 上。打乱输出的 CPDAG 会使 F1 下降 12.0 个百分点；全测试集审计显示预测 CPDAG 与参考图较为接近，ID 骨架 F1 为 0.960，精确匹配率为 75.9%。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种有限而具体的设计原则：若任务标签由某个潜在结构对象定义，应将该对象显式外化、约束其表示形式，并验证后续答案是否真正利用该对象。证据表明，单有详细的 PC 脚手架和自由文本中间步骤并不足够；但实验结论主要适用于本文评测任务与模型，不能直接推广为普遍因果推理能力的证明。

Abstract: Corr2Cause asks whether a causal claim holds in every DAG compatible with observed correlations and conditional independencies. We frame this as latent-object reasoning: the label is defined by a CPDAG query, but free-form chain-of-thought often collapses the Markov-equivalence-class problem into local pattern matching. We propose Structured Thinking, a two-turn pipeline that first externalizes a typed, schema-constrained CPDAG summary and then answers against that graph state. On the Corr2Cause full test, Structured Thinking raises Qwen3.5-27B from $73.0$ to $86.4$ $F_1$(Yes) over a strong PC-instruction baseline in the primary paired run ($+13.4$ pp; McNemar $p=2.4\times 10^{-6}$; bootstrap $95\%$ CI [$+8.4$, $+18.6$]); across three full-ID seeds, the mean gain is $+8.1 \pm 5.3$ pp. A PC-scaffolded two-turn prose control reaches only $67.6$ $F_1$, indicating that a detailed PC scaffold plus a schema-free prose intermediate is not sufficient. The same pattern holds on Qwen3.6-27B, Paraphrase-OOD, and GPT-5.4-mini. Scrambling the emitted CPDAG costs $12.0$ pp $F_1$, and a full-split audit shows close agreement with the reference CPDAG (ID skeleton $F_1$ $0.960$; exact match $75.9\%$). These results support a bounded design principle: externalize the latent object that defines the label, constrain its form, and test whether downstream answers use it.

</details>


### [205] [Up and Down the Abstraction Ladder: Code-Based Skills for Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31076)
*Bartłomiej Cupiał,Jens Tuyls,Maciej Wołczyk,Davide Paglieri,Martin Klissarov,Benjamin Eysenbach,Piotr Miłoś,Karthik R. Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究语言智能体使用代码技能作为动作抽象的效果。实验显示，技能在 NetHack 中显著提升游戏进展、降低推理成本，并加快强化学习；技能与原始动作结合可保留灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体在需要长序列低层操作的环境中，逐步选择原始动作效率低、学习困难。可复用技能有望提高效率，但技能抽象可能无法覆盖所有情境，因此需要权衡生产力与灵活性，并系统评估其对性能、推理成本和学习的影响。

Method: 在 NetHack 环境中构建 CodeHack 技能库，为代码实现的技能配备自然语言描述。比较仅使用原始动作、仅使用语义技能，以及技能与原始动作结合的智能体；在零样本提示、监督微调和强化学习三种设置下评测。

Result: 零样本评测中，相比原始动作，技能使游戏进度接近三倍，并使每局推理成本降低86%。技能与原始动作结合仍保留大部分收益，同时允许在必要时回退到低层动作。强化学习中，技能智能体在相同训练预算下的地下城层级平均增益达到原始动作智能体的7.2倍。

Conclusion: 提供技能库能够提升语言智能体的任务表现、推理效率和学习速度；同时保留原始动作，可在技能库覆盖不足时提供灵活性。作者发布了 CodeHack 及其训练、评测代码。

Abstract: Language agents struggle to act and learn in environments that require long sequences of low-level actions. Code-based abstractions can make these agents more productive by letting them invoke reusable skills instead of repeatedly selecting individual actions. The code handles recurring local decisions, while the language model decides which skills to use and how to combine them. Yet abstractions are leaky, and situations beyond a skill's capabilities may require a return to primitive actions. Motivated by this tradeoff between productivity and flexibility, we systematically study how code-based action abstraction affects the performance, inference cost, and learning of language agents. We study this in NetHack, a challenging, long-horizon game environment, using CodeHack, our library of code-based skills with natural-language descriptions. We use this library to compare agents restricted to primitives with those using semantic skills alone or in combination with primitives. We evaluate these agents in three settings: zero-shot prompting, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning. Across a broad zero-shot evaluation on NetHack, we find that compared with primitives, skills nearly triple game progression, while reducing inference cost per episode by 86%. Combining skills with primitives retains much of this benefit while preserving a path back down to low-level actions. Finally, in RL, we find that skill-based agents learn significantly faster than agents acting on primitives, achieving a 7.2x larger average gain in dungeon level over the same training budget. These results show that a supplied skill library can improve performance, efficiency, and learning, while retaining primitives provides flexibility when the library is insufficient. We release CodeHack together with training and evaluation code.

</details>


### [206] [OmouAI: Argumentative Human-AI Policy Deliberation with Simulated Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31078)
*Stylianos Loukas Vasileiou,Antonio Rago,William Yeoh,Georgina Curto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 OmouAI：将大语言模型与计算论证结合，构建可由人类参与、可审查且解释更忠实的政策审议系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体辩论虽有应用潜力，但在人机协作及高风险政策场景中，容易出现迎合用户立场（sycophancy）和解释不忠实的问题。

Method: 系统让多个模拟角色（如利益相关者、领域专家或反方质疑者）围绕政策主张分别生成论据，并将论据纳入共享的论证框架。人类用户可质疑、补充和修订论据，提供监督；随后系统依据确定性的论证语义，评估论据与外部目标（如联合国可持续发展目标）的关系。

Result: 摘要介绍了系统设计及其预期机制：通过角色多样性与人类审查降低迎合倾向，并利用确定性论证评估提供更忠实的解释；目标改善或恶化情况被用作政策建议的指标。摘要未报告定量实验结果或实证比较。

Conclusion: OmouAI 提供了一种将 LLM 辩论、形式化论证和人类监督结合的政策审议方案，旨在提升审议的包容性、可解释性与可信度。其实际效果仍需通过进一步评估验证。

Abstract: Debates amongst agents driven by large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated vast potential in various applications, but when these interactions include humans and take place in high-stakes environments, e.g., in public policy deliberations, they are beset with issues such as sycophancy and a lack of faithful explanations. To tackle these issues, we present OmouAI, an interactive and inclusive deliberation system that uses LLMs in combination with computational argumentation, a field which excels in representing and reasoning within debates. OmouAI allows a human user to deliberate policy claims for real-world challenges with simulated personas, e.g., representing stakeholders, domain experts or devil's advocates, towards reducing sycophancy. Each persona generates its own arguments, and the arguments of all parties form a shared argumentation framework. Users can then contest, add and revise arguments, providing crucial human oversight. Then, arguments are evaluated using deterministic argumentative semantics against external goals, such as the UN Sustainable Development Goals, guaranteeing faithful explanations. The advancement or worsening of the goals thus serve as indicators for the policy recommendations.

</details>


### [207] [Monitor Jailbreaking: Evading Chain-of-Thought Monitoring Without Encoded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31121)
*Julian Schulz*

Main category: cs.AI
